Conclusion: SnapPhysics 证明了几何重建+结构化场景图引导 VLM 可在单图条件下有效估计物理属性，使物理可交互的 MR 体验无需人工调参。其局限在于依赖单图与 VLM 先验，真实场景评估规模与泛化性仍有待验证。

Abstract: We propose SnapPhysics, a training-free framework that reconstructs 3D objects and estimates their physical properties such as mass, friction, and center of gravity from a single image. For physically coherent interactions in mixed reality (MR), such properties are as important as geometry. Prior approaches infer them by analyzing object dynamics in video, which is computationally costly, or by querying vision-language models (VLMs) on single images, which lacks geometric grounding and inter-object relationships. We address these limitations by combining instance-level 3D reconstruction and spatial alignment with a physics-aware scene graph that encodes these relationships and per-object metric geometry as structured context for VLM-based property reasoning. Experiments on 3D-FRONT show that SnapPhysics improves scene-level F-Score by 18.6% over the best learning-based method, and on real captured scenes with ground-truth mass, it reduces the mean absolute log difference error (mALDE) by up to 20.5% and improves log-scale correlation ($r^2_{\mathrm{ls}}$) by up to 19.6% over VLM-only estimation. SnapPhysics enables physically interactive MR experiences without manual parameter tuning. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [KoUniTalk: A Lightweight Articulation-Centered Korean-English 3D Talking Face Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19840)
*Hyunjung Chung,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KoUniTalk将VOCASET与韩语3D说话人脸数据通过变形迁移统一到1,176顶点的发音中心模板，构建韩英跨语言3D说话人脸基准（22说话人、4,978序列、642,781帧），用于语音驱动面部发音建模的训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量3D说话人脸数据集以英语为主，韩语3D面部运动数据因网格拓扑、空间尺度、坐标系与时间采样差异难以与标准英语基准结合，缺少跨语言统一的可控评测空间。

Method: 基于变形迁移（deformation transfer）将VOCASET与韩语语音3D说话人脸数据重定向到共享网格拓扑；不提出新变形迁移算法，也不做完整头部身份保持头像，而是构建身份中性的规范化输出空间；统一模板含1,176顶点，聚焦嘴部及邻近中下面部区域；对语义嘴部关键点轨迹（张口、嘴宽、开口率、张口动态）进行评估；因韩语官方测试集未公开，额外定义说话人不相交的韩语划分。

Result: 输出维度从15,069与72,147降至3,528，分别相对VOCASET/FLAME与原始韩语网格减少4.27倍和20.45倍；处理后匹配基准含22说话人、4,978序列、642,781帧；嘴部关键点轨迹评估表明重定向保留了语音相关运动；原始清单计数与处理后计数分开列出。

Conclusion: KoUniTalk提供了一个紧凑、身份中性的发音模板空间，支持韩英跨数据集评估语音驱动3D面部动画模型，可作为跨语言说话人脸建模的基准与工具。

Abstract: High-quality 3D talking face datasets remain largely English- centric, and Korean 3D facial motion data are difficult to combine with standard English benchmarks because of differences in mesh topology, spatial scale, coordinate system, and temporal sampling. We present KoUniTalk, a lightweight articulation-centered Korean-English 3D talk- ing face benchmark that retargets VOCASET and the released Korean speech-based 3D talking face data to a shared mesh topology using de- formation transfer. Rather than proposing a new deformation-transfer algorithm or a full-head identity-preserving avatar dataset, KoUniTalk provides an identity-neutral canonical output space for controlled speech- driven facial articulation training and evaluation across English and Ko- rean. The unified template contains 1,176 vertices and focuses on the mouth and adjacent lower- and mid-face regions, reducing the output dimensionality from 15,069 and 72,147 dimensions to 3,528 dimensions, corresponding to 4.27-fold and 20.45-fold reductions compared with VO- CASET/FLAME and the original Korean mesh, respectively. To exam- ine whether retargeting preserves speech-relevant motion, we evaluate semantic mouth-landmark trajectories, including mouth opening, mouth width, aperture ratio, and mouth-opening dynamics. Since the official test set of the Korean dataset is not publicly released, we additionally define a subject-disjoint Korean benchmark split. The processed matched benchmark contains 22 speakers, 4,978 sequences, and 642,781 frames, enabling Korean-English cross-dataset evaluation of speech-driven 3D fa- cial animation models in a single compact articulation-template space. Source-reported inventory counts are listed separately from these pro- cessed counts

</details>


### [36] [PACE: Precise AI Cinematic Expression: A Typed Specification for Script-Grounded Previsualization and Geometric Conformance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19853)
*Bing Duan,Qiang Guo,Linpu Li,Zhijian Mao,Min Zhu,Zhirui Ren,Yiwei Yan,Xi Chu,Xiaoding Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE 提出一种类型化场景表示与编译器，将剧本层级的空间声明（人物站位、道具、相机取景）逐级继承并编译为扩散模型提示词与以米为单位的3D灰盒场景，并通过相机求解器让实际成像与声明取景一致；同时以字段级度量替代模型主观评判，在 Automatic Drive 剧本与204个外部导演分镜上量化了"取景保真"与"动作还原"之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剧本到影片之间存在一个本质上是空间性的规划问题：谁站在哪里、相机从哪里看到什么。现有图像扩散模型仅凭自由文本提示生成镜头，这一空间规划被模型自身默认先验替用户决定，导致人物位置、取景与剧本声明脱节。作者希望把这一空间规划显式化、可声明、可度量，而不是交给生成模型隐式决定。

Method: 提出 PACE（Precise AI Cinematic Expression）类型化表示：包含剧本证据、所需角色/道具/场景，以及每个主体的站位与相机行为。取值在其所属层级（script/scene/shot/panel）写一次即可向下继承。编译器把该表示同时转成两样东西：发给扩散模型的提示词，和以米为单位构建的3D场景；再由相机求解器放置相机，使"声明的取景"即为"构建出的取景"。在声明值变为几何之处，PACE 逐字段测量编译相机与分阶段渲染相对声明的偏差，而非让模型做主观评判。

Result: 在 Automatic Drive 剧本的11个场景中，每个单主体面板的拍摄主体位置误差在画幅宽度的1.2%以内；当主体为两个或三个时，单一相机位姿无法同时满足所有位置，此时残差被显式报告而非被吸收。在204个外部导演分镜镜头上，交付的人眼/头部高度分别是：来自导演文字的阶段性目标值的1.906倍、来自编译提示词的1.733倍、灰盒控制的0.955倍；即取景保持最好的条件，反而画出所描述动作的能力最差。在30个镜头上显式声明姿态，可将画出动作的比例从58.9%提升至74.4%，同时不改变取景。过渡、拟合运动以及对生成面板的人工审核仍属未解决问题。

Conclusion: PACE 表明，把镜头空间规划从自由文本中解放出来、以类型化、可按层级继承的声明配合编译器与相机求解器，可以在可量化层面精确控制取景并度量其与声明的偏差，且揭示了取景保真与动作生成能力之间的结构性张力；姿态声明可在不牺牲取景的情况下显著提升动作还原度。转场、运动拟合与人工审核环节仍有待解决。

Abstract: Between a screenplay and a film sits a planning problem that is spatial first: who stands where, and what a camera sees from where it stands. An image diffusion model asked for a shot in free text settles that plan by its own defaults. We present PACE (Precise AI Cinematic Expression), a typed representation for the plan: the screenplay evidence, the characters, props and locations it needs, where each subject stands, and what the camera does. A value is written once at the level it belongs to (script, scene, shot or panel) and inherited below it. A compiler turns the result into both the prompt sent to the diffusion model and a 3D scene built in metres, and a camera solver places the camera so that the declared framing is the framing built. Where a declared value becomes geometry, PACE measures, field by field, how far the compiled camera and the staged render sit from the declaration, rather than asking a model to judge. On the 11-scene Automatic Drive screenplay, every staged single-subject panel places its subject within 1.2% of frame width of its declared position; with two or three subjects one camera pose cannot satisfy every position, and the residual is reported rather than absorbed. On 204 external director-storyboard shots, delivered head height is 1.906 times the staged target from the director's words, 1.733 from the compiled prompt, and 0.955 with the greybox control; the condition that holds framing best draws the described action least. Declaring the pose on 30 shots raises the action drawn from 58.9% to 74.4% without moving the framing. Transitions, fitted motion and human review of the generated panels remain open. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [37] [Socialized UAV Cross-Task Learning: Towards Cross-Granularity Collaboration through Hierarchical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19867)
*Xinjie Yao,Ruipu Zhao,Yunqi Zhu,Zhihe Fan,Zhoupeng Guo,Weihao Li,Zhen Wang,Qilong Wang,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 UAV 跨粒度联合检测与分割基准 CrossUAV 及渐进自适应协作框架 CGSC，通过调控任务交互的时机、位置与强度减少干扰、利用粗细粒度互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构任务联合学习（特征共享、蒸馏、辅助监督）在跨任务粒度不匹配时易产生干扰、教师偏差或单向崩塌，作者认为跨粒度学习本质是层级交互调控问题；UAV 场景的视觉偏移与检测/分割目标恰好构成粗-细粒度知识源。

Method: 构建 CrossUAV 无人机检测+实例分割统一评测基准；提出 Cross-Granularity Socialized Collaboration（CGSC）框架，按网络层级渐进激活跨任务交互，并依据任务贡献自适应调节交互强度，抑制有害干扰、利用粗-细粒度互补结构。

Result: 大量实验显示检测与分割两个任务均获得一致性提升，验证层级动态交互作为跨粒度协作机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 跨粒度任务协作应被建模为层级交互调控而非简单任务耦合，CGSC 为该思路提供了可行的动态调控方案。

Abstract: Joint learning across heterogeneous tasks is often treated as task coupling through feature sharing, distillation, or auxiliary supervision. However, in cross-task learning, mismatched representational and supervisory granularities make such coupling prone to interference, teacher bias, or unidirectional collapse. We argue that cross-granularity learning is fundamentally a problem of hierarchical interaction regulation rather than simple task coupling. This issue is particularly evident in UAV perception, where visual shifts and detection--segmentation objectives naturally form coarse- and fine-grained knowledge sources. To systematically study this problem, we introduce CrossUAV, a UAV benchmark for joint object detection and instance segmentation that provides a unified evaluation platform for cross-granularity task collaboration. To address these challenges, we propose Cross-Granularity Socialized Collaboration (CGSC), a progressive and adaptive framework that regulates when, where, and how tasks exchange information across network hierarchies. CGSC progressively activates cross-task interactions and adaptively adjusts the strength according to task contribution, suppressing harmful interference while exploiting complementary coarse- and fine-grained structures. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent improvements on both tasks, validating hierarchical dynamic interaction as an effective mechanism for cross-granularity collaboration.

</details>


### [38] [PART: Learning 3D Part Assembly and Retrieval with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19872)
*Ruchao Bao,Wenzheng Wu,Chucheng Xiang,Zhongyuan Liu,Yuan Liu,Jinxin Dong,Ligang Liu,Ziqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PART：首个从零件库中检索并装配3D形状的统一Transformer框架，将检索+6-DoF位姿估计建模为集合预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D装配工作多假设零件集合已预先给定，只做位姿回归；而实际应用中需要从大规模零件库中自主选择合适零件，这一更实用的"检索式装配"设定尚未被探索。该设定面临三大挑战：搜索空间随库规模指数增长、输出长度可变（不同目标需要的零件数不同）、以及连续的6-DoF位姿估计。

Method: 将零件检索与装配形式化为集合预测（set prediction）问题，设计统一的Transformer框架：以目标形状和零件库为输入，直接输出可变长度的"检索零件+6-DoF位姿"序列。利用零件位姿估计与目标分割之间的对偶性，通过联合训练和一个新颖的分割增强优化模块进行互相强化。自建80K+形状的大规模数据集用于训练与评测。

Result: 在80K+形状数据集上验证了方法有效性，并展示出对场景布局、图像目标以及真实世界扫描的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 检索式3D装配可作为集合预测问题被统一Transformer框架有效求解，且能泛化到场景、图像与真实扫描等多样化输入。

Abstract: 3D assembly is fundamental to modern manufacturing and digital content creation. In this paper, we present PART, a unified transformer-based framework for 3D part retrieval and assembly: given a target shape and a part library, PART automatically selects the appropriate parts and predicts their 6-DoF poses to reconstruct the target. While prior work has achieved impressive progress on assembling a pre-defined set of parts, this more practical retrieval-based setting remains largely unexplored. The task faces three key challenges: (i) a combinatorially explosive search space that grows exponentially with library size; (ii) variable-length outputs, as different targets require different numbers of parts; and (iii) continuous 6-DoF pose estimation for part assembly. To address these, we formulate retrieval and assembly as a set prediction problem and design a novel transformer-based framework that retrieves parts and regresses their poses with variable-length output. Additionally, we exploit the duality between part pose estimation and target segmentation through joint training and a novel segmentation-enhanced optimization module. Finally, We curate a large-scale dataset of 80K+ shapes, and the results show that PART generalizes to scene layouts, image targets, and real-world scans. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [39] [BINDER: A Latent Variable Model for Probabilistic Medical Image Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19875)
*Stefano Cerri,Amirhossein Hassankhani,Yaël Balbastre,Koen Van Leemput*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于互信息准则的概率医学图像配准模型BINDER，利用潜在体素级对应关系推导闭式迭代更新，实现高效demons式非线性配准与高维3D多模态配准不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像配准中，互信息是常用的多模态相似性准则，但其概率建模、优化推导及不确定性量化尚不充分；多模态场景下高维3D变形的配准不确定性此前难以量化。

Method: 构建以互信息配准准则为基础的概率模型，核心是假设两幅图像间存在潜在体素级对应关系的空间插值技术；利用这些隐变量推导仅含闭式迭代更新的优化与MCMC采样算法；应用于非线性配准时得到类demons高效优化算法，并给出对应采样器。

Result: 在多种单模态与多模态配准任务上表现稳健的即插即用性能；首次实现具有极高维3D变形情况下的多模态配准不确定性量化；开源代码BINDER。

Conclusion: 该工作为通用医学图像配准提供了统一的概率建模框架，兼顾高效优化与不确定性量化，代码开源便于复现与推广。

Abstract: We propose a new probabilistic model for general-purpose medical image registration that builds upon the mutual information registration criterion. It centers around a spatial interpolation technique that assumes latent voxel-wise correspondences between the images being registered. By exploiting these latent variables, we derive dedicated optimization and MCMC sampling techniques that only involve closed-form iterative updates. When applied to nonlinear registration, an efficient demons-like optimization algorithm is obtained that shows robust out-of-the-box performance across a variety of monomodal and multimodal registration tasks. We also demonstrate a corresponding sampler that can quantify, for the first time, uncertainty in multimodal registration scenarios with very high-dimensional 3D deformations. Our code, which we call BINDER (Bayesian INference for DEformable Registration), is freely available at this https URL .

</details>


### [40] [SlugTrails: An Egocentric Benchmark for Floor Plan Localization in Large Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19876)
*Yunqian Cheng,Roberto Manduchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SlugTrails：面向大型公共室内空间的楼层平面图视觉定位基准，含3栋校园建筑、6层、22089 m²，30 Hz Aria眼镜记录，CAD语义平面图与激光锚点对齐；统一三种观测协议（单帧行走、静止多视角扫掠、带里程计行走流），评测5类几何与学习系统，发现stock权重近乎零性能而微调大幅提升，并给出室内数据瓶颈而非架构瓶颈的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有楼层平面图室内视觉定位工作多在小型住宅环境开发与评测，与真实部署中的大型公共建筑差异显著；缺少面向大空间、真实自我中心传感、跨范式可比的基准与协议，导致方法的实际可用性无法判断。

Method: 构建SlugTrails基准：3栋校园建筑、6层、22089 m²平面图轮廓，30 Hz Aria眼镜记录，CAD导出带语义类别与通行空间掩码的平面图，轨迹经激光测量的锚点对齐至平面图坐标系。统一协议覆盖三种受限视场下采集几何的方式：单帧行走、静止多视角扫掠、带里程计的行走流。在原生传感配置下评测5个代表性几何与学习系统，并进行微调、跨数据集泛化（LaMAR）与从零训练的对比分析。

Result: 官方权重（stock checkpoints）在SlugTrails上近乎失效（行走单帧最高仅0.004 R@1m30°）；在SlugTrails上微调使所有可训练方法族在三项任务上均提升（如F³Loc单帧0.0→0.141，序列0.03→0.66），增益随观测累积而叠加；同一微调权重在无LaMAR训练下提升LaMAR跨数据集泛化（序列R@1m：F³Loc 0.048→0.143，UnLoc 0.063→0.127）；仅用SlugTrails从零训练远低于基于官方权重微调。

Conclusion: 楼层平面图室内定位当前的瓶颈在于室内数据而非网络架构；SlugTrails提供了大空间、统一协议与真实自我中心传感的评测平台，并开源数据集、协议与工具，支撑跨范式比较与后续数据驱动的改进。

Abstract: Floor-plan-based indoor visual localization enables infrastructure-free positioning, but most methods are developed and evaluated in small residential environments unlike the large public buildings of real deployment. We introduce SlugTrails, a floor plan localization benchmark for large indoor spaces under realistic egocentric sensing: $30$ Hz Aria glasses recordings across three campus buildings and six floors ($22089$ m$^2$ of floor plan outline), CAD-derived floor plans with semantic classes and circulation space masks, and trajectories aligned into the floor plan frame using laser-surveyed anchors. One protocol covers three practical ways of gathering geometry under a limited field of view -- a single walking frame, a stationary multi-view sweep, and a walking stream with odometry -- so methods designed for different regimes are compared on the same buildings and ground truth. Evaluating five representative geometric and learned systems under their native sensing configurations, we find that stock checkpoints (official released weights) are near zero on SlugTrails (at most $0.004$ R@1m30$^{\circ}$ on walking single frames), while fine-tuning on SlugTrails improves every trainable family on all three tasks (e.g., F$^3$Loc $0.0 \rightarrow 0.141$ single-frame and $0.03 \rightarrow 0.66$ sequential), with gains compounding as observations accumulate. The same fine-tuned weights also improve cross-dataset generalization on LaMAR with no LaMAR training (sequential R@1m $0.048 \rightarrow 0.143$ for F$^3$Loc and $0.063 \rightarrow 0.127$ for UnLoc), whereas train-from-scratch on SlugTrails alone stays far below fine-tuning from stock weights -- evidence that floor plan localization is currently limited by indoor data rather than by architecture. We release the dataset, protocols, and tools at this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [BinoGen: Scaling egocentric binocular data for embodied visual perception and learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19881)
*Chunpeng Li,Ya-tang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BinoGen：自动化生成大规模、具身感知（embodiment-aware）自我中心双目视觉经验的框架，联合生成场景、物体、外观、轨迹与双目相机配置，产出2000万+带密集多模态标注的图像数据，用于提升深度估计、检测、视频目标跟踪，并研究具身差异对感知学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心双目视觉观测与密集标注数据采集成本高、难度大；且视觉经验不仅由环境决定，还受观察者具身属性（视高、视场角、双目几何、运动方式）影响，现有数据难以系统解耦这两类变化。

Method: BinoGen 将环境变化与观察者变化联合建模：生成式场景合成、概率化物体实例化、外观随机化、随机轨迹生成、可配置双目相机设置；输出同步双目视频及深度图、光流、表面法线、语义图、物体坐标、相机位姿等密集多模态监督；据此构建超2000万张标注图像数据集，并提供"类人"与"类鼠"两种具身设置在同环境下的配对观测。

Result: 引入 BinoGen 数据可持续提升真实世界视觉感知（深度估计、目标检测、视频目标跟踪）；具身特定自适应显著提升性能，联合训练使单一模型在两种具身上均具竞争力。

Conclusion: 大规模、可控的生成式视觉经验能有效改善具身感知，并提供研究具身—感知关系的可控实验手段。

Abstract: Embodied visual perception relies on temporally coherent visual experience accumulated through continuous engagement with the environment. However, collecting large-scale egocentric binocular observations together with dense annotations remains costly and difficult. Moreover, visual experience is shaped not only by the environment but also by the embodiment of the observer, including viewing height, field of view, binocular geometry, and motion through the scene. To address these challenges, we present BinoGen, an automated framework for generating large-scale, embodiment-aware egocentric binocular visual experiences in indoor environments. BinoGen jointly models environmental and observer variation through generative scene synthesis, probabilistic object instantiation, appearance randomization, stochastic trajectory generation, and configurable binocular camera setups. The framework produces synchronized binocular videos together with dense multimodal supervision, including depth maps, optical flow, surface normals, semantic maps, object coordinates, and camera poses. Using BinoGen, we construct a dataset comprising more than 20 million annotated images for supervised learning. We demonstrate two complementary utilities of BinoGen. First, incorporating BinoGen data consistently improves real-world visual perception, including depth estimation, object detection, and video object tracking. Second, paired human-inspired and mouse-inspired observations from the same environments enable controlled investigation of how observer embodiment affects perceptual learning. Embodiment-specific adaptation substantially improves performance, while joint training enables a single model to perform competitively across both embodiments. Together, these results demonstrate that large-scale, controllable visual experience can improve embodied perception...

</details>


### [42] [GS-PI: An Optimization-Decoupled Appearance Decomposition Approach for Generating PBR Gaussian Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19907)
*Jieting Xu,Rengan Xie,Zijian Huang,Zehui Jin,Rui Wang,Yuchi Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-PI 提出优化解耦框架，将 PBR 材质生成建模为 3D 点云上的几何条件扩散过程，通过多尺度跨视角条件实现多视角一致的材质预测与可重光照 PBR-GS 资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gaussian Splatting 编码的是烘焙辐照度，光照与几何纠缠，难以接入 PBR 管线；现有逆渲染方法联合优化材质与光照，目标相互竞争，导致严重歧义与残余光照伪影。2D 扩散方法则受像素对应问题困扰。

Method: 从预训练高斯模型提取点云，直接在 3D 域上用条件扩散预测 PBR 属性；引入多尺度跨视角条件机制，融合全局语义先验、源锚定光度线索与绝对空间学习的视角方向条件信号，压缩多视角证据并缓解跨视角投影错位；再经可微光栅化蒸馏回高斯，得到完全可重光照的 PBR-GS 资产，无需代理网格。

Result: GS-PI 优于近期逆渲染基线，用"学习式扩散 + 短程目标驱动蒸馏"替代逐场景联合光照/BRDF 优化，且能防止高光被烘焙进本征颜色。

Conclusion: 优化解耦 + 3D 域扩散为逆渲染提供了一条多视角一致、无需代理网格的新路径，但属逐场景资产分解，泛化性与下游机器人/仿真应用尚待验证。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting (GS) excels at novel-view synthesis but encodes baked-in radiance, tightly entangling illumination with geometry and preventing seamless integration into physically based rendering (PBR) pipelines. Existing inverse-rendering methods attempt to disentangle materials via joint optimization, but often suffer from competing objectives that cause severe ambiguities and residual lighting artifacts. To overcome this, we present GS-PI, a novel optimization-decoupled framework that casts PBR material generation as a geometry-conditioned diffusion process on 3D point clouds. By operating directly in the 3D domain, our method inherently guarantees multi-view consistency, sidestepping the severe pixel correspondence issues that challenge 2D diffusion approaches. We introduce a multi-scale cross-view conditioning mechanism that integrates three complementary components: a global semantic prior, source-anchored photometric cues, and an absolute spatial learned view-direction conditioning signal. This design efficiently compresses complex multi-view evidence, mitigating cross-view projection misalignment and successfully preventing specular highlights from baking into intrinsic colors. By extracting a point cloud from a pre-trained Gaussian model, predicting PBR attributes via conditional diffusion, and distilling them back through differentiable rasterisation, we yield a fully relightable PBR-GS asset. GS-PI outperforms recent inverse-rendering baselines while replacing per-scene joint illumination/BRDF optimization with a learned diffusion pass followed by a short target-driven distillation, without requiring proxy meshes.

</details>


### [43] [CitySTAR: Structured and Topology-Aware Reasoning for Open-Vocabulary Urban 3D Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19911)
*Shuai Zhang,Hongye Hou,Qinghe Liu,Zhuoxiao Li,Dongli Wu,Jing Ou,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CitySTAR：免训练的城市级3D grounding框架，将描述语义重构为可计算约束，通过开放词汇场景图、成对超图拓扑建模与双向验证、反思式跨模态定位，实现可解释的城市场景3D目标定位，并发布增强基准CitySTAR-3D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D grounding方法依赖特征相似度或直接匹配，难以将自然语言意图与十亿级城市点云中隐含的语义与几何结构关联。作者因此将城市级3D grounding重新表述为结构化约束推理问题。

Method: 免训练框架CitySTAR：将原始十亿级城市点云提升为可查询的开放词汇3D实例场景图，用CodeLLM驱动的工具为节点属性与3D空间关系提供多模态证据；用成对超图建模目标-上下文拓扑，并做双向拓扑验证以消解结构歧义；最后通过Reflective Cross-modal Grounding模块融合拓扑一致性与以候选为中心的2D视觉证据，在度量感知的3D上下文图上决策。同时构建CitySTAR-3D基准。

Result: 在CitySTAR-3D等基准上，CitySTAR持续提升开放世界城市3D grounding性能，并保持较强的可解释性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将城市级3D grounding建模为结构化约束推理是可行且有效的路径，可绕开训练依赖，借助场景图、超图拓扑与跨模态证据提升开放词汇定位的可解释性与泛化。

Abstract: 3D grounding aims to localize target entities in complex scenes from natural language and plays a fundamental role in embodied perception and spatial reasoning. However, existing approaches mostly rely on feature similarity or direct matching, making it difficult to connect natural-language intent with the implicit semantic and geometric structures hidden in billion-scale urban point clouds. We reformulate city-scale 3D grounding as structured constraint reasoning, where description semantics are organized into computable cross-modal constraints over open-vocabulary 3D entities, attributes, and spatial relations. We present CitySTAR, a training-free framework for reasoning-driven urban 3D grounding. CitySTAR lifts raw billion-scale urban point clouds into a query-ready scene graph of open-vocabulary 3D instances, with CodeLLM-driven tools supplying multimodal evidence for node attributes and 3D spatial relations. It then models target-context topology with paired hypergraphs and performs bidirectional topology verification for structural disambiguation. Finally, a Reflective Cross-modal Grounding module integrates topology consistency and candidate-centered 2D visual evidence to make decisions over a metric-aware 3D context graph. To further support this setting, we introduce CitySTAR-3D, an enhanced benchmark that improves semantic coverage, instance completeness, bounding-box fidelity, and spatial-relation complexity in city-scale 3D grounding. Extensive experiments show that CitySTAR consistently improves open-world urban 3D grounding while maintaining strong interpretability and generalization.

</details>


### [44] [DirtyMoCap: Robust Motion Capture from Unconstrained Markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19927)
*Long Wang,Shuting Zhao,Shen Yan,Siyuan Yu,Xiaoben Li,Zeyu Cai,Yumeng Hou,Yuliang Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirtyMoCap：面向无序、稀疏、含噪光学动捕标记点的无布局依赖框架，通过“代理锚点”中间表示 + 递归滑窗跟踪 + 可微 Gauss-Newton 拟合 SMPL-H，实现全序列人体姿态、平移与体型重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学动捕精度高但依赖严格标记点布局与干净轨迹，实际系统中标记点常为稀疏、含噪、无序且配置未知的点云。需要一种能在任意（甚至损坏的）标记布局下鲁棒恢复参数化人体模型的方法。

Method: 核心洞察是把无序标记观测映射到固定集合的"代理锚点"（骨骼关节 + 体表点）作为稳定中间表示；用递归滑窗架构对锚点做初始化与长序列跟踪；再用自研可微 Gauss-Newton 求解器将 SMPL-H 拟合到跟踪锚点，恢复全身姿态、平移与体型。通过显式推导几何残差，端到端学习自适应观测置信度、平滑项与先验权重，动态适应输入可靠性。并实现自定义 CUDA 求解器加速。

Result: 在多种含噪标记配置上，单一训练模型即可泛化到任意布局，关节与顶点重建精度一致优于配置特定的 SOTA 基线；CUDA 求解器相比标准 PyTorch 实现最高 100 倍加速。此外应用于传统武术异构原始光学动捕数据，产出时序连贯的 SMPL-H 重建数据集。

Conclusion: 该工作打通了"损坏原始标记 → 参数化人体模型"的鸿沟，无需标记布局先验即可鲁棒重建，并可处理真实世界异构动捕数据，具备开源代码与数据。

Abstract: Optical motion capture delivers high-fidelity human motion, but its reliance on strict marker layouts and clean trajectories severely limits its real-world applicability. In practice, tracking systems frequently output unconstrained markers: sparse, noisy, and unordered point clouds with unknown or varying configurations. To bridge the gap between corrupted raw markers and parametric human models, we introduce DirtyMoCap, a robust, marker-layout-free framework. Our core insight is to map unordered marker observations to a fixed set of "proxy anchors" comprising skeletal joints and body surface points, which serve as a stable intermediate representation. We first initialize and track these anchors over long sequences using a recurrent sliding-window architecture. Then, a custom differentiable Gauss-Newton solver fits the SMPL-H model to the tracked anchors to recover full-body pose, translation, and shape. By explicitly deriving geometric residuals, our solver learns adaptive observation confidence, smoothness, and prior weights end-to-end, adapting dynamically to the reliability of the input data. Extensive experiments on diverse, noisy marker configurations demonstrate that DirtyMoCap successfully generalizes across arbitrary layouts using only a single trained model. It consistently outperforms state-of-the-art configuration-specific baselines in both joint and vertex reconstruction accuracy, while our custom CUDA solver achieves up to a 100x speedup over standard PyTorch implementations. We further apply DirtyMoCap to heterogeneous raw optical MoCap recordings of traditional Chinese martial arts, yielding a Kung Fu motion dataset of temporally coherent SMPL-H reconstructions. Code and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [Enhanced Knowledge Distillation for Detection Transformer via Teacher Prediction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19964)
*Yitong Xing,Yuhao Cheng,Yanping Li,Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TPRD，一种即插即用的DETR知识蒸馏模块，通过利用各阶段预测信息在蒸馏前精炼教师预测，提升监督质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DETR计算开销大，难以部署到边缘设备；现有DETR蒸馏方法只关注对齐蒸馏点，忽略了教师监督本身的质量问题。作者观察到DETR存在分阶段非单调预测行为：早期阶段定位或分类正确的预测在后期可能退化，部分负样本预测变得越来越过度自信，导致仅依赖当前阶段预测会给出不准确且不一致的监督。

Method: TPRD包含三个组件：(1) 正预测校正（PPC），从更早阶段恢复更准确的预测来校正退化的正样本，保证可靠的定位和分类信号；(2) 负预测抑制（NPS），抑制过度自信负样本的影响，避免误导性监督；(3) 最大暗知识保留（MDKP），选择性精炼目标类logits同时保留非目标类关系，以维持信息性暗知识。

Result: 在MS COCO和PASCAL VOC上进行的大量实验证明了方法的有效性与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过在蒸馏前精炼教师预测，TPRD以即插即用方式提升了DETR蒸馏的监督质量，为边缘设备上高效检测器的训练提供了通用且有效的改进手段。

Abstract: Detection Transformers (DETRs) achieve strong performance in object detection but remain challenging to deploy on edge devices due to their high computational cost. Existing DETR distillation methods mainly focus on aligning distillation points, while largely overlooking the quality of the teacher's supervision itself. We observe that due to stage-wise non-monotonic prediction behavior in DETRs, well-localized or correctly classified predictions from earlier stages may degrade in later ones, and some negative predictions become increasingly overconfident. As a result, relying solely on the current stage's predictions yields inaccurate and inconsistent supervision. To address this issue, we propose Teacher Prediction Refinement Distillation (TPRD), a plug-and-play module that refines teacher predictions before distillation by exploiting stage-wise prediction information. TPRD improves supervision quality through Positive Prediction Correction (PPC), which corrects degraded positive predictions by restoring more accurate ones from earlier stages, ensuring reliable localization and classification signals, and Negative Prediction Suppression (NPS) suppresses the influence of overconfident negatives, preventing them from providing misleading supervision to the student. To preserve informative dark knowledge, we further introduce Maximum Dark Knowledge Preservation (MDKP), which selectively refines target-class logits while retaining non-target relations. Extensive experiments on MS COCO and PASCAL VOC demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [46] [Beyond the Foreground: FOV-Aware Polyp Image Synthesis via Lesion-Guided Adaptive Mucosal Context Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19966)
*Tong Wang,Yuting He,Bin Ren,Yutong Xie,Guanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LAMP：首个基于病灶引导自适应黏膜上下文传播的前景引导息肉图像合成框架，显式分离病灶、有效黏膜与镜头外部区域，显著提升生成质量并稳定提升下游分割模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜标注稀缺，合成图像-掩码对可缓解该问题，但真实合成需在保留给定病灶的同时生成相容黏膜。现有前景引导方法将所有非前景像素视为背景并依赖局部融合，直接用于结肠镜会带来两类问题：非黏膜黑色区域污染生成组织；局部推理导致黏膜纹理与光照不一致。

Method: 提出 LAMP：(1) 用视场（FOV）掩码显式分离病灶、有效黏膜与镜头外部区域；(2) 病灶到黏膜交叉注意力（Lesion-to-Mucosa cross-attention）为有效黏膜位置提取病灶外观条件；(3) FOV 约束的多方向 Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value 在合法组织支撑域上传播条件；(4) 自适应门控将其残差融合进扩散 U-Net。

Result: 在五个息肉数据集上的大量实验表明，LAMP 在整体生成质量上大幅优于现有方法，并一致地提升五个下游分割模型的表现。

Conclusion: LAMP 通过显式区域分离与全局上下文传播，解决了前景引导合成中背景污染与局部不一致问题，为医学图像合成中的前景-背景协调提供了可推广的建模范式；代码将开源。

Abstract: Synthetic image and mask pairs can alleviate scarce colonoscopy annotations, but realistic synthesis requires preserving the supplied lesion while generating compatible mucosa. Existing foreground-guided methods treat all non-foreground pixels as background and rely mainly on local integration. Directly applying them to colonoscopy causes two problems: non-mucosal black regions contaminate generated tissue, and local reasoning produces inconsistent mucosal texture and illumination. We propose LAMP, the first foreground-guided framework for polyp image synthesis based on lesion-guided adaptive mucosal context propagation. LAMP explicitly separates the lesion, valid mucosa, and camera exterior using a field-of-view (FOV) mask. Lesion-to-Mucosa cross-attention extracts lesion appearance conditions for valid-mucosa locations, while FOV-constrained multidirectional Vision Receptance Weighted Key Value propagates them over legal tissue support. An adaptive gate then controls their residual fusion into the diffusion U-Net. Extensive experiments on five polyp datasets demonstrate that LAMP substantially outperforms existing methods in overall generation quality and consistently improves five downstream segmentation models. Our code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [47] [An Event Preserving Velocity Invariant Representation for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19973)
*Mikihiro Ikura,Luna Gava,Jiahang Wu,Chiara Bartolozzi,Arren Glover*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCARF（Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields），一种实时、速度不变的event相机事件表示方法，保留原始事件而非转换为图像式表示，同时处理快速运动、静止场景和独立运动物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: event相机的异步独立像素电路带来低延迟、高时间分辨率优势，但也引入算法挑战：慢速运动下观测缺失、快速运动下运动模糊。现有速度不变表示大多通过将事件转换为图像式表示而丢弃了时间信息。

Method: SCARF构造一组中心激活感受野（Centre Active Receptive Fields）的事件集合表示，直接在原始事件层面聚合，无需图像化转换，从而保留时间信息并保持速度不变性；能够一致地处理快速运动、静止场景与独立运动物体。

Result: 在计算效率和表示质量两方面均取得state-of-the-art性能（摘要仅给出定性结论，未提供具体指标/数据集数值）。

Conclusion: SCARF为event相机提供了一种实时、速度不变且保留原始事件时间信息的表示范式，适用于实时视觉任务。

Abstract: Event cameras provide low-latency, high temporal resolution perception for real-time vision tasks such as this http URL novel circuitry (i.e. asynchronous, independent pixels) that enables these advantages also introduces new algorithmic challenges. Velocity-invariant representations alleviate missing observations under slow motion and motion blur under fast motion, but most discard temporal information by converting events into image-like representations. We propose Set of Centre Active Receptive Fields (SCARF), a real-time velocity-invariant representation that preserves raw events while consistently handling fast motion, stationary scenes, and independently moving objects. SCARF achieves state-of-the-art performance in both computational efficiency and representation quality.

</details>


### [48] [QCPruner: Query-Conditioned Population Coverage for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19990)
*Shengli He,Yongchao Liang,Roumeng He,Junjie Zeng,Jiyuan He,Can Wu,Li Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCPruner 提出免训练的双边效用加权视觉 token 剪枝方法，将查询效用同时作用于视觉目标与候选代表，基于视觉亲和度的覆盖实现非负设施选址目标（单调次模，保留 1-1/e 贪心保证），在 LLaVA-1.5/NeXT/Video 与 Qwen2.5-VL 上取得最高平均相对性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 的视觉 token 负载高，需要在固定预算下进行免训练剪枝以减少后层计算；剪枝既要保留与查询相关的证据，又要避免冗余。现有方法只做排序、多样性子集选择或覆盖率优化，未使用共享的"逐视觉查询效用"同时对视觉目标与候选代表进行加权。

Method: 提出 QCPruner：以关键词匹配的查询锚点为条件，融合两种跨模态线索形成查询效用，并将该效应用于视觉亲和度覆盖框架中的视觉目标与候选代表两端（双边效用加权）。由此构造的非负设施选址目标函数具有单调性与次模性，保留标准 (1-1/e) 贪心近似保证，且无需模型训练或参数更新。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-Video、Qwen2.5-VL 上，于每个报告 token 预算下均取得所评估完整系统剪枝方法中最高平均相对性能。LLaVA-1.5-7B 上 32/576 token 保留 96.1% 无剪枝性能（最强基线 93.9%）；Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上 256/1296 token 为 96.7% 对 92.5%。

Conclusion: 双边查询条件效用加权可同时刻画视觉目标与候选代表，使次模覆盖剪枝在免训练设定下更有效；该方法在多个 MLLM 骨干与多种 token 预算下一致优于现有完整系统剪枝方法。

Abstract: The high visual-token load in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) motivates training-free pruning to reduce later-layer computation, but under a fixed budget, pruning must preserve query-relevant evidence while avoiding redundancy. Existing methods rank tokens, diversify selected subsets, or optimize coverage without using a shared per-visual query utility to weight both visual targets and candidate representatives. We introduce QCPruner, which makes both roles query-conditioned through bilateral utility weighting. Using keyword-matched query anchors, QCPruner fuses two cross-modal cues into utility and applies it to both visual targets and candidate representatives within visual-affinity-based coverage. The resulting nonnegative facility-location objective is monotone and submodular, retains the standard (1-1/e) greedy guarantee, and requires no model training or parameter updates. Across LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-Video, and Qwen2.5-VL, QCPruner achieves the highest average relative performance among evaluated complete-system pruning methods at every reported token budget. At 32 of 576 tokens on LLaVA-1.5-7B, it retains 96.1% of unpruned performance, versus 93.9% for the strongest evaluated baseline. At 256 of 1296 tokens on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, the corresponding values are 96.7% and 92.5%.

</details>


### [49] [AVTrace: Diagnosing Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning in Omni Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19991)
*Longyin Zhang,Parth Sakhare Mahendra,Chengwei Wei,Ning Zhang,Lim Ming Chong,Sirui He,Ai Ti Aw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AVTrace 音视频时序推理评测套件（34,114 训练样本，dev 3,500 / test 7,000），覆盖起始与区间定位、同步性、下一步预测、跨模态定位、事件链解析与事件条件理解；五个开源 omni 模型均低于同步验证的多数类基线 0.556，参数高效时序后训练可小幅提升 Gemma4-E4B-it。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 omni 模型能描述视频内容，但是否真能定位事件时间、保持事件顺序、判断音视频同步尚不清楚；语义参考文本重叠常被误当作时序定位能力的代理指标，需要一个能区分各子任务的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建 silver-standard 诊断套件 AVTrace，包含 onset/span grounding、synchronization、next-step prediction、cross-modal localization、chain parsing、event-conditioned comprehension 等任务；在各自输入配置下评测五个开源 omni 模型，采用 reference-blind 响应归一化加确定性评分；对 Qwen3-Omni-30B 做开发集扰动分析（模态移除、视觉输入处理变化）；对 Gemma4-E4B-it 做参数高效时序后训练；并在三个外部图像基准上检验迁移效应与 teacher-forcing perplexity。

Result: 五个现成系统在同步验证上均低于测试集多数类基线 0.556，在事件链解析与事件条件定位/理解上得分偏低；Qwen3-Omni-30B 对模态移除和视觉输入处理变化表现为任务相关敏感性，但未能分离其底层原因；参数高效时序后训练在若干指标上提升 Gemma4-E4B-it；三个外部图像基准上任务指标仅小幅变化（含部分退化），而 teacher-forcing perplexity 下降。

Conclusion: 语义参考文本重叠不能作为时序定位的代理指标；AVTrace 能定位任务特定弱点，并可作为时序后训练的测试平台。

Abstract: Omni models can describe video content, but can they locate events in time, preserve event order, and judge audio-visual synchronization? We introduce AVTrace (Audio-Visual Temporal Reasoning Assessment and Capability Evaluation), a silver-standard diagnostic suite spanning onset and span grounding, synchronization, next-step prediction, cross-modal localization, chain parsing, and event-conditioned comprehension. It contains 34,114 training examples and category-balanced development and test splits of 3,500 and 7,000 examples. We evaluate five open omni models under their respective input configurations using reference-blind response normalization followed by deterministic scoring. All five off-the-shelf systems score below the test split's majority-label baseline of 0.556 on synchronization verification, and obtain low scores on chain parsing and event-conditioned grounding and comprehension. Development-set perturbations reveal task-dependent sensitivity in Qwen3-Omni-30B to modality removal and changes in visual input processing, without isolating their underlying causes. Parameter-efficient temporal post-training improves Gemma4-E4B-it on several benchmark metrics. On three external image benchmarks, task metrics change modestly, including some degradations, while teacher-forcing perplexity decreases. Together, these findings show that semantic reference-text overlap should not be treated as a proxy for temporal localization, and that AVTrace can identify task-specific weaknesses while providing a testbed for temporal post-training.

</details>


### [50] [GRF-Recon: Global Ray-Field Optimization for Long-Sequence Feed-forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20012)
*Enpeng Li,Yunzhou Zhang,Zhiyao Zhang,Dexuan Lyu,Chenyu Wang,Chiyuan Cui,Cheng Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的前馈3D重建框架，通过粗到细轨迹对齐、LoRA注入单目几何先验、混合权重稀疏光线场优化与联合光线误差的轨迹拼接，实现长单目序列的稳定可扩展重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建效率高，但扩展到大规模单目场景受限于GPU显存、局部几何退化与长期轨迹漂移；现有分块优化策略几何约束有限，难以维持全局一致性。

Method: 1) 粗到细轨迹对齐 + 轻量几何先验注入；2) 通过LoRA适配将单目几何线索蒸馏进前馈主干，提升细结构深度精度并保持推理效率；3) 混合权重稀疏光线场优化，用高频几何特征引导局部点云精化并施加帧间光线约束；4) 高效轨迹拼接策略联合光线误差优化，显式抑制累积漂移。

Result: 大量实验表明轨迹精度可与代表性SLAM系统竞争，同时在大规模场景中保持全局一致的3D重建。

Conclusion: 分块式优化可通过跨帧几何耦合与轨迹拼接获得可扩展的全局一致性前馈重建，逼近SLAM级轨迹精度。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction provides an efficient paradigm for scene modeling from image sequences. Scaling these models to large monocular scenarios are constrained by excessive GPU memory footprint, degraded local geometry, and long-term trajectory drift. Existing chunk-based optimization strategies provide limited geometric constraints and fail to maintain global consistency over extended trajectories. We present a unified framework for stable and scalable feed-forward 3D reconstruction from long monocular sequences. Our approach builds on coarse-to-fine trajectory alignment augmented by lightweight geometric prior injection. Distilling monocular geometric cues into the feed-forward backbone via LoRA adaptation improves depth accuracy on fine structures while preserving inference efficiency. We introduce a hybrid-weight sparse ray-field optimization that leverages high-frequency geometric features to guide local point-cloud refinement and enforce consistent inter-frame ray constraints. Unlike prior chunk-based methods, this establishes strong cross-frame geometric coupling while maintaining scalability. Finally, an efficient trajectory stitching strategy with joint ray-error optimization explicitly reduces accumulated drift. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves competitive trajectory accuracy compared with representative SLAM systems, while maintaining globally consistent 3D reconstruction in large-scale scenarios.

</details>


### [51] [Astronex-World 1.0: Real-Time Interactive World Model Foundation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20034)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Astronex-World 1.0 是一个开源可控视频世界模型基础模型，基于 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B，支持相机轨迹、连续动作与具身标识控制，含双向与因果（block-causal + KV cache）两种生成模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型缺乏细粒度的相机-动作联合可控性、持久生成能力与低成本训练/推理方案；同时缺少面向具身智能与自动驾驶的可扩展动作接口。

Method: 以 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B 为先验，用 PRoPE 注入相机内外参，用 64 维动作流调制每层 Transformer；五阶段训练：双向相机/动作控制 → 转 block-causal → 少步蒸馏 → 混合域动力学恢复 → 非对称 DMD/DMD2 分布匹配；支持在 rollout 指定位置插入文本事件。

Result: 因果模型输出 832x480@24fps；全部五阶段训练仅用 2×NVIDIA L20 48GB，单卡可实时流式生成；WBench Navi 73.5、WBench Full 70.0；有13.6B LongCat-Video、14B Helios，接近 22B LTX-2.3，优于同源 5B 先验在后训练的 YUME 1.5。

Conclusion: 以 5B 规模实现可控、实时、可分发的视频世界模型，并预留动作输入输出接口，面向具身智能与自动驾驶后训练；体现小算力训练与低延迟推理的可行性。

Abstract: We present Astronex-World 1.0, an open controllable video world-model foundation. Given a text prompt (text-to-video) or an initial observation (image-to-video), the model predicts future visual states under frame-aligned camera trajectories, continuous actions, and an embodiment identifier, and accepts text events inserted at a specified position of a rollout. The family provides a bidirectional model for full-context generation and a causal model with block-causal attention and cross-block KV caching for persistent generation, both built on the Wan2.2-TI2V-5B prior. PRoPE injects camera intrinsics and extrinsics, while a 64-dimensional action stream modulates every Transformer layer. A five-stage training path develops bidirectional camera and action control, converts the backbone to block-causal generation, distills a few-step student, restores mixed-domain dynamics, and applies asymmetric DMD/DMD2 distribution matching. The causal model generates 832x480 video at 24 fps. All five training stages run on two NVIDIA L20 48 GB GPUs, and the causal model streams in real time on one. It scores 73.5 on WBench Navi and 70.0 on WBench Full. On Full, this 5B model is above the 13.6B LongCat-Video and the 14B Helios, within one point of the 22B LTX-2.3, and above YUME 1.5, which is post-trained from the same 5B prior on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. The reserved action input and output interfaces allow post-training for embodied intelligence and autonomous driving.

</details>


### [52] [A Free Lunch? Adapting PP-OCRv6 for Historical Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20064)
*Benjamin Kiessling*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究将轻量级无循环文本识别器PP-OCRv6适配于历史手写体/印刷体行识别，在拉丁与阿拉伯文字材料上系统对比CRNN基线及基于Qwen3.5的Medusa大模型识别器，发现异构预训练显著提升泛化性，微调后的PP-OCRv6在历史拉丁文字HTR上可超越大型VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型虽报告分数亮眼，但在历史自动文本识别（ATR）中因计算成本高、依赖大规模预训练及幻觉问题而难以实用落地；历史ATR仍主要依赖紧凑的CRNN行识别器。轻量级无循环识别器有潜力兼具大模型精度与CRNN的实用性，但尚未在历史书写材料上获得全面评估。

Method: 适配近期紧凑文本识别器PP-OCRv6（不含强语言建模）用于历史行识别，与传统CRNN进行多阶段对比：广义预训练、领域特定训练、语料级微调、手稿级少样本适配，覆盖多语言拉丁与阿拉伯文字材料；并与基于Qwen3.5的Medusa识别器进行对比。

Result: 从零训练时PP-OCRv6未能稳定超越CRNN基线；但异构预训练带来显著更好的泛化能力。经微调的PP-OCRv6可超越面向历史拉丁文字HTR定制的大型VLM（Medusa）。

Conclusion: 轻量级无循环识别器经合理的异构预训练与微调，可在历史ATR中以低计算成本达到甚至超越大型VLM的精度，为历史文本识别提供实用替代方案。

Abstract: Despite impressive reported scores, large vision-language models have seen limited practical uptake in historical automatic text recognition because of their computational cost, dependence on large-scale pretraining, and hallucination. Historical ATR therefore continues to rely largely on compact CRNN line recognizers, which are visually grounded and trainable on modest data. Lightweight recurrence-free recognizers promise the accuracy of larger models with the practical advantages of CRNNs, yet have not been comprehensively evaluated on historical writing. We adapt PP-OCRv6, a recent compact text recognizer without strong language modeling, for historical line recognition and compare it with a conventional CRNN across generalized pretraining, domain-specific training, corpus-level fine-tuning, and manuscript-specific few-shot adaptation on multilingual Latin- and Arabic-script material. While PP-OCRv6 does not consistently outperform the baseline when trained from scratch, heterogeneous pretraining produces markedly better generalization. Comparisons with the Qwen3.5-based Medusa recognizer further show that fine-tuned PP-OCRv6 can outperform a large VLM tailored towards historical Latin-script HTR.

</details>


### [53] [PointEvent: Rethinking Event-based Tiny Object Detection via Serialized Motion Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20066)
*Zongze Wu,Baofeng Jia,Weiqi Yan,Jingyuan Zhang,Yu Zang,Xiaoyu Chen,Jing Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointEvent 提出序列化运动证据累积范式，将事件流组织为局部保持的空时路径与时序保持的时间路径，用轻量事件级状态空间框架交替扫描，实现对远距离异步事件的连续性建模，在微小无人机检测上以最少参数和最快推理达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机时间分辨率高、运动敏感，适合微小无人机检测，但远距离目标事件稀疏、碎片化，易被杂波和自运动淹没。现有方法依赖稠密事件表示或局部稀疏时空建模，导致计算冗余或跨远距离异步事件的运动连续性建模碎片化。

Method: 提出"序列化运动证据累积"，将运动连续性视为有序的证据传播过程：通过潜在互补序列化，把同一事件流分别组织为保留局部性的空时路径与保留时序的时间路径；据此设计 PointEvent——轻量事件级状态空间框架，在互补顺序上交替进行序列化扫描，逐步整合超出固定局部邻域的碎片化运动证据；并用高分辨率事件分支保留细粒度目标响应，紧致上下文调制抑制干扰。

Result: 实验表明 PointEvent 在参数量最少、实测推理速度最快的情况下取得 SOTA 性能；代码已开源。

Conclusion: 以序列化状态空间建模替代稠密表示与局部稀疏建模，可有效累积稀疏碎片化的远距离运动证据，为事件相机微小目标检测提供高效轻量方案。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and motion sensitivity for tiny UAV detection, yet distant targets generate sparse and fragmented events that are easily overwhelmed by clutter and ego-motion. Existing methods mainly rely on dense event representations or local sparse spatiotemporal modeling, resulting in redundant computation or fragmented modeling of motion continuity across distant asynchronous events. To address this limitation, we introduce serialized motion evidence accumulation, which treats motion continuity as an ordered evidence propagation process. Specifically, the same event stream is organized into locality-preserving spatiotemporal paths and chronology-preserving temporal paths through the latent complementary serializations. Based on this principle, we propose PointEvent, a lightweight event-wise state-space framework that alternates serialized scans across the complementary orders, progressively consolidating fragmented motion evidence beyond fixed local neighborhoods. A high-resolution event branch preserves fine-grained target responses, while compact context modulation suppresses interference. Experiments demonstrate that PointEvent achieves SOTA with the fewest parameters and fastest measured inference among the compared methods. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [54] [G^2RA-NET: Graph-based Cross-Slice Relation Modeling with Attention Gating for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20088)
*Shengye Wang,Zonglin Wu,Liang Fan,Yule Xue,Haozhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 G²RA-Net 医学图像分割框架，用图神经网络建模跨切片关系并结合注意力门控提升各向异性体积图像的分割一致性与边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性医学体积图像中切片间关联建模效率低，现有方法难以兼顾局部特征表达与体数据上下文，导致分割一致性和精度受限。

Method: GSRM 将每层切片视为图节点，通过图消息传递跨连续切片传播语义上下文；CSAG 用注意力引导的特征调制筛选相关邻域上下文并强化目标解剖区域。整体为图关系建模 + 注意力门控的端到端分割框架。

Result: 在脑 MRI 与腹部 CT 数据集上，分割精度与边界质量优于代表性方法，消融实验验证了各模块设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 图结构跨切片建模与注意力门控结合可有效提升各向异性医学体积分割性能，但缺乏公开代码与更广泛数据集验证。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation supports quantitative clinical analysis and computer-aided diagnosis. Recent methods for medical image segmentation have improved both local feature representation and volumetric context modeling. However, existing methods still strug- gle to efficiently model cross-slice relations in anisotropic volumet- ric images, limiting segmentation consistency and accuracy. This pa- per proposes G^2RA-Net, a medical image segmentation framework that combines graph-based cross-slice relation modeling with atten- tion gating. Graph-Based Slice Relationship Modeling (GSRM) cap- tures anatomical dependencies across consecutive slices by repre- senting each slice as a graph node and propagating semantic con- text through graph message passing. The Cross-Slice Attention Gate (CSAG) then selects relevant neighboring context and emphasizes target anatomical regions through attention-guided feature modula- tion. Experiments on brain MRI and abdominal CT datasets demon- strate that G^2RA-Net outperforms representative methods in seg- mentation accuracy and boundary quality. Ablation studies further validate the proposed design.

</details>


### [55] [A Smaller Transformer in Your Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20100)
*Dhananjay Tomar,Marius Aasan,Andreas Kleppe,Adín Ramírez Rivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Transformer-Within-Transformer（TWT），一种后处理方法，将连续的冗余ViT层融合为单个学习到的代理层，在减半深度的条件下保持性能，并在病理学任务上持平或提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在深度方向上存在局部相似的计算阶段，暗示层间冗余；但现有利用该冗余的方法要么无法减少推理计算量，要么严重损害模型表达能力。

Method: 形式化块冗余的统一视角，解耦几何结构与特定代理干预；据此提出TWT，将若干连续冗余层融合成一个可学习的代理层，实现事后（post-hoc）参数与推理计算压缩。

Result: 参数量与推理计算量下降；在自然图像上以一半深度达到与原模型相当的性能；在多组下游组织病理学设置中持平甚至优于原始基线。

Conclusion: 冗余层融合可在不牺牲表达力的前提下实现有效深度压缩，为ViT后处理加速提供统一框架。

Abstract: Recent findings indicate that Vision Transformers settle into locally similar computational phases, implying a level of depthwise computational redundancy. However, existing methods to exploit this redundancy either fail to reduce inference compute or severely degrade model expressivity. In this work, we formalise a unified view of block redundancy that decouples the geometry from specific surrogate interventions. We then introduce Transformer-Within-Transformer (TWT), a post-hoc method that fuses contiguous groups of redundant layers into a single learned surrogate layer. TWT reduces parameter count and inference compute while remaining competitive with original models using half the depth on natural images, and in several downstream histopathology settings, TWT matches or even improves on the original baseline.

</details>


### [56] [AnyviewMeter: Adapting Robotic Reward Models with Camera Geometry and Multi-View Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20106)
*Yuang Tu,Runjia Tan,Yujie Yan,Jinghan Hu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyviewMeter：一种几何条件化的机器人奖励模型适配框架。结合LoRA微调、token对齐的Plücker射线条件与同步块注意力，在预训练Robometer上实现视图不变的进度奖励预测，支持单视图与联合多视图评估，显著降低视角变化与遮挡下的进度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人奖励模型从视觉观测评估任务执行，但其预测会随相机视角和遮挡变化而改变，即便任务状态未变。因此将预训练奖励模型适配到本地任务时，必须考虑任务是以何种视角被观测的，需要显式引入相机几何信息。

Method: 提出AnyviewMeter框架：在预训练Robometer上做参数高效适配，融合低秩微调（LoRA）与token对齐的Plücker射线条件，将相机几何注入视觉特征及注意力的查询与键；同时采用同步块注意力，在预训练解码器内部融合同步的多视图信息；支持单视图奖励预测与联合多视图评估两种模式。

Result: 在PickCube上，单视图适配在所有相机组中均改善进度预测，视场变化下平均绝对误差相较RGB微调降低约21%。在多个仿真操作任务中，联合多视图预测相较单视图RGB平均，进度误差降低41–69%，并在约88%的任务-相机组中改善时间排序。在固定相机与腕部相机的真实任务中，MAE相较平均RGB微调下降约21%。

Conclusion: 相机几何条件与联合多视图证据是任务特定机器人奖励模型适配的有效组成部分，能显著提升视角变化与遮挡下的鲁棒性，并已获得仿真与真实任务的实证支持。

Abstract: Robotic reward models evaluate task execution from visual observations, but their predictions can change with camera viewpoint and occlusion even when the underlying task state is unchanged. Adapting a pretrained reward model to a local task therefore requires accounting for how that task is observed. We introduce AnyviewMeter, a geometry-conditioned adaptation framework for robotic reward models that represent task progress as a scalar reward signal. It combines low-rank fine-tuning with token-aligned Plucker rays and synchronous block attention: ray conditioning incorporates camera geometry into visual features and attention queries and keys, while block attention fuses synchronized views inside the pretrained decoder. The framework supports both single-view reward prediction and joint multi-view evaluation through parameter-efficient adaptation of a pretrained Robometer model. On PickCube, single-view adaptation improves progress prediction in every camera group and reduces mean absolute error under a changed field of view by approximately 21% relative to RGB fine-tuning. Across simulated manipulation tasks, joint multi-view prediction reduces progress error by 41-69% compared with averaging single-view RGB predictions and improves temporal ordering in approximately 88% of task-camera groups. On real tasks with fixed and wrist-mounted cameras, mean absolute error decreases by approximately 21% relative to averaged RGB fine-tuning. These results support camera geometry and joint visual evidence as useful components of task-specific robotic reward adaptation.

</details>


### [57] [Cross-Modal Attention Acts as a Frequency Filter: Why Verbose Prompts Improve Robustness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20139)
*Farooq Ahmad Wani,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Mujtaba Hussain Mirza,Oleksandr Pryymak,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Iacopo Masi,Pasquale Minervini,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现视觉语言模型对图像损坏的鲁棒性受问题措辞的正反两方面影响，并归因于"问题条件跨模态注意力"在图像块上诱导的频谱滤波：冗长提问扩展滤波器频带从而提高鲁棒性，细粒度问题将滤波集中于少数视觉尺度反而更脆弱。基于Qwen3-VL与LLaVA-OneVision在GQA、CLEVR上的实验验证了该滤波器视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在图像损坏（corruption）下表现脆弱，而现有研究多关注视觉侧或架构侧的鲁棒性，忽略了文本侧——即提问措辞——对鲁棒性的影响。作者希望揭示问题表述如何双向调节VLM对损坏的敏感度，并为提升鲁棒性提供低成本、可操作的实践手段。

Method: 从"问题条件跨模态注意力"（question-conditioned cross-modal attention）出发，提出频谱滤波视角：将注意力对图像块的作用视为对视觉信号在空间频率上的滤波。通过对比冗长改写（如加入"Please look carefully..."）与语义复杂/细粒度提问（如颜色+空间关系）两种条件，分析滤波器的频带宽度与集中度；并检验当滤波器频带与损坏所占据的空间频率重合时答案漂移最大。在Qwen3-VL与LLaVA-OneVision上，于GQA和CLEVR数据集进行验证，并测试"填充提示"（pad the prompt）这一实用配方。

Result: （1）冗长改写显著提升鲁棒性，8B模型上漂移方差降低70%–81%；（2）语义复杂或细粒度问题反而加剧脆弱性；（3）两种效应均可由问题条件跨模态注意力的频谱滤波器解释，漂移最大出现在滤波器与损坏频率重合处；（4）简单的提示填充策略在图像损坏条件下仍带来可测量的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 问题措辞通过调制跨模态注意力的频域滤波特性，从正反两个方向决定VLM在图像损坏下的鲁棒性；这一"滤波器视角"具有可解释性与预测力，并导出"填充提示"的低成本鲁棒性增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are fragile under image corruption. We find that the wording of the question affects VLMs in two opposite ways. Verbose questions make VLMs substantially more robust---e.g., rephrasing "Is there a cat?" into "Please look carefully and answer: is there a cat?". Conversely, VLMs become more fragile under corruption when the question is semantically complex or finer-grained, e.g., "what colour is the cup left of the chair?" instead of "is there a cup?". Both effects stem from question-conditioned cross-modal attention, which induces a spectral filter over image patches: verbose questions broaden its frequency support, while fine-grained questions concentrate it onto fewer visual scales. The model's answer drifts most when this filter and the corruption sit on the same spatial frequencies. We test the filter view on Qwen3-VL and LLaVA-OneVision across GQA and CLEVR; verbose paraphrasing reduces drift variance by 70--81% on the 8B models. The practical recipe---pad the prompt---further yields measurable gains in accuracy, even under image corruption.

</details>


### [58] [Bridging Modalities on the Cortex: Surface-based MRI to PET Translation with a Diffusion Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20147)
*Yitong Li,Alexandra Samoylova,Fabian Bongratz,Timo Grimmer,Dennis M. Hedderich,Igor Yakushev,Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DB-SUiT：面向皮层流形的表面扩散桥框架，将MRI合成为FDG-PET皮层表面，用于痴呆诊断，跨队列与跨病理泛化良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDG-PET是痴呆诊断高敏感生物标志物，但成本高、有辐射、可及性差。MRI到PET的跨模态合成是替代方案，但现有体素级生成方法未显式建模高度褶皱的皮层几何，而疾病相关模式主要分布在皮层表面。

Method: 提出表面扩散桥框架DB-SUiT，直接在皮层流形上做MRI→PET翻译。设计条件式球面U形视觉Transformer（SUiT）：球面卷积编码器提取多尺度表面特征，瓶颈Transformer建模长程空间依赖，并融合人口学与皮层下条件以精炼合成；扩散桥保持表面拓扑。

Result: 在两个数据集（含不同痴呆类型）上高保真合成，显著优于基线。自动分类中合成PET表面较MRI提升14.2%、较PET体积提升11.3%，接近真实PET。盲法阅片：合成PET诊断准确率85.5%，MRI为75.8%，真实PET为95.2%。在含训练未覆盖亚型的外部队列上无需重训即展现跨队列、跨病理泛化。

Conclusion: 基于皮层表面的扩散桥跨模态合成可低成本、无辐射地逼近真实FDG-PET的诊断价值，并具备跨队列与跨病理泛化能力。

Abstract: Cortical hypometabolism measured by Fluorodeoxyglucose Positron Emission Tomography (FDG-PET) is a highly sensitive biomarker for dementia diagnosis. However, high costs, radiation exposure, and limited accessibility constrain its clinical utility. While cross-modal synthesis from Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) offers a promising alternative, existing volumetric generation methods do not explicitly account for the highly folded cortical geometry, where disease-related patterns predominantly reside. To address this, we introduce a novel surface-based diffusion bridge framework DB-SUiT for MRI-to-PET translation that operates natively on the cortical manifold. A conditional Spherical U-shaped vision Transformer (SUiT) is specifically designed to model the intricate cross-modal relationships while preserving surface topology. It combines spherical convolutional encoders for multi-scale surface feature extraction with bottleneck Transformers to capture long-range spatial dependencies, while incorporating demographic and subcortical conditions to refine the synthesis. Evaluated on two datasets, including subjects with different dementia types, DB-SUiT demonstrates high-fidelity synthesis that substantially outperforms other baselines. In automated dementia classification, synthesized PET surfaces improve performance over MRI by 14.2% and PET volumes by 11.3%, approaching the performance of real PET surfaces. In a blinded reader study, synthetic PET achieved 85.5% diagnostic accuracy, compared with 75.8% for MRI and 95.2% for real PET. This further demonstrates cross-cohort and cross-pathology generalization, as the model was evaluated without retraining on an external cohort that included a dementia subtype not represented during training. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [59] [Task-Oriented Semantic Feature Transmission for Multi-Task Satellite Remote Sensing over Low-SNR Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20150)
*Shuoyuan Sun,Hongyu Wang,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出任务导向的卫星遥感传输框架，跳过图像重建，直接传输多任务预训练骨干提取的语义特征，用轻量通道适配模块(CAM)压缩维度并在信道损伤后恢复特征，在AWGN下场景分类与目标检测均优于重建导向的JSCC基线，低SNR提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统"先重建后推理"范式以像素级保真度为目标，与分类、检测等下游任务目标错位，低信噪比下尤为突出。

Method: 冻结多任务预训练骨干，用轻量CAM压缩特征维度以节省带宽，特征恢复器修复信道破坏后的任务相关结构；在随机SNR训练下联合优化CAM与任务头，并施加任务级与特征级监督。

Result: 在AWGN信道下，场景分类与目标检测任务上均一致优于重建导向JSCC基线，跨SNR条件，低SNR区间增益最大。

Conclusion: 语义/任务导向的特征传输可替代像素重建范式，提升遥感下游任务性能，尤其适用于高噪声低SNR场景。

Abstract: Conventional satellite remote sensing transmission follows a reconstruct-then-infer paradigm that optimizes pixel-level fidelity, creating an objective mismatch with downstream tasks such as classification and detection, especially at low SNR. This paper investigates a task-oriented framework that bypasses image reconstruction and directly transmits semantic features extracted by a multitask-pretrained backbone. A lightweight channel adaptation module (CAM) compresses feature dimensionality for bandwidth reduction, and a feature restorer recovers task-relevant structure after channel corruption. With the backbone frozen, the CAM and task-specific downstream heads are jointly optimized with task and feature-level supervision under random-SNR training. Under the adopted AWGN setting, experiments on scene classification and object detection show consistent gains over reconstruction-oriented JSCC baselines across different SNR conditions, with the largest improvements in the low-SNR regime.

</details>


### [60] [Ischemic Stroke Segmentation and Net Water Uptake Quantification on Multicenter Non-Contrast CT Using Supervised Target-Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20151)
*Linus Britt,Maximilian Nielsen,Susan Klapproth,Andre Kemmling,Michael H. Lev,Gabriel Broocks,Rene Werner,Thilo Sentker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究基于nnU-Net构建域感知深度学习框架，在四中心801例NCCT数据上实现缺血性卒中病灶自动分割，并通过目标域微调支持净水摄取量（NWU）的量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCCT上梗死低密度及NWU的定量评估需手动/半手动勾画，常依赖CT灌注或DWI引导，限制临床适用性；跨多中心异质数据的NCCT自动分割仍具挑战。研究旨在开发并外部验证域感知的NCCT卒中分割框架，评估其支持NWU量化的可行性。

Method: 回顾性多中心研究，纳入四个数据集共801例。以nnU-Net为基础，在汉堡大学医学中心与急性缺血性卒中数据集的NCCT上训练；为适应新域，在波士顿（n=11）与ISLES（n=75）的目标域子集上微调，并在未参与微调的留出病例上评估。自动化分割与NWU值同专家参考进行比较。

Result: 病灶≥30 mL时，中位Dice为0.68（波士顿）、0.56（ISLES）。纳入以ISLES为主的小病灶后，急性病灶分割中位Dice为0.54（IQR 0.30–0.70，波士顿）；相较治疗后梗死（ISLES挑战主要目标）的NCCT病灶分割中位Dice为0.20（IQR 0.03–0.41）。自动化NWU平均绝对误差为1.37个百分点（SD 1.61，波士顿）。

Conclusion: 目标域微调可支持跨异质外部队列的纯NCCT梗死分割，但性能随域而变。该方法无需高级成像即可从基线NCCT获得低误差NWU量化，支持进一步前瞻性临床验证。

Abstract: Objectives: Quantitative assessment of infarct hypodensity on non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT), including net water uptake (NWU), requires manual or semi-manual lesion delineation, often guided by CT perfusion or diffusion-weighted MRI, limiting clinical applicability. Automated segmentation on NCCT could enable efficient biomarker extraction such as NWU but remains challenging across heterogeneous multicenter data. This study aimed to develop and externally test a domain-aware deep learning framework for ischemic stroke segmentation on NCCT and assess its suitability for NWU quantification. Materials & Methods: In this retrospective multicenter study of 801 patients from four datasets, an nnU-Net-based model was trained on NCCT scans from the University Medical Center Hamburg-Eppendorf and the Acute Ischemic Stroke Dataset. To adapt to new domains, the model was fine-tuned on target-domain subsets from Boston (n=11) and ISLES (n=75), with evaluation on held-out cases not used for fine-tuning. Automated segmentations and NWU values were compared with expert references. Results: For lesions $\geq$ 30 mL, median Dice was 0.68 (Boston) and 0.56 (ISLES). Including smaller lesions, which predominated in ISLES, median Dice was 0.54 (interquartile range [IQR] 0.30-0.70) for acute lesion segmentation (Boston dataset) and 0.20 (IQR 0.03-0.41) for NCCT lesion segmentations when compared to post-treatment infarct (primary target of the ISLES challenge). Automated NWU mean absolute error was 1.37 percentage points (SD 1.61, Boston). Conclusion: Target-domain adaptation supported NCCT-only infarct segmentation across heterogeneous external cohorts, although performance varied across domains. The approach enabled low-error NWU quantification from baseline NCCT without advanced imaging, supporting further prospective clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [61] [Needles in a Raystack: Ultra-Sparse LiDAR Occupancy Detection for Bat Tracks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20160)
*Nico Klar,Pankaj Rana,Nizam Gifary,Jakob Traub,Aamir Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 利用LiDAR射线堆栈中的体素占据检测，用轻量3D U-Net从超稀疏夜间蝙蝠飞行点云中恢复前景占位轨迹，替代失效的重建式自编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间物种（如蝙蝠）难以现场观测，LiDAR是其重要监测手段，但蝙蝠飞行在夜间形成的3D时空数据极度稀疏，标准重建损失倾向只预测背景、漏掉真实飞行路径，因此需要重新界定问题并设计适配方法。

Method: 将问题建模为以传感器为中心的LiDAR射线堆栈中的逐体素占据检测；提出轻量3D U-Net，保持时间分辨率，用跳跃连接保留空间细节，并以加权二元交叉熵结合Dice损失应对强类别不平衡；同时构建基于重建的3D卷积自编码器作为基线。

Result: 在开阔田野的真实蝙蝠LiDAR记录中（并用声学监测交叉验证），重建式3D卷积自编码器基线无法恢复前景轨迹；所提U-Net在诊断实验中恢复出稀疏前景占据，并沿蝙蝠飞行轨迹生成连贯的占据模式。

Conclusion: 稀疏占据检测范式可作为验证规模实验的实用基础，并支持后续飞行轨迹聚类以及将蝙蝠活动信息融入生物多样性感知的风机限转策略。

Abstract: Monitoring flying animals is important for understanding and protecting biodiversity, but nocturnal species such as bats are difficult to observe in the field. Using LiDAR, bat movements at night result in ultra-sparse 3D spatio-temporal data in which standard reconstruction losses tend to predict only background and miss real flight paths. We study this problem as voxel-wise occupancy detection in sensor-centric LiDAR raystacks. A lightweight 3D U-Net is proposed that preserves temporal resolution, uses skip connections for spatial detail, and combines weighted binary cross-entropy with Dice loss to handle the strong class imbalance. In real LiDAR recordings of bats over open fields, cross-checked with acoustic monitoring, a reconstruction-based 3D convolutional autoencoder baseline fails to recover foreground trajectories. In contrast, the proposed U-Net recovers sparse foreground occupancy in diagnostic experiments and produces coherent occupancy patterns along bat flight trajectories, providing a practical basis for validation-scale experiments, later clustering of flight tracks, and future integration of bat activity information into biodiversity-aware turbine curtailment strategies.

</details>


### [62] [MTF-Net: Multi-Modal Temporal Feature Fusion Network for Pedestrian Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20178)
*Md Mahfuzur Rahman,Pengzhan Zhou,A. F. M. Abdun Noor,Md Imam Ahasan,Md Mustafizur Rahman,Fang Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MTF-Net：以门控线性单元（GLU）驱动的多模态时序融合网络，联合边界框动力学、人体姿态关键点、局部上下文与场景语义，预测行人过街意图，在PIE/JAAD上达0.95/0.94 AUC并保持实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需提前预判行人意图以实现安全、主动的交互。现有方法局限在于：要么仅在单模态内部建模时序依赖，要么只在粗糙语义层级做模态融合，难以充分捕获跨模态的时序交互信息。

Method: 提出MTF-Net多模态时序特征融合网络。输入四类互补模态：边界框动力学、人体姿态关键点、局部上下文、场景级语义。在循环融合框架中引入基于GLU的自适应模块，调节跨模态信息流；设置三条专用时序编码分支，并配合注意力引导的融合头，实现多时间尺度上的可解释、高效特征交互。

Result: 在PIE与JAAD基准上，MTF-Net超过近期基于Transformer和图的模型，PIE达最高0.95 AUC，JAAD达0.94 AUC，同时保持实时推理性能，可提前数帧预测行人过街意图。

Conclusion: 可靠的拟人意图预测主要来源于有原则的多模态融合设计，而非架构复杂度的堆叠。

Abstract: Accurately predicting pedestrian intentions is crucial for ensuring safe and proactive interaction between autonomous vehicles and pedestrians. However, existing approaches often depend on architectures that either model temporal dependencies within individual modalities or fuse modalities only at coarse semantic levels. To address these limitations, we propose MTF-Net, a novel Multi-Modal Temporal Feature Fusion Network that jointly models kinematic, appearance, and contextual cues for pedestrian intention prediction. MTF-Net integrates four complementary modalities-bounding-box dynamics, human pose keypoints, local context, and scene-level semantics within a recurrent fusion framework enhanced by gated linear units (GLUs). These GLU-based modules adaptively regulate cross-modal information flow, enabling interpretable and efficient feature interaction across temporal scales. Through three dedicated temporal encoding branches and an attention-guided fusion head, the proposed model robustly anticipates pedestrian crossing intentions several frames before they occur. Extensive evaluations on the PIE and JAAD benchmarks demonstrate that MTF-Net surpasses recent transformer- and graph-based models, achieving up to 0.95 AUC on PIE and 0.94 AUC on JAAD, while maintaining real-time performance. The results highlight that reliable pedestrian intention prediction arises from principled multi-modal fusion rather than excessive architectural complexity.

</details>


### [63] [MoSSGate: Memory-Modulated State-Space Gating for Skin Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20181)
*Anum Awan,Mahnoor Buriro,Muhammad Younas Khan,Md Imam Ahasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoSSGate即插即用模块，结合边界感知空间门控、外部记忆调制器与并行2D状态空间建模，在有限算力下兼顾全局上下文与精细边界，实现皮肤病变分割的SOTA精度与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病变分割对计算机辅助诊断至关重要，但现有卷积与Transformer模型在有限计算预算下难以同时捕捉长程空间依赖与精细边界细节。这种全局上下文建模与边界定位之间的权衡常导致过分割、预测碎片化或漏检细薄边缘结构，因此需要一个兼顾精度与效率的解决方案。

Method: 提出MoSSGate——面向U-Net的即插即用模块，包含三部分：(i) 边界感知空间门控，将长程信息传播限制在有效区域；(ii) 外部记忆调制器，提供样本自适应的动态控制；(iii) 并行2D状态空间建模，以线性复杂度高效聚合全局上下文。整体设计实现自适应、上下文感知的信息传播，同时保持病变边界的锐利与准确。

Result: 在ISIC 2017与ISIC 2018基准上取得SOTA精度与高效性：mIoU分别为86.3%和85.9%，Dice分别为92.6%和90.6%，且FLOPs显著少于多数竞争性CNN方法，展现出有利的精度-效率权衡，适用于高分辨率医学图像分割。

Conclusion: MoSSGate通过门控与状态空间建模的耦合，在受限算力下有效缓解了全局建模与边界定位的矛盾，为高分辨率医学图像分割提供了一种高精度、低开销的可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate skin lesion segmentation is crucial for reliable computer-aided dermatological diagnosis, yet existing convolutional and transformer-based models often struggle to jointly capture long-range spatial dependencies and fine boundary details under limited computational budgets. This trade-off between global context modeling and boundary-aware localization frequently leads to over-segmentation, fragmented predictions, or missing thin peripheral structures. To address this challenge, we propose MoSSGate, a plug-and-play module for U-Net that integrates (i) boundary-aware spatial gating to restrict long-range propagation to informative regions, (ii) an external memory modulator that provides sample-adaptive dynamic control, and (iii) parallel 2D state-space modeling for efficient global context aggregation with linear complexity. The proposed design enables adaptive, context-aware information propagation while preserving sharp and accurate lesion boundaries. Extensive experiments on the ISIC 2017 and ISIC 2018 benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy with strong efficiency, achieving 86.3% and 85.9% mIoU and 92.6% and 90.6% Dice, respectively, while requiring substantially fewer FLOPs than most competing CNN-based methods. These results highlight a favorable accuracy efficiency trade-off for high-resolution medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [64] [A Two-Stage Multi-Scale Attention-Based Network for Weakly Supervised Cataract Fundus Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20222)
*Xiaoyong Fang,Yue Wang,Xiangyu Li,Wanshu Fan,Dongsheng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两阶段多尺度注意力网络TSMSA-Net，用于弱监督白内障眼底图像增强；先做域变换合成类真实白内障图像以解决配对数据缺失，再用多尺度注意力阶段提取细节、抑制伪影，在Kaggle和ODIR-5K上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白内障导致视力损失并妨碍后续诊断，但白内障眼底图像增强面临两个难题：真实配对的白内障-清晰眼底图像极难获取（监督信号缺失），且增强过程中视网膜细粒度细节恢复不足、易产生伪影。

Method: 两阶段框架：（1）域变换阶段，将正常眼底图像转换为类真实白内障图像，从而人工构造配对数据，规避配对采集困难；（2）多尺度注意力增强阶段，通过多尺度注意力机制学习更有效的特征，以恢复细节并抑制增强伪影；整体以弱监督方式训练。

Result: 在Kaggle与ODIR-5K数据集上，TSMSA-Net在无配对图像条件下仍优于现有最先进的白内障眼底图像增强方法，并具备一定泛化能力；增强后的图像还能提升白内障图像上的血管分割与分类性能。

Conclusion: 域变换合成配对数据＋多尺度注意力增强的组合可有效缓解配对数据稀缺与细节丢失问题，增强结果对下游血管分割、分类任务有正向增益，但方法本质属医学图像增强，与具身智能、物理AI等核心方向无关。

Abstract: Cataract is a major cause of vision loss and hinders further diagnosis. However, cataract fundus image enhancement often grapples with challenges such as limited paired cataract retinal images and insufficient recovery of fine details in the retinal images. To mitigate these challenges, we in this paper propose a two-stage multi-scale attention-based network (TSMSA-Net) for weakly supervised cataract fundus image enhancement. Our TSMSA-Net leverages the domain transformation to synthesis paired real-like cataract images, solving the problem of difficult acquisition of paired images. To further extract detailed information from fundus images and reduce the generation of artifacts during the enhancement process, we propose a multi-scale attention-based stage to learn more useful features for cataract image enhancement. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K demonstrate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms current state-of-the-art cataract fundus images enhancement even without paired images and exhibits certain generalization ability. Experimental results on Kaggle and ODIR-5K datasets indicate that our TSMSA-Net outperforms the current state-of-the-art methods for cataract fundus image enhancement, even in the absence of paired images. Additionally, it demonstrates a certain level of generalization capability. The enhancement also can improve the performance of vessel segmentation and classification in cataract images.

</details>


### [65] [Scene-Q: Confidence-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Querying of 3D Scenes with Selective VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20235)
*Juno Kim,Yesol Park,Hye-Jung Yoon,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Scene-Q：一种置信度感知的由粗到细开放词汇三维查询框架，用温度缩放归一化编码器分数，仅对低置信度查询调用推理 VLM 在 top-K 候选上重排，兼顾效率与歧义消解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内机器人开放词汇三维场景理解系统多依赖对比式图文编码器的余弦相似度检索，效率高但在标签近义或多相似实例并存时表现脆弱，易误检误匹配。

Method: 对编码器相似度分数做温度缩放校准以产生置信度估计；高置信度查询直接走快速检索路径，低置信度查询才在小型 top-K 候选集上，结合原始多视角图像与实例边界框调用推理型 VLM 进行上下文感知的重排。

Result: 在 ScanNet200 的开放词汇三维实例分割任务及真实重建场景的自然语言三维实例检索任务上均取得提升，空间类与关系类查询增益最大，同时相当比例的查询仍保留在快速路径上。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的级联检索—推理范式可有效缓解对比检索的近义与多实例歧义问题，在控制计算开销的同时提升开放词汇三维实例定位与检索性能。

Abstract: Indoor mobile robots require open-vocabulary scene understanding that grounds natural-language queries in a consistent 3D map. Many existing systems ultimately rely on cosine-similarity retrieval with contrastive image--text encoders, which is efficient but brittle when labels are near-synonymous or multiple similar instances appear. We present Scene-Q, a confidence-aware coarse-to-fine querying framework that normalizes encoder scores with temperature scaling and selectively invokes a reasoning VLM only for low-confidence cases. High-confidence queries are answered by fast retrieval, while ambiguous ones are reranked over a small top-K candidate set using the original multi-view images and instance bounding boxes, enabling context-aware disambiguation at low cost. Scene-Q improves open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation on ScanNet200 and natural-language 3D instance retrieval on real-world reconstructions, with the largest gains on spatial and relational queries while keeping a substantial fraction of queries on the fast path.

</details>


### [66] [SAGE-Yoga: Multi-Cue Learning for Yoga Pose Classification and Joint-Level Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20245)
*Hung Le Chi,Khanh Minh Huynh,Long Nghia Tran Pham,Tan Phuc Huynh,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SAGE-Yoga，一个从单张 RGB 图像统一完成瑜伽姿态分类与关节级纠正的由粗到细框架：先用 bagging 视觉骨干集成生成候选类别排序，再用 margin 门控按需触发几何验证，最后检索 medoid 参考姿态并对比关节角分布给出可解释纠正反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有瑜伽姿态分析依赖单一视觉预测，难以区分视觉相似姿态，且将姿态分类与纠正视为两个独立任务，缺乏统一且可解释的框架。

Method: SAGE-Yoga 采用由粗到细流程：(1) 基于 bagging 的互补视觉骨干集成，输出排序候选姿态类别；(2) margin-based 门控机制，在视觉预测置信时保留结果，仅对歧义样本调用几何验证；(3) 确定类别后检索 medoid 参考姿态，将观测关节角与类特定分布比较以定位错位关节；(4) 将偏差转化为可执行的纠正反馈。

Result: 在 Yoga-82 数据集上，视觉集成达到 89.0% Top-1 准确率；完整框架提升至 90.7% Top-1 和 90.1% Macro-F1。

Conclusion: 互补视觉证据与选择性几何验证的结合，提升了细粒度姿态分类效果，并实现了可解释的关节级纠正。

Abstract: Automated yoga analysis requires both accurate pose classification and interpretable feedback on pose execution. However, existing methods often rely on a single visual prediction, struggle to distinguish visually similar poses, and treat pose classification and correction as separate tasks. To address these limitations, we propose SAGE-Yoga, a unified coarse-to-fine framework for yoga pose classification and joint-level correction from a single RGB image. Inspired by how yoga instructors assess posture using multiple complementary cues, SAGE-Yoga first employs a bagging-based ensemble of complementary visual backbones to generate a ranked set of candidate pose classes. Additionally, a margin-based gating mechanism preserves confident visual predictions while invoking geometric verification only for ambiguous cases. Moreover, once the final pose class is determined, SAGE-Yoga retrieves a medoid reference pose and compares the observed joint angles with class-specific distributions to identify misaligned joints. Finally, these deviations are translated into actionable corrective feedback. Empirically, experiments on the Yoga-82 dataset show that the visual ensemble achieves 89.0% Top-1 accuracy, while the complete framework improves performance to 90.7% Top-1 accuracy and 90.1% Macro-F1. These results demonstrate that combining complementary visual evidence with selective geometric verification improves fine-grained pose classification while enabling interpretable, joint-level correction.

</details>


### [67] [Distance to Class Prototypes: Active Learning for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20248)
*Licheng Zhang,Zheng Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在检测器训练目标中加入监督对比项，构建逐目标嵌入空间，用无标注检测框到其预测类别区域的加权距离作为主动学习采样信号，单次前向即可获得，仅增加 8.3% 参数（2.89M），在 VOC/COCO 上稳定优于类别后验，并与需 3–50 次前向的集成/MC-dropout 准则相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在新场景部署深度目标检测器的主要瓶颈是标注成本，主动学习通过挑选值得标注的图像降本，但其效果取决于对未标注图像的评分信号。常用信号要么是廉价但校准差的类别后验，要么是更准但需多次前向（多模型/多次随机前向）的集成或 MC-dropout 不一致度，代价随候选池规模剧增。作者希望在单网络单次前向的预算内获得比后验更丰富的信号。

Method: 在检测器训练目标中引入监督对比损失，塑造逐目标的嵌入空间，使类内距离紧凑、类间距离分离；对每个未标注检测，计算其嵌入到预测类别所占据区域的距离，并以预测置信度加权，形成主动学习采集分数。该准则无需集成、无需辅助预测器、无需重复推理，仅增加 2.89M 参数（相对裸检测器 +8.3%），并采用单阶段检测器以便将选择决策与检测器强度解耦。

Result: 在 PASCAL VOC 与 MS-COCO 上，该方法在每一次进行选择的轮次中都优于同一检测器的后验准则，最高提升 1.08% mAP50，而运行间波动仅 0.02%–0.18%，即在噪声之外呈稳定增益；同时与需每张未标注图像 3–50 次前向的集成与 Monte Carlo dropout 准则保持竞争性。

Conclusion: 主动学习的选择信号可以在不引入集成、辅助模型或重复推理的前提下，通过对比学习塑造的嵌入几何显著增强；单次前向的嵌入距离×置信度准则在目标检测场景下是后验之外更有效且低成本的替代方案。

Abstract: Deploying a deep object detector in a new setting is limited less by architecture than by the cost of annotating data from that setting. Active learning lowers the cost by choosing which images to label, and the choice is only as good as the signal used to score an unlabeled image. That signal is usually the class posterior, which is cheap but poorly calibrated, or the disagreement across several models or several stochastic passes, which is better but multiplies inference over a pool far larger than the labeled set. We propose a signal richer than the posterior yet still read from one forward pass of one network. A supervised contrastive term added to the training objective shapes a per-object embedding space in which distance encodes class membership, and an unlabeled detection is scored by how far it lies from the region occupied by its predicted category, weighted by its confidence. The criterion needs no ensemble, no auxiliary predictor and no repeated inference, and its entire cost is 2.89M parameters, an increase of 8.3% over a bare detector. On PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO it beats the posterior of the same detector in every round in which a selection is made, by up to 1.08% mAP50 against run to run deviations of 0.02% to 0.18%, and it stays competitive with ensemble and Monte Carlo dropout criteria costing three to fifty forward passes per unlabeled image. Experiments use the single-stage detector under which the compared criteria report their results, so that the selection decision is isolated from the strength of the detector.

</details>


### [68] [Generative Verification: Rethinking the Uncertainty Signal for Active Learning of Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20262)
*Licheng Zhang,Zheng Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"生成式验证"主动目标检测范式：用独立的条件扩散模型从预测框内像素重推标签，生成分布与检测标签的不一致度作为采集信号，天然融合定位与分类误差，在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上优于多种不确定性/集成准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动目标检测的采集函数都让"被改进的模型"同时充当"被查询的模型"，信号来自检测器自身输出不确定性，因而对自信的错误检测不敏感。作者希望引入一个独立的验证者，打破这种自指结构，使采集信号能覆盖错位框、背景框和错标签三类失效模式。

Method: 构建独立的条件扩散模型作为验证器，其扩散目标不是图像而是标签表示。给定预测框内的裁剪像素，验证器反向过程随机采样，重复生成得到标签分布；分布集中度反映证据确定性（替代分类器的单点估计）。生成结果与检测器预测标签的不一致度即采集分数，无需手动加权分类项与定位项。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC与MS-COCO上，该信号优于输出不确定性、特征几何、扰动与集成类准则；在MS-COCO上每轮约提升1个mAP50，且在早期轮次（自信检测错误最普遍时）优势最大。

Conclusion: 将采集函数与被改进模型解耦（独立生成式验证器）可从结构上融合分类与定位失效模式，并优先挑出"自信但错误"的高代价样本；该机制在标准检测基准上验证有效，为主动学习/主动感知提供新范式。

Abstract: Nearly every acquisition function for active object detection shares one arrangement, in that the model being improved is also the model being interrogated. We depart from it. In generative verification an independent generative model re-derives the label of a detection from the pixels inside its predicted box, and the disagreement between the two becomes the acquisition signal. Two properties follow from the arrangement itself rather than from any tuning. A displaced box, a box on background and a correct box carrying the wrong label all yield a crop that fails verification, so the failure modes arrive already combined in one scalar and the hand-weighted classification and localization terms of existing criteria are no longer needed. And because the verifier never observes the detector confidence, confidently wrong detections score highest, although a self-derived signal reads them as uninteresting and they are the costliest to leave unlabeled. We build the verifier as a conditional diffusion model whose diffusion target is a label representation rather than an image. Its reverse process is stochastic, so repeated generations return a distribution whose concentration reports how firmly the evidence determines the label, where a classifier returns a single point estimate. On PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO the signal outperforms output-uncertainty, feature-geometry, perturbation and ensemble criteria, gaining about one mAP50 point per round on MS-COCO, with its largest margins in the early rounds where confident detector errors are most common.

</details>


### [69] [AI or Real: Detecting Partially Altered Videos Under Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20263)
*Tamoghna Chakraborty,Md Nurul Absur,Sourya Saha,Saptarshi Debroy*

Main category: cs.CV
