TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向边缘设备部署的轻量级全帧部分操纵AI生成视频检测器，通过将DINOv2-Base知识蒸馏至冻结的MobileNetV3-Small学生模型，并结合软标签迁移、注意力多样性正则、帧级监督与残差特征适配器，在5.5万样本拼接测试集上以150.4 MB模型、3.65 ms/16帧的速度逼近教师模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型的普及使检测威胁从完全伪造片段转向部分操纵视频，而现有基于4亿+参数基础骨干的检测器资源开销过大，无法满足边缘部署需求，因此需要一种无需人脸检测预处理的轻量级全帧检测方案。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏框架，将DINOv2-Base教师模型蒸馏至冻结的MobileNetV3-Small学生模型；流程包含温度退火软标签迁移、注意力多样性正则化、帧级监督，以及将ImageNet特征条件化为伪影检测特征的残差特征适配器；并针对部分操纵场景两类失效模式，通过视频内时序难负样本缓解合法场景切换误报，通过校准感知采样缓解纯真实类别的阈值误校准。

Result: 在55,393样本、假帧比例6.2%~31.2%的拼接测试集上，学生模型弥补了与DINOv2-Base教师之间58%的性能差距（AUC 0.766），在RTX A4000上每16帧仅需3.65 ms，检查点150.4 MB，符合边缘设备内存与延迟预算。

Conclusion: 研究表明通过知识蒸馏与针对性失效模式处理，可在边缘硬件上实现高效的全帧部分操纵AI视频检测，兼顾精度与部署可行性，为资源受限场景下的AI伪造视频检测提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative video models has shifted the practical detection threat from fully fabricated clips to partially manipulated footages. Although modern detectors achieve strong accuracy using foundation backbones of 400M+ parameters, their resource footprint precludes edge deployment. In this paper, we present a lightweight full-frame detector for partially manipulated AI-generated video, designed for deployment on edge hardware without face-detection preprocessing. The system distills a DINOv2-Base teacher into a frozen MobileNetV3-Small student through a pipeline that combines temperature-annealed soft-label transfer, attention-diversity regularization, frame-level supervision, and a residual feature adapter that conditions ImageNet features for artifact detection. We additionally target two failure modes specific to the partial-manipulation regime: false positives on legitimate scene cuts, addressed through within-video temporal hard negatives; and threshold-level miscalibration on the dominant pure-real class, addressed through calibration-aware sampling. Evaluation on a 55,393-sample spliced test set across fake-frame ratios from 6.2% to 31.2% demonstrates the student model closing 58% of the gap to the DINOv2-Base teacher (AUC 0.766) while running at 3.65 ms per 16-frame clip on RTX A4000 with a 150.4 MB checkpoint compatible with edge-device memory and latency budgets.

</details>


### [70] [CleanVideo: Adaptive Concept Erasure for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20267)
*Junchi Liao,Hongji Li,Wenrui Zhou,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CleanVideo：面向视频扩散模型的选择性概念擦除框架，用三模态门控控制低维子空间干预，在擦除目标概念的同时保持视觉质量与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念擦除在图像生成模型上已有研究，但扩展到视频时非平凡：目标概念在帧间与去噪步间逐步出现且形态变化，固定干预会漏擦或引入模糊、抖动与内容失真。

Method: 提出CleanVideo：在低维子空间进行选择性干预，由三模态门控机制联合处理时空视觉特征、时间步信号与文本语义，动态决定干预的位置、时机与是否干预；当代理概念可清晰定义时，将擦除内容引导至自然的替代概念，同时保留非目标内容。

Result: 在三个视频扩散模型上，CleanVideo有效擦除目标概念，同时保持视觉保真度与时序连贯性，在帧级与视频级评测中优于现有基线，并在保护流程完整的条件下优于概念恢复攻击场景。

Conclusion: 三模态门控的低维子空间干预为视频概念擦除提供了可行方案，兼顾擦除效果与生成质量，但结论依赖保护流程保持完整的假设。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to selectively eliminate undesired visual semantics from pre-trained generative models without compromising their general utility. Extending concept erasure from images to video is nontrivial. Target concepts emerge gradually and vary across frames and denoising steps. As a result, fixed interventions may miss the target or introduce blurring, jitter, and content distortion. We propose CleanVideo, a selective erasure framework that performs low-dimensional subspace intervention controlled by a tri-modal gating mechanism. By jointly processing spatiotemporal visual features, timestep signals, and textual semantics, CleanVideo determines where, when, and whether to intervene, steering erased content toward natural surrogate concepts when such surrogates can be clearly defined while preserving non-target content. Experiments on three video diffusion models show that CleanVideo effectively erases target concepts while maintaining visual fidelity and temporal coherence, outperforming existing baselines under frame-level and video-level evaluations and under concept-recovery attacks when the protected pipeline remains intact.

</details>


### [71] [Queries Knew More Than We Thought: Uncovering Latent Knowledge in Segmentation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20283)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究冻结 DETR 类分割模型的“输出选择瓶颈”：模型已算出的候选掩码中含有有用结果，但部署选择规则未将其暴露。作者提出 HYDRA，一种仅基于缓存冻结输出训练的轻量选择器，在保持基线选项与校准余量的条件下进行路由，从而在不新增查询、不重算骨干、不更新权重的情况下提升 Mask2Former、MaskDINO、OneFormer 与 SAM 3 的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割器在昂贵计算完成后仍可能失败：查询条件化候选中存在有效掩码，但部署时的选择规则无法将其暴露，造成“隐藏余量”被浪费。作者希望在不增加推理成本、不重训模型的前提下恢复这部分已有但未被暴露的能力。

Method: 首先用仅基于真值的 oracle 量化冻结 DETR 族模型已计算掩码候选中的隐藏余量。随后提出 HYDRA：仅使用缓存的冻结输出训练的小型选择器，在推理时对缓存候选打分，并与显式保留基线选项比较，仅当经过保留数据校准的余量表明候选显著更优时才执行切换。方法在训练集缓存上训练、在保留数据上校准。另以配对 LoRA 作为对照，检验轻量权重适配是否能消除瓶颈；并通过二分匹配下的查询专门化分析与 TinyDETR 受控实验验证机制。

Result: HYDRA 在 ADE20k 与 COCO 上使 Mask2Former、MaskDINO、OneFormer 最高提升 +7.41 dataset mIoU；在八个域上使 SAM 3 平均提升 +9.4 class-macro prompt-IoU，同时通过校准保留有效预测。配对 LoRA 对照显示，轻量权重适配无法消除该瓶颈：暴露的预测常持平或更差，而在适配后的候选上做路由仍可恢复精度。

Conclusion: 冻结分割器不应只以暴露的掩码来评价，还应以其抑制掉的有用候选来评价。输出选择是 DETR 族分割的独立瓶颈，可通过缓存输出上的轻量、带校准的路由有效缓解，且该效应与二分匹配下的查询专门化相关。

Abstract: Modern segmenters often fail after the expensive computation has already been done: a useful mask is present among the model's query-conditioned candidates, but the deployed selection rule does not expose it. We study this output-selection bottleneck in frozen DETR-family models. A ground-truth-only oracle first shows substantial hidden headroom in already-computed mask proposals. This raises a simple question: How can we better use the masks a segmenter has already computed but does not expose? We then ask whether that headroom can be recovered without adding queries, generating new masks, rerunning the backbone, or updating weights. HYDRA is a small selector trained only on cached frozen outputs. At inference time, it scores the cached candidates against an explicit keep-baseline option and acts only when a held-out calibrated margin indicates the selected candidate is sufficiently better. Trained on training-split caches and calibrated on held-out data, HYDRA improves Mask2Former, MaskDINO, and OneFormer by up to +7.41 dataset mIoU points on ADE20k and COCO, and improves SAM 3 by +9.4 class-macro prompt-IoU points on average across eight domains while preserving useful predictions through calibration. Paired LoRA controls show that lightweight weight adaptation does not remove the bottleneck: exposed predictions are often flat or worse, while routing over the adapted candidates still recovers accuracy. Finally, we connect the effect to query specialization under bipartite matching and verify it in a controlled TinyDETR study. These results show that frozen segmenters should be evaluated not only by the masks they expose, but also by the useful candidates they suppress.

</details>


### [72] [TinyCNN: A 193K-Parameter Network for On-Device Plant Disease Detection, with a Cross-Dataset Robustness Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20290)
*Ngoc-Bao Ho-Lam,Thai-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅19.3万参数的轻量卷积网络TinyCNN，用于端侧植物病害分类，PlantVillage达98.88%准确率，并分析知识蒸馏压缩与跨数据集鲁棒性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限地区专家诊断稀缺但移动设备普及，需要可端侧部署的低功耗病害识别模型，契合SDG 2与Green AI原则，通过减少参数与算力降低推理能耗与成本。

Method: TinyCNN采用深度可分离卷积块，193,190个可训练参数，224×224输入下110.05M MACs；在38类PlantVillage基准上评测；对vanilla知识蒸馏做α∈{0.3,0.5,0.7}与T∈{2,4}的消融，选定α=0.3、T=4；用Grad-CAM分析PlantVillage→PlantDoc跨数据集失败的注意力来源。

Result: PlantVillage测试准确率98.88%、macro-F1 98.03%，比ResNet18小约58倍、比MobileNetV2教师小11.8倍；蒸馏后TinyCNN准确率98.81%；跨数据集到PlantDoc出现显著鲁棒性差距，Grad-CAM显示注意力集中于叶片外背景，符合捷径学习。

Conclusion: TinyCNN是可部署、低能耗的农业智能基础模块，但真实田间鲁棒性仍是持久落地的主要障碍。

Abstract: Detecting crop disease early is central to sustainable agriculture and food security under United Nations Sustainable Development Goal 2 (Zero Hunger), and is especially urgent in resource-constrained regions where expert diagnosis is scarce but low-cost mobile devices are widespread. This paper presents TinyCNN, a lightweight convolutional neural network for on-device plant disease classification. TinyCNN uses depthwise separable convolution blocks and contains only 193,190 trainable parameters with 110.05M MACs for a 224x224 input image. On the 38-class PlantVillage benchmark, TinyCNN achieves 98.88% test accuracy and 98.03% macro-F1 while being approximately 58x smaller than ResNet18 and 11.8x smaller than a MobileNetV2 teacher, directly reducing the energy, memory, and cost footprint of inference in line with Green AI principles. The paper further analyzes vanilla knowledge distillation as a sustainable model-compression strategy; an ablation over alpha in {0.3, 0.5, 0.7} and T in {2, 4} selects alpha=0.3, T=4, producing a distilled TinyCNN with 98.81% test accuracy. Finally, cross-dataset evaluation from PlantVillage to PlantDoc reveals a substantial robustness gap under real-world conditions, which a Grad-CAM analysis attributes to off-leaf, background-driven attention consistent with shortcut learning. TinyCNN is thus an energy-efficient, deployable building block for sustainable agricultural intelligence, while field robustness remains the key barrier to durable real-world impact.

</details>


### [73] [Not All Layers Are Equal: Dynamic Layer Routing for Reliable CLIP OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20299)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可学习的层选择问题（而非层融合），用弱OOD上下文监督训练轻量路由在CLIP各层深度上挑选稀疏、锚定末层的专家，实现高效OOD检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通过人工设计方法聚合跨层信息以提升OOD检测，但"该信任哪些层"本身是否为可学习问题尚未被探讨。作者将问题从"如何融合层"转为"对给定输入应信任哪些层"。

Method: 采用可泛化的弱OOD上下文构造方式提供监督（实验显示优于现有SOTA机制的OOD监督），据此训练一个轻量router，在CLIP的层深度上为每个输入选择稀疏的、以最后一层为锚点的专家（layer expert）。方法命名为Voyager。

Result: 在三个不同基准上OOD检测均获提升。ImageNet-1K上Voyager平均FPR@95为18.86，比最强的同类prompt-learning方法优8.8个点。增益在多种监督源下保持，包括现有SOTA prompt-learning方法所用的监督源，说明关键收益来自可学习router而非监督源。router训练约2分钟、内存占用小于1GB，比当前prompt-learning方法约高效20倍。

Conclusion: 层选择是可学习问题；稀疏、锚定末层的可学习路由能高效提升OOD检测，且性能主要归因于router组件而非OOD监督上下文，兼具实用性与高计算效率。

Abstract: Information aggregation across model layers are revealed to improve OOD detection. In contrast to crafting a method for layer-wise information aggregation in recent work, we investigate if layer selection is a learnable problem. In other words, we transpose the question from how to fuse layers to one asking which layers to trust for an input. Using a generalizable, weak, out of distribution context crafting approach for supervision, shown to be more effective than state of the art methods' mechanisms, we formulate learning a lightweight router to select a sparse, final-layer-anchored expert over CLIP's layer depth for OOD detection. Across three diverse benchmarks we demonstrate our learnable routing method dubbed Voyager improves OOD detection. On ImageNet-1K, Voyager achieves an average FPR@95 of 18.86, outperforming the strongest, comparable, prompt-learning method by 8.8 points. These gains persist across multiple supervision sources, including those used by existing state-of-the-art prompt-learning methods, demonstrating that, whilst our weak OOD supervision context is highly effective, the key advantage is realized from the learnable router component rather than the supervision source. Significantly, Voyager is highly practical; router learning takes approximately two minutes using less than 1 GB of memory, making it approximately 20x more efficient than current prompt-learning approaches. Anonymized Code: this https URL

</details>


### [74] [AgriScope: Pixel-Grounded Multimodal Understanding for Agricultural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20325)
*Abderrahmene Boudiaf,Mohamad Alanssari,Irfan Hussain,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgriScope统一像素级农业多模态框架，支持图像级/区域级/像素级理解，并构建500K图像、11M样本的AgriGround像素级指令调优数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业图像理解需细粒度识别病害、虫害、作物结构与物种，但现有MLLM仅输出文本、缺乏像素级视觉定位能力。

Method: AgriScope通过生物语义编码、稠密空间表示与像素解码，将生物专用语义表征与稠密空间定位结合；AgriGround经多阶段自动标注管线（多模态字幕生成、短语级定位、分割掩码生成、任务导向指令合成）构建。

Result: 在多个农业视觉-语言任务上验证了AgriScope在像素级多模态理解上的有效性，并建立农业视觉-语言学习与视觉定位的基准。

Conclusion: 该工作提供统一的像素级农业多模态理解框架与大规模定位数据集，数据集与代码将公开。

Abstract: Agricultural image understanding requires fine-grained recognition of plant diseases, pests, crop structures, and botanical species under complex real-world conditions. Despite recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), existing models remain limited to text-only outputs and lack pixel-level visual grounding capabilities. In this work, we introduce AgriScope, a unified pixel-grounded multimodal framework for agricultural image understanding. AgriScope jointly supports image-level, region-level, and pixel-level understanding within a unified framework, enabling tasks such as grounded caption generation, referring expression segmentation, and multi-turn multimodal interaction for agricultural imagery. AgriScope integrates biologically specialized semantic representations with dense spatial grounding through biological-semantic encoding, dense spatial representations, and pixel decoding. To support large-scale grounded learning, we introduce AgriGround, a large-scale pixel-grounded agricultural multimodal instruction-tuning dataset containing over 500K images and 11M instruction-following samples spanning plant disease analysis, crop and weed identification, insect pest recognition, and fine-grained botanical understanding. AgriGround is constructed through a multi-stage automatic annotation pipeline that integrates multimodal caption generation, phrase-level grounding, segmentation mask generation, and task-oriented instruction synthesis to produce densely grounded supervision. Extensive experiments across multiple agricultural vision-language tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of AgriScope in pixel-grounded multimodal understanding, establishing a strong benchmark for agricultural vision-language learning and visual grounding. The dataset and code will be made publicly available at ( this https URL )

</details>


### [75] [FreqDINO++: A Frequency-Guided Multi-Task Routing Vision Foundation Model for Universal Ultrasound Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20340)
*Qing Xu,Yixuan Zhang,Yue Li,Xiangjian He,Qian Zhang,Mainul Haque,Rong Qu,Wenting Duan,Jieyun Bai,Zhen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqDINO++ 是一个面向超声图像统一分析的多任务视觉基础模型，通过频率引导路由适配器、频率感知特征增强器与任务对齐协同解码器，在参数高效的前提下联合完成分割与分类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声图像分析需同时处理病灶分割与良恶性分类等任务，但视觉基础模型存在显著的自然图像—超声域间差距；现有方法多针对单一任务微调重型编码器，计算开销大且忽略了异构任务间的共性。

Method: 提出 FreqDINO++ 频率引导多任务路由视觉基础模型：MR-Adapter 实现任务通用与任务专属知识的参数高效集成；F²-Enhancer 捕获超声多尺度频率特征；TC-Decoder 通过全局-局部 token 交互促进稠密预测任务与全局预测任务的协同。

Result: 在大规模多任务与外部单任务超声基准上，于 27 类临床任务场景中一致优于强基线及最新基础模型，并对未见数据展现出良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 频率先验与多任务路由机制可有效弥合域差距并复用任务共性，为超声及其他医学影像的统一多任务基础模型提供可行范式。

Abstract: Ultrasound image analysis plays a crucial role in cancer screening and prenatal diagnosis, yet comprehensive assessment requires jointly addressing tasks such as lesion segmentation and benign-malignant classification. While recent vision foundation models have shown remarkable universal representations, unlocking their potential for ultrasound is bottlenecked by the considerable domain gap from natural images. Existing methods typically fine-tune heavy vision encoders for isolated tasks, incurring substantial computational overhead while overlooking the underlying commonalities across heterogeneous tasks. In this work, we propose FreqDINO++, a frequency-guided multi-task routing vision foundation model for universal ultrasound analysis. We first introduce a Multi-task Routing Adapter (MR-Adapter) to support parameter-efficient integration of task-common and task-specific knowledge, a Frequency-aware Feature Enhancer (F$^2$-Enhancer) is then designed to capture the rich multi-scale frequency characteristics of ultrasound images, and a Task-aligned Collaborative Decoder (TC-Decoder) is devised to promote collaboration between dense and global prediction tasks through global-local token interaction. Extensive experiments on large-scale multi-task and external single-task ultrasound benchmarks demonstrate that FreqDINO++ consistently outperforms strong baselines and recent foundation models across 27 diverse clinical task scenarios, while also showing promising generalization to unseen data. The code is at this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [Fast Cross-Strength Multi-Contrast Brain MRI Translation using Latent Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20341)
*Siddharth Srivastava,Till Bretschneider*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于条件潜在桥匹配的统一条件模型，实现跨场强MRI的可控合成，单步推理、多任务覆盖，并给出消融与开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同场强下获取的MRI在噪声、分辨率、均匀性和对比度上差异显著，限制了跨采集设置的可比性并增加下游分析难度，需要统一的场强间转换方法。

Method: 以条件潜在桥匹配（conditional latent bridge matching）为框架，构建单一条件生成模型，无需任务特定架构或分开训练，即可处理场强到场强及跨模态-场强的可控合成。

Result: 在MRIxFields2026挑战赛验证阶段三项任务上均取得极具竞争力结果；单步推理即可生成，30层轴位切片的全模态与场强组合少于90秒，全容积跨模态-场强转换少于70秒（单张NVIDIA A5000）。

Conclusion: 统一条件桥匹配模型可高效、可控地完成多任务MRI场强合成，兼顾精度与推理速度，并通过消融验证各组件作用。

Abstract: Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) acquired at different field strengths exhibits pronounced variation in noise, resolution, homogeneity, and contrast, which limits comparability across acquisition settings and complicates downstream analysis. We address this with a unified conditional model for controllable field-to-field synthesis, built on the framework of conditional latent bridge matching. Our single model achieves highly competitive results across the validation phase for all three tasks of the MRIxFields2026 challenge without task-specific architectures or training. We achieve fast generation with only a single inference step, producing all modality and field-strength combinations for $30$ axial slices in under $90$ seconds, as well as cross-modality-strength translation for a full volume in under $70$ seconds, on a single NVIDIA A5000 GPU. We further provide extensive ablations regarding different components of our solution. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [77] [EliGSiR: Continual RGB-D Mapping with Gaussian Splatting under Bounded Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20348)
*Björn Ellensohn,Elmar Rueckert,Christian Rauch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EliGSiR提出一种面向在线RGB-D的增量式3D高斯溅射建图框架，通过地图引导视角调度、负载自适应监督分辨率和定向几何增长三项机制，动态分配有限优化预算，在Replica、TUM RGB-D、ScanNet++及真实传感器序列上提升重建质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D高斯溅射假设观测集封闭且优化时间充裕，而持续RGB-D建图要求在线接收新观测并保留既有重建区域，预算受限下如何取舍视角、分辨率与几何容量成为核心问题。

Method: EliGSiR包含三个机制：Map-Guided View Scheduling过滤冗余新视角并依据当前地图状态重访保留视角；Load-Adaptive Fidelity按当前建图负载调整监督分辨率而非固定分辨率日程；Targeted Geometry Growth将深度监督与高斯创建解耦，仅在重复RGB-D观测指示结构缺失或错位处增加几何容量。

Result: 在Replica、TUM RGB-D、ScanNet++及真实RGB-D序列上评估。TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household上，使用与受控基线相同的真值位姿达21.52 dB，SplaTAM为19.42 dB；采用实时ORB-SLAM3位姿时达23.02 dB/155.5 s，CaRtGS用其原生跟踪器为20.10 dB/230.9 s。

Conclusion: 通过自适应视角调度、监督保真度与几何增长，EliGSiR在预算受限的持续建图中改善优化预算利用率，兼顾最终重建质量与获取过程中的可用地图，并相较受控基线取得更高精度与更快速度。

Abstract: Conventional 3D Gaussian Splatting assumes a closed set of observations and long optimization schedules. Continual RGB-D mapping in contrast poses the problem that new observations arrive online, while previously reconstructed regions must be preserved. We present EliGSiR (Evidence-guided Load-adaptive Incremental Gaussian Splatting with Image Replay), a continual Gaussian mapper that controls how the available optimization budget is used as the reconstruction evolves. Map-Guided View Scheduling filters redundant incoming views and reconsiders retained views according to the current state of the map. Load-Adaptive Fidelity adjusts supervision resolution to the current mapping load instead of following a fixed resolution schedule. Targeted Geometry Growth separates depth supervision from Gaussian creation and adds geometric capacity only where repeated RGB-D observations indicate missing or misplaced structure. Together, these mechanisms adapt which views are optimized, how much image detail is used, and where the representation grows while mapping remains active. We evaluate EliGSiR on Replica, TUM RGB-D, ScanNet++, and real RGB-D sensor sequences, considering both the final reconstruction and the map available throughout acquisition. On TUM RGB-D fr3/long_office_household, EliGSiR reaches 21.52 dB with the same ground-truth mapping poses used by the controlled baselines, compared with 19.42 dB for SplaTAM. In the tracked-pose comparison, EliGSiR with live ORB-SLAM3 poses reaches 23.02 dB in 155.5 s, compared with 20.10 dB in 230.9 s for CaRtGS using its native tracker. We further evaluate reconstruction throughout acquisition and show how EliGSiR adaptive view scheduling, supervision fidelity, and geometry growth improve the use of the available mapping budget.

</details>


### [78] [MM-Future: Multi-Mode Joint World-Action Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20377)
*Shuai Liu,Hechangle Gong,Hao Jiang,Runlin He,Junxiang Zhan,Kai Huang,Sheng Yang,Shaoqing Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MM-Future 提出一种多模态世界-动作模型（World-Action Model），通过联合生成多条配对的"场景-动作"假设并建模其双向交互，实现自动驾驶中决策与场景演化的耦合不确定性建模，在 NAVSIM 与 HUGSIM 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶本质上是"决策—场景演化"在多模态不确定性下的耦合过程：动作影响未来场景，未来场景又反馈影响动作选择。现有方法多将预测与规划解耦，或仅做单模态（单条）轨迹/场景推理，难以表达多模态未来与双向交互，导致在交互密集、多解场景中规划鲁棒性不足。

Method: (1) 生成多组配对的场景-动作假设，每组由一个结构化动作先验与一个独立的未来场景源初始化；(2) 使用模态感知扩散 Transformer（modality-aware diffusion Transformer）对每对假设内的场景与动作做双向协同演化；(3) 将多视角视频压缩为面向规划的表示 MM-Tokens，以支撑高效多模态 rollout；(4) 采用未来条件化的提议打分器（future-conditioned proposal scorer），结合共享历史上下文与其配对预测未来对轨迹候选排序。

Result: NAVSIM navtest 上达到 94.0 PDMS、91.5 EPDMS；在 HUGSIM 零样本闭环评测中取得 32.3 HD-Score。消融实验表明相较单模态变体与仅动作（action-only）变体均有稳定提升，验证了多模态联合世界-动作建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 将"世界模型"与"动作模型"在多模态假设层面显式耦合，并以规划导向 token 压缩支撑高效 rollout，是提升自动驾驶不确定性下规划性能的有效路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving involves coupled decision-making and scene evolution under multi-mode uncertainty. To capture this coupling and uncertainty, we introduce MM-Future, a world-action model that generates multiple paired scene-action hypotheses and models bidirectional interaction within each pair. Each hypothesis is initialized from a structured action prior and an independent future scene source, which are then co-evolved through a modality-aware diffusion Transformer. To support efficient multi-mode rollout, MM-Future compresses multi-view video into planning-oriented representations, dubbed MM-Tokens. Finally, a future-conditioned proposal scorer ranks trajectory candidates by shared history context and their paired predicted future. On NAVSIM navtest, MM-Future achieves 94.0 PDMS and 91.5 EPDMS, while attaining a 32.3 HD-Score in zero-shot closed-loop evaluation on HUGSIM. Ablations show consistent improvements over both single-mode and action-only variants, validating the benefit of multi-mode joint world-action modeling.

</details>


### [79] [Compact Vision Models for Iris Presentation Attack Detection under Presentation Attack Instrument Shift and Environmental Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20386)
*Athanasios Angelakis,Marta Gomez-Barrero*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在LivDet-Iris 2017的Notre Dame子集上,以无预训练、无数据增强的方式训练三个参数量≤0.26M的紧凑视觉模型,并通过固定验证阈值评估其在已知攻击、未知攻击、图像退化及混合测试分区上的虹膜呈现攻击检测性能,发现未知攻击下误差大幅上升,最优模型亦不具备部署就绪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键子系统在开发或验证阶段表现可靠,但在实际部署中会遇到训练数据中不存在的呈现攻击工具(PAI)或采集条件,即PAI驱动的域偏移与环境退化。作者希望量化紧凑模型在这种分布外条件下的性能衰减幅度,并检验模型间的比较优势是否能转化为部署可用性。

Method: 选用三个紧凑的、从零训练的计算机视觉模型(每个可训练参数不超过约26万),均在无外部预训练、无数据增强条件下训练并跑五个随机种子;在LivDet-Iris 2017的Notre Dame子集上评测,评估分区包括已知攻击、未知攻击、损坏图像以及池化测试集;采用验证集上选定的单一阈值原样迁移至各测试分区,报告APCER、BPCER与D-EER等指标。

Result: 从已知攻击到未知攻击呈现,APCER上升17.11–30.47个百分点,D-EER上升7.38–12.73个百分点。在验证选中阈值下,ZACH-ViT取得最低的未知攻击APCER(47.69±4.84%)与D-EER(38.87±0.93%);Compact-TransMIL取得最低BPCER;在APCER上限为10%时,ZACH-ViT的未知攻击BPCER最低(81.29±1.95%)。

Conclusion: 在未知PAI条件下所有紧凑模型的绝对误差都很高,说明最佳紧凑模型在横向比较中占优并不意味着其满足实际部署要求;未知攻击泛化与采集鲁棒性仍是虹膜PAD走向可用部署的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Iris presentation attack detection (PAD) is security-critical when a subsystem that appears reliable during development encounters presentation attack instruments (PAIs) or acquisition conditions absent from validation data. We benchmark three compact scratch-trained computer-vision models, each with at most approximately 0.26 million trainable parameters, on the Notre Dame subset of LivDet-Iris 2017 under PAI-driven domain shift and environmental degradation. All models are trained without external pretraining or data augmentation and evaluated over five seeds. A validation-selected threshold is transferred unchanged to the known-attack, unknown-attack, corrupted, and pooled test partitions. From known to unknown attack presentations, Attack Presentation Classification Error Rate (APCER) increases by 17.11-30.47 percentage points and Detection Equal Error Rate (D-EER) increases by 7.38-12.73 percentage points. At the validation-selected threshold, ZACH-ViT obtains the lowest unknown-attack APCER (47.69 +/- 4.84%) and D-EER (38.87 +/- 0.93%), while Compact-TransMIL obtains the lowest Bona Fide Presentation Classification Error Rate (BPCER). ZACH-ViT also gives the lowest unknown-attack BPCER at an APCER limit of 10% (81.29 +/- 1.95%). The high absolute errors show that the comparative advantage of the best compact model does not constitute deployment readiness under unknown PAIs.

</details>


### [80] [TouchSight: Bare-Handed Tactile Prediction from Egocentric Video via Generative Visual Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20414)
*Danyan Zhou,Jinxuan Lu,Jiawei Lin,Tianxing Chen,Chuqiao Lyu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TouchSight 提出单目第一人称视觉框架，用500小时压力手套数据训练，并通过生成模型把戴手套视频重渲染为裸手视频（TwinTouch-20H，20小时配对数据），从而在无触觉传感器的情况下实现全手密集接触力预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号对灵巧操作至关重要，但必须在接触界面直接测量，导致大规模数据采集依赖侵入式、昂贵且受限的仪器。作者希望仅从第一人称视觉中恢复密集触觉力，摆脱采集时的触觉硬件依赖。

Method: 以单目第一人称视觉为输入，基于500小时压力手套记录与大规模手-物交互数据训练；构建TwinTouch-20H：用生成式视频模型把戴手套记录重渲染为裸手外观并更换背景，同时保留原始测量触觉标签作为监督，从而弥合手套训练数据与裸手真实场景之间的外观差异。

Result: TouchSight 可同时从戴手套与生成裸手视频预测密集接触力；在 OakInk2 上超越先前接触预测方法；在未见数据集的自然裸手第一人称视频上具备定性泛化能力；随手套监督数据规模增大性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明密集触觉信号可以仅由第一人称视觉恢复，无需在采集时使用触觉仪器，为规模化触觉数据的视觉替代方案提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Tactile signals provide direct contact and force measurements that are essential for understanding physical interactions and enabling dexterous robotic manipulation. However, tactile sensing requires direct measurement at contact interfaces, making large-scale data collection reliant on intrusive, costly, and restrictive instrumentation. We present TouchSight, a monocular egocentric vision framework for dense full-hand contact force prediction that leverages 500 hours of pressure-glove recordings and extensive hand-object interaction (HOI) data. To address the appearance gap between gloved training data and bare-hand real-world scenarios, we construct TwinTouch-20H: 20 hours of paired visual data in which generative video models re-render gloved recordings as bare-hand observations against new backgrounds while preserving the original measured tactile labels. TouchSight predicts dense force from both gloved and generated bare-hand videos, outperforms prior contact prediction methods on OakInk2, qualitatively generalizes to natural bare-hand egocentric videos from unseen datasets, and improves consistently as glove supervision scales. These results demonstrate that dense tactile signals can be recovered from egocentric vision alone, without tactile instrumentation at capture time.

</details>


### [81] [WeVisDoc: From Coverage to Capability for Robust End-to-End Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20423)
*Hao Yu,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Jiabo Zhan,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeVisDoc是一个两阶段数据中心化文档解析框架：Stage I通过异构数据和结构保持的退化合成扩展覆盖，Stage II用探针集诊断残差错误并指导定向数据构建与token预算再分配，在OmniDocBench v1.6与PureDocBench上均取得领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档解析训练语料偏向常见文档类型与干净数字页，而单纯扩大覆盖范围无法指明如何解决解析器剩余弱点，因此需要以数据为中心、可诊断的框架来提升跨布局与采集条件的鲁棒性。

Method: 两阶段数据中心化框架：Stage I 用异构数据与结构保持退化合成扩展语义、结构与外观覆盖；Stage II 通过留出探针集在固定视觉-结构簇内测量Stage I解析器的残差错误，据此指导定向数据构建并重新分配目标token预算。

Result: WeVisDoc-4B在OmniDocBench v1.6上Overall达95.38，在PureDocBench三条赛道平均Overall为75.54，四项设置中均排名第一；相比Stage I，Stage II在2B与4B模型上均提升两个基准的Overall，退化赛道增益更大，Real Degraded赛道4B模型提升4.03分。

Conclusion: 以诊断驱动的数据构建与预算再分配，可有效针对解析器残余弱点提升端到端文档解析鲁棒性，且模型规模越大收益越集中于退化场景。

Abstract: Document parsing converts document images into structured content and requires reliable performance across diverse layouts and acquisition conditions. Yet training corpora are biased toward common document types and clean digital pages, while expanding coverage alone does not specify how to address a parser's remaining weaknesses. We present WeVisDoc, a two-stage data-centric framework for robust end-to-end document parsing. Stage I broadens semantic, structural, and appearance coverage through heterogeneous data and structure-preserving degradation synthesis. Stage II uses a held-out probe to measure the Stage I parser's residual errors within fixed visual-structural clusters. These diagnostics guide targeted data construction and reallocation of the target-token budget. WeVisDoc-4B achieves an Overall score of 95.38 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and a mean Overall score of 75.54 across the three PureDocBench tracks, ranking first among the compared end-to-end parsers in all four settings. Compared with Stage I, Stage II improves Overall scores for the 2B and 4B models on both benchmarks, with larger gains on the degraded PureDocBench tracks, including a 4.03-point gain for the 4B model on the Real Degraded track.

</details>


### [82] [When Do Language-Grounded Explanations Help? A Graph-Bottleneck for Farm Monitoring Interpretable Sheep Facial Pain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20427)
*Alam Noor,Miguel Guti'errez Gait'an*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于羊面部疼痛量表（SPFES）的概念瓶颈模型，先证明注意力解释不可信，再用逐区域状态标注监督概念分类器，以0.05–0.10的κ代价换取可验证的概念学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动面部疼痛识别可实现绵羊连续福利评估，但落地依赖信任：饲养员无法采信一个没有依据的分数。作者希望模型解释是真正有语义依据的，而非事后装饰，因此先检验注意力解释是否可信。

Method: 第一阶段让每个检测到的面部分区对SPFES临床描述符的文本嵌入做注意力，并配有可学习门控与生成文本；第二阶段去除外观旁路，改用概念瓶颈：分类器只读取SPFES概念分数，并用图像级流程通常丢弃的逐区域状态标注进行监督。

Result: 注意力解释不成立：消融整个描述符仅使预测logit变化约1e-4，最受关注线索与预测疼痛等级一致率仅32.6%。概念瓶颈在Cohen's κ上损失0.05–0.10，但少数类疼痛指示状态被恢复至基率的3.5–8.3倍，耳与眼的严重度排序无需严重度监督即自发出现。仅去掉监督时κ不变而概念准确率降至0.109，说明架构必要性不等于语义有效性。同时指出类别不平衡下汇总概念准确率具误导性，并给出七种方法的交叉验证、协议对齐基准。

Conclusion: 注意力与生成文本提供的解释力不等于真实语义因果效用；只有通过概念瓶颈与逐区域监督才能得到可验证学习的概念，但需以判别性能小幅下降为代价，评估时应避免不平衡下的汇总准确率。

Abstract: Automated pain recognition from facial expression could make continuous welfare assessment practical in sheep, but adoption depends on trust: a stockperson cannot act on a score that arrives without justification. We ground a model in the Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES) by letting each detected facial region attend over text embeddings of the clinical descriptors and then test whether the resulting explanations mean anything. They do not. Ablating an entire descriptor changes the predicted logit by about $10^{-4}$, and the most-attended cue agrees with the predicted pain level in only $32.6\%$ of regions, although the attention maps, the learned gate, and the generated text all proposed otherwise. We therefore remove the appearance bypass with a concept bottleneck whose classifier reads only SPFES concept scores, supervised by per-region state annotations that image-level pipelines discard. This costs $0.05$--$0.10$ in Cohen's $\kappa$ but yields concepts that are demonstrably learned: minority pain-indicating states are recovered at $3.5$--$8.3\times$ their base rates, and the ear and eye severity orderings emerge without severity supervision. Removing the supervision alone leaves $\kappa$ unchanged while concept accuracy falls to $0.109$, showing that architectural necessity does not imply semantic validity. We also show that pooled concept accuracy is misleading under clinical imbalance and provide a cross-validated, protocol-matched benchmark of seven methods on this dataset.

</details>


### [83] [Cross-Architecture Foundation-Model Distillation for Edge Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20441)
*Fabian Schmalstieg,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将3亿参数Prithvi-EO-2.0遥感基础模型蒸馏为0.7M参数的EfficientViT-B0学生模型，用教师监督无标签Sentinel-2影像扩充训练集，实现Sen1Floods11洪水分割的边缘部署（INT8 TensorRT 1.5MB，Jetson Xavier NX 5.57ms/512×512）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型洪水分割性能强，但参数量过大，难以部署在显存受限的边缘硬件上；同时人工标注的Sen1Floods11训练场景仅252个，标注预算有限。

Method: 以微调后的Prithvi-EO-2.0（300M）为教师，蒸馏到EfficientViT-B0（0.7M）学生；教师对额外无标签Sentinel-2影像生成伪标签以扩充学生训练集；设置几何匹配的对照实验排除标签来源单一解释；进一步把教师监督池扩到2,500景；最后做激活替换与量化感知训练，导出8-bit INT8 TensorRT引擎。另用固定MNDWI阈值作为谱规则基线。

Result: 在252景匹配预算下，教师监督训练与直接训练相当，并在STURM-Flood上跨配置提升；扩充到2,500景后，float学生于Sen1Floods11测试split取得0.787水类IoU（教师0.822），在STURM-Flood上达到与教师相当，WorldFloods-v2上仍略低。量化后INT8引擎1.5MB，Jetson Xavier NX上512×512图像GPU计算5.57ms，运行时显存约14MB。固定MNDWI阈值在两个干净外部基准上与两模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 基础模型监督可将固定人工标注预算放大为更大的训练集，并得到紧凑可部署的边缘模型；两个干净外部基准更应视为泛化性测试，而非学习模型优于谱规则的证据。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can provide strong flood-segmentation performance, but their size limits deployment on memory-constrained edge hardware. We distill a 300-million-parameter Prithvi-EO-2.0 teacher, fine-tuned on the 252 manually labeled Sen1Floods11 training scenes, into a 0.7-million-parameter EfficientViT-B0 student. The teacher supervises additional unlabeled Sentinel-2 imagery, allowing the student training set to grow without new manual annotations. At the matched budget of 252 scenes, teacher-supervised training is competitive with direct training and improves STURM-Flood performance across tested configurations; a geometry-matched control shows that label source alone does not explain the difference. Scaling the teacher-supervised pool to 2,500 scenes narrows the remaining student--teacher gap: the float student reaches 0.787 water intersection over union on the Sen1Floods11 test split against 0.822 for the teacher, matches the teacher on STURM-Flood under our evaluation protocol, and remains below it on WorldFloods-v2. After activation replacement and quantization-aware training, the student runs as a 1.5-megabyte 8-bit integer (INT8) TensorRT engine on a Jetson Xavier NX at 5.57 milliseconds of graphics processing unit (GPU) compute per 512-by-512 image, with approximately 14 megabytes of runtime device memory. A fixed modified normalized difference water index (MNDWI) threshold is competitive with both models on the two clean external benchmarks, so we interpret those benchmarks as generalization tests rather than as evidence of learned-model superiority over a spectral rule. The results support the conclusion: foundation-model supervision can amplify a fixed manual annotation budget into a substantially larger training set and yield a compact, deployable edge model.

</details>


### [84] [SenseFuse: Label-Free Fusion of Image and Shape Encoders for Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20475)
*Euiseok Han,Tri Ton,Hwanhee Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SenseFuse：一种无标签融合方法，在掩码标注阶段平衡 2D 图像编码器与 3D 形状编码器，实现开放词汇 3D 实例分割，兼顾互补性与自适应权重选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇 3D 场景理解是机器人空间推理与操作的基础；现有开放词汇方法在掩码标注时受限于 2D 图像特征或图像蒸馏表示，未充分利用 3D 形状信息。作者观察到 2D 与 3D 编码器的失败模式高度不重叠，而不同 2D 编码器之间错误高度相关，说明 2D–3D 组合天然互补。

Method: SenseFuse 仅改进现有流程的掩码标注阶段：融合 2D 图像编码器与 3D 形状编码器的标签预测；引入自适应机制，通过无标签敏感性度量（label-free sensitivity measure）在单场景的未标注候选提案上于毫秒级估计场景级融合权重，无需训练或真实标签。

Result: 在 ScanNet200、Replica、ScanNet++ 上各设置下标注精度均提升，恢复 oracle 权重可得增益的 67–100%（中位数 93%）；在报告的 22 项设置中 21 项提升实例 AP。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 利用 2D 与 3D 编码器失败模式的不重叠性进行无标签融合，是一种低成本、即插即用的开放词汇 3D 实例分割增强手段，为机器人场景理解提供更可靠的标注。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is fundamental for robotics, laying the groundwork for spatial reasoning and object manipulation. While closed-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation heavily leverages 3D shape information, state-of-the-art open-vocabulary methods remain predominantly restricted to 2D image features or image-distilled representations during mask labeling. In this paper, we propose SenseFuse, a label-free fusion method that balances 2D image and 3D shape encoders for robust open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation, refining only the mask-labeling stage of existing pipelines. We reveal that 2D image and 3D shape encoders exhibit largely disjoint failure patterns and rarely share identical wrong labels, whereas two 2D image encoders frequently repeat the same errors. This distinct behavior makes the 2D and 3D pair inherently complementary. We introduce an adaptive mechanism that selects a scene-level fusion weight to maximize a label-free sensitivity measure, estimated directly from a single scene's unlabeled proposals in milliseconds. SenseFuse improves labeling accuracy in every evaluated setting across ScanNet200, Replica, and ScanNet++, recovering 67-100% (median 93%) of the gain achievable with an oracle weight, and it raises instance AP in 21 of 22 reported settings. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [85] [Grounded Product Understanding in Livestream Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20508)
*Xinyu Zhang,Junjie Chen,Jiawei Ge,Qianlong Li,Libin Ma,Baokun Pan,Yahui Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GPUB大规模电商直播基准（3000实例、31K商品、多时刻时序标注），定义Grounded Product Understanding（GPrU）联合任务并给出统一模型UniPro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用商品理解基准将商品检索与时序定位割裂评估，缺乏商品身份与时序证据对应关系的联合评测，而直播中商品信息分散于多个时刻，下游应用（如商品导向直播切片）需要二者协同。

Method: 构建GPUB基准，含3000个直播实例、质量可控的多时刻时序标注与31K时尚商品目录；设计三项评测任务：主任务GPrU要求从直播视频与候选商品集中联合识别目标商品并定位支撑时刻，另设商品检索与商品时刻定位两个子任务；并提出UniPro统一模型，从共享多模态编码中导出商品对齐且时序结构化的表示。

Result: 现有多模态模型在GPrU上表现困难，最佳基线仅10.13% Pair mAP@.3；UniPro将Pair mAP@.3提升至21.53%，并在GPrU上达到37.23% Joint R@1@.3。

Conclusion: GPUB填补了商品身份与时序证据联合评测的空白，UniPro提供有效基线，但GPrU任务仍远未解决，存在显著提升空间。

Abstract: E-commerce livestreams have emerged as an important channel for presenting products to online consumers, containing multiple products whose information is scattered in different moments. This poses significant challenges for downstream product understanding applications, such as product-centric livestream clipping, where models need to identify the product and its relevant segments for information gathering. However, existing benchmarks for general product understanding typically evaluate product retrieval and temporal localization in isolation, leaving the critical correspondence between product identity and temporal evidence largely unassessed. To address this limitation, we introduce GPUB, a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,000 livestream instances with quality-controlled multi-moment temporal annotations and a catalog of over 31K fashion products. GPUB supports three evaluation tasks: the main task Grounded Product Understanding (GPrU) requires jointly identifying the target product and localizing its supporting moments from a livestream video and a candidate product set; Product Retrieval and Product Moment Localization serve as two complementary subtasks. Evaluation of existing multimodal models shows that GPrU remains highly challenging, with the best-performing baseline achieving only 10.13% Pair mAP@.3. To narrow the performance gap, we further develop UniPro, a unified product understanding model that derives product-aligned and temporally structured representations from shared multimodal encoding, improving Pair mAP@.3 to 21.53% while achieving 37.23% Joint R@1@.3 on GPrU.

</details>


### [86] [Automated Goldsmith's Mark Retrieval in Silverware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20509)
*Atmik Tiwari,Vincent Christlein,Mark Fichtner,Freya Gohlke,Birgit Schübel,Theresa Witting,Heike Zech,Mathias Zinnen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一套AI辅助的金匠标记检索流水线，结合标记定位与度量学习微调，系统比较ResNet-50、ViT-S/16与DINOv2 ViT-S/14三种主干及无裁剪/人工裁剪/学习检测裁剪三种策略，最佳配置达mAP 62.63%、Top-1 73.74%，并公开数据集、代码与网页界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术史研究中金匠标记对文物鉴定与断代至关重要，但专家需人工将查询标记与数百个已记录样本逐一比对，过程枯燥且高度依赖专业知识。作者希望以AI检索流水线减轻这一负担，并推动数字人文领域的可复现应用。

Method: 构建检索流水线：先做标记定位，再在三种主干（ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、监督式ViT-S/16、自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14）上做度量学习微调；系统评估三种裁剪策略（不裁剪、人工真值裁剪、基于学习检测的裁剪）及其与主干的交互作用。

Result: 最强配置为DINOv2 ViT-S/14 + 人工裁剪 + 度量学习微调，取得mAP 62.63%、Top-1准确率73.74%。实验表明自监督预训练与标记定位是影响最大的两个因素；学习式裁剪可恢复人工裁剪的大部分收益，且推理阶段无需真值标注。

Conclusion: 自监督预训练与标记定位是提升检索效果的关键，学习式裁剪在无标注推理下具备实用价值。作者公开人工标注数据集与代码库，并通过公开网页界面部署系统，以支持数字人文领域的复现与采用。

Abstract: For art historians, goldsmith marks play a critical role in the identification and dating of artifacts. In practice, experts must manually compare a query mark against hundreds of documented examples, a process that is both tedious and highly dependent on specialist knowledge. To address this, we present an AI-assisted retrieval pipeline that combines mark localization with metric-learning fine-tuning across three backbone architectures: an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, a supervised ViT-S/16, and a self-supervised DINOv2 ViT-S/14. We conduct a systematic evaluation of cropping strategies, where we measure the impact of no cropping, manual ground-truth cropping, and learned detection-based cropping, and assess their interaction with each backbone. Our strongest configuration, DINOv2 ViT-S/14 with manual crop and metric-learning fine-tuning, achieves an mAP of 62.63% and a Top-1 accuracy of 73.74%. Our experiments show that self-supervised pretraining and mark localization are the two most impactful factors, with learned cropping recovering the majority of the gain from manual cropping without requiring ground-truth annotations at inference time. To enable reproducibility and adoption in the digital humanities, we release our manually annotated dataset and codebase, and deploy the system via a public web interface.

</details>


### [87] [A Dual-Stream Regulated Reconstruction and Segmentation Network with Hierarchical Artifact-Prior Modeling for Ultra-Low-Field Pediatric Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20562)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Leo Milecki,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，在单一推理管线内同时完成0.064T超低场儿科MRI的质量评估、增强与多结构分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低场(0.064T)儿科MRI信噪比低、解剖边界弱、伪影频繁，导致自动质量评估、增强与分割性能受限；同时密集标注稀缺。

Method: 基于3D U-Net的双流耦合网络：先重建增强的uLF体数据，再融合原始与增强图像进行皮层下分割；引入源自全脑掩膜的非目标脑组织辅助类以稳定边界；以伪影图为条件的预测头，从重建残差与冻结的分割特征回归七项伪影评分；利用图谱到目标的微分同胚配准做标签传播，并正则化解剖重建。

Result: 在LISA 2026 Challenge三项任务上给出验证集结果（摘要未提供具体数值）。

Conclusion: 多任务耦合与配准驱动的标注传播可缓解uLF MRI的低信噪比与标注稀缺问题，为儿科uLF MRI提供统一的评估-增强-分割方案。

Abstract: Automated quality assessment, enhancement, and segmentation of multiple structures in $0.064\,\mathrm{T}$ ultra-low-field pediatric MRI are limited by a low signal-to-noise ratio, weak anatomical boundaries, and frequent artifacts. We present a unified framework for the LISA 2026 Challenge that performs all three tasks together within one inference pipeline. A network with two coupled streams, built on a 3D U-Net, first reconstructs an enhanced uLF volume and then combines the original and enhanced images for subcortical segmentation. To improve boundary stability, we add an auxiliary class covering brain tissue outside the target structures, derived from whole brain masks. A head conditioned on an artifact graph predicts the seven artifact ratings from reconstruction residuals and frozen segmentation features. We address the scarcity of dense annotations using diffeomorphic registration from atlas to target for label propagation and to regularize anatomical reconstruction. We report validation results across all three tasks.

</details>


### [88] [DocAttriBench: Benchmarking Answer Grounding in Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20574)
*Luca De Grandis,Silvia Cappelletti,William Raccagni,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Rita Cucchiara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DocAttriBench (DAB)：面向 Document VQA 的元素级答案溯源大规模基准，用 MAPPET（基于 mask 困惑度归因）自动生成 237k 文档、296k 问答的布局元素级 grounding 标注，并评测多模态 LLM 的答案准确率与溯源能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Document VQA 的答案 grounding 仍缺标注：现有基准要么无 grounding 标注，要么标注质量有限，人工构建 grounded 数据集成本高昂，因此需要可扩展的自动标注基准来推动可验证、可信的文档问答模型。

Method: 提出 MAPPET（Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution）：结合文档版面分析与语言建模，对候选布局元素（文本块、表格、图像）逐一掩蔽，测量掩蔽后困惑度上升幅度，将答案归因于对模型置信度贡献最大的元素；据此对多个已有 Document VQA 数据集自动生成元素级 grounding 标注，构建 DAB 基准，并在其上评测具备 grounding 能力的多模态 LLM（答案准确率、归因准确率、整体答案质量）。

Result: 构建出含 237k 文档、296k 问答对且带元素级 grounding 的 DAB；评测显示更大模型总体答案准确率更高，但即便最强模型也常无法定位支持答案的版面元素（答案正确 ≠ 归因正确）。

Conclusion: DAB 为开发 grounded、可验证、可信的 Document VQA 模型提供了可扩展基准，并揭示当前 MLLM 在源归因上的明显短板；数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Answer grounding in document visual question answering remains an open challenge: most benchmarks lack grounding annotations or provide limited-quality labels, while constructing grounded datasets still requires costly manual effort. We introduce DocAttriBench (DAB), a large-scale benchmark for fine-grained, element-level source attribution in Document VQA, grounding answers to specific layout elements such as text blocks, tables, and images. To build DAB, we propose a Mask-based Perplexity-Derived Attribution method (MAPPET) that combines document layout and language modeling to identify the most informative element for each answer. MAPPET measures the increase in perplexity after masking candidate elements and attributes the answer to the element contributing most to model confidence. Applying MAPPET to multiple existing Document VQA datasets yields DAB, with 237k documents and 296k question-answer pairs with element-level grounding. We benchmark grounding-capable multimodal LLMs on DAB, evaluating answer accuracy, attribution accuracy, and overall answer quality. Results show that while larger models generally achieve higher answer accuracy, even the strongest models often fail to localize the supporting elements. DAB provides a scalable benchmark for developing grounded, verifiable, and trustworthy Document VQA models. Dataset and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [RawSLAM: Online HDR Gaussian SLAM from Linear Radiance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20589)
*Marina Orozco González,Luis Merino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个在线 HDR 高斯 SLAM 框架，直接在单曝光 16-bit 线性 HDR 图像上跟踪与建图，配有无 MLP 的对数高斯颜色参数化、Reinhard 压缩光度目标与结构引导梯度加权，并发布 RawSLAM 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密视觉 SLAM 几乎只依赖 8-bit 色调映射 LDR 输入，在极端光照下阴影与高光会导致跟踪漂移和建图崩溃；而已有的 RAW/HDR 重建流程均为离线，依赖 SfM 预处理，无法应对大帧间运动。

Method: 三个核心组件：(1) 架构无关的 HDR 高斯泼溅模块，对高斯颜色特征采用无 MLP 的对数参数化；(2) Reinhard 范围压缩光度损失；(3) 结构引导的空间梯度加权。同一公式可原样运行于标准 8-bit 输入。

Result: 在轨迹与重建精度上均优于 MonoGS 的直接 HDR 适配版；在 8-bit 输入下误差约为 MonoGS 基线的一半；HDR 高斯模块可无缝迁移至 SplaTAM、Gaussian SLAM 与 DROID-W，消除这些系统在挑战性光照序列上的全部跟踪失败；并引入 RawSLAM 数据集（10 段真实室内序列，含 16-bit RAW、对齐深度、IMU 与 OptiTrack 位姿）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在线、单曝光线性 HDR 高斯 SLAM 可行，且关键组件具备跨系统可迁移性；代码与数据集即将公开。

Abstract: Current dense visual SLAM systems rely almost exclusively on 8-bit tonemapped Low Dynamic Range (LDR) inputs, limiting their robustness in extreme lighting where shadows and highlights trigger tracking drift and mapping collapse. Conversely, existing raw and High Dynamic Range (HDR) reconstruction pipelines operate strictly offline. They depend on Structure-from-Motion preprocessing and are not suited for large inter-frame motion. We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first online Gaussian SLAM framework that tracks and maps directly on single-exposure 16-bit linear HDR imagery. Our method rests on three core components: an architecture-agnostic HDR Gaussian Splatting module featuring an MLP-free logarithmic parameterization of Gaussian color features; a Reinhard range-compressed photometric objective; and structure-guided spatial gradient weighting. Combined, these components allow our approach to outperform a direct HDR adaptation of MonoGS in both trajectory and reconstruction accuracy, while rendering natively in linear scene radiance for post-rendering processing. The same formulation runs unchanged on standard 8-bit inputs, roughly halving the MonoGS baseline error. Furthermore, our HDR Gaussian module transfers seamlessly to SplaTAM, Gaussian SLAM, and DROID-W, eliminating all tracking failures these systems suffer on challenging illumination sequences. To enable this research, we introduce RawSLAM: a dataset of 10 real-world indoor sequences featuring 16-bit RAW imagery, aligned depth, IMU measurements, and external OptiTrack poses. Code and dataset will be made publicly available soon.

</details>


### [90] [PhGS: Post-Hoc Pruning and Refinement of Single-View Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Reconstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20623)
*Rinto Yagawa,Han Cheng,Dieter Schmalstieg,Hideo Saito,Shohei Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种与骨干网络无关的单视图前馈3DGS压缩流水线，冻结基模型，通过重要性评分剪枝+轻量可训练循环精修模块迭代恢复图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图前馈3DGS每条相机射线预测固定数量高斯，导致严重空间冗余；现有压缩策略多针对多视图场景、依赖跨视图一致性，无法兼容单图模型，而重训练前馈网络成本高。

Method: 保持基模型冻结的后处理方案：设计重要性评分驱动的剪枝机制，剔除冗余高斯基元；引入可训练的轻量循环精修模块，对存活基元进行迭代更新以恢复渲染质量；整个流水线不依赖特定骨干，支持推理时灵活设定保留率。

Result: 可与现有单视图前馈3DGS基线无缝集成，在保持新视角渲染保真度的同时实现显著显存缩减，并支持按应用需求调整推理时保留比例。

Conclusion: 后处理式剪枝+循环精修是通用且高效的单视图前馈3DGS压缩范式，避免重训练基模型，具有骨干无关性与推理期灵活性。

Abstract: Recent single-view feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation predicts a fixed number of Gaussians per camera ray, introducing severe spatial redundancy. Most existing compaction strategies target multi-view setups to exploit cross-view consistency and are incompatible with single-image models. Instead of retraining the base feed-forward network to directly output compact representations, our insight is to keep the base models frozen and apply post-hoc pruning and recurrent refinement to the generated Gaussians. Consequently, we propose a backbone-agnostic compaction pipeline for single-view feed-forward 3DGS that couples an importance-score-based pruning mechanism with a trainable, lightweight recurrent refinement module, which iteratively updates the surviving primitives to restore image quality. Our results demonstrate seamless integration with existing baselines while preserving novel-view rendering fidelity and achieving high memory reduction. Furthermore, our method supports flexible inference-time keep ratios for application needs.

</details>


### [91] [Refinement Is Inherently Editable: Training-Free Prompt-to-Prompt Image Editing with Generative Refinement Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20633)
*Yulong Chen,Ziqian Zhang,Haoyu Zhang,Ao He,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineEdit：无需训练的文生图编辑框架，用生成式精化网络通过二值图像码的全局精化耦合编辑定位与内容生成，在PIE-Bench九类编辑中取得最优背景保持与CLIP分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散编辑器的空间控制不精确，会导致编辑不完整或误改无关区域；因果自回归编辑器受固定解码顺序限制，难以修订早期决策。作者希望在没有额外训练、外部掩码或注意力控制的前提下，实现既能引入目标改动又能保持无关内容的高质量编辑。

Method: 提出RefineEdit，基于生成式精化网络（Generative Refinement Network）的训练无关prompt-to-prompt编辑框架。核心是对二值图像码进行全局精化，从而将编辑定位与内容生成耦合。方法从源图像的中间状态初始化编辑分支以复用已生成的布局，比较源分支与编辑分支对同一源采样比特所赋概率，用其符号差选择可编辑的位置与比特；被选中的比特沿编辑方向精化，其余比特复制源状态。为跨精化步稳定编辑，采用自适应空间冻结（限制掩码过度扩张）与有限比特锁定（使近期选中的比特保持可编辑）。

Result: 在PIE-Bench的九个编辑类别上，RefineEdit在PSNR、LPIPS、MSE、SSIM等背景保持指标上取得最佳分数，并在所评估方法中获得最高的整图CLIP分数与编辑区域CLIP分数。

Conclusion: 通过二值码的全局精化实现编辑定位与生成的耦合，可在无训练、无外部掩码、无注意力控制条件下取得更优的编辑质量与背景保持，为文本引导图像编辑提供了一种通用且轻量的方案。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing must introduce the requested changes while preserving unrelated source content. Diffusion-based editors rely on spatial controls whose inaccuracies can leave edits incomplete or alter unrelated regions. Causal autoregressive editors face a further constraint: their fixed decoding order limits revision of earlier decisions. We introduce RefineEdit, a training-free prompt-to-prompt image editing framework built on a Generative Refinement Network. Our key idea is to couple edit localization with content generation through the global refinement of binary image codes, allowing editing evidence to be reassessed as the image evolves. RefineEdit initializes an editing branch from an intermediate source state, reusing the emerging layout. We compare the probabilities assigned by the two branches to the same source-sampled bits, using their signed differences to select editable positions and bits. Selected bits follow editing refinement, while the remaining bits copy the evolving source state. To stabilize editing across refinement steps, adaptive spatial freezing limits unnecessary mask expansion, while finite bit locking keeps recently selected bits editable. The framework requires no additional training, external masks, or attention control. Across nine editing categories of PIE-Bench, RefineEdit achieves the best background-preservation scores in PSNR, LPIPS, MSE and SSIM, together with the highest whole-image and edited-region CLIP scores among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [92] [PROVIA: Procedure State Tracking for Online Mistake Detection in Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20638)
*Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Jimmy Weissert,Luc Maria Scherrer,Cedric Zöllner,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Jiale Wei,Junwei Zheng,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROVIA:在线错误检测方法,通过分离"事实状态"(学习到的步骤摘要)与"可接受进度"(由正确演示经贝叶斯状态合并归纳出的自动机后验),仅在校正分支允许状态转移,并用序贯检验将逐帧错误概率转为告警。在完整试次、假警报预算和纯时间基线控制下评测,在四个数据集上排名最佳,58-70 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线错误检测方法采用"首次错误协议"(first-mistake protocol),将每段录像切断于首次错误处,使得一个完全不看视频的固定时间规则在所有样本上都正确,评测因此失去意义。作者希望在被试自然犯错并恢复的完整试次上评测,并将时序本身作为控制基线。

Method: PROVIA 维护两个分离的记录:(1)事实状态,即对每个执行者所做步骤(含错误)的学习摘要;(2)可接受进度,即由正确演示经贝叶斯状态合并(bayesian state merging)归纳出的自动机状态的确切后验,以及对每个执行者执行状态的后验。过程-状态转移仅发生在"正确状态"分支上,错误分支与纠正分支保留源状态。用序贯检验(sequential test)将逐帧错误概率转为告警。

Result: 在 CaptainCook4D、IndustReal、HoloAssist、IMPACT-ego 上,以单一滤波器和单一优化规则在受控基线中错误排序表现最佳。在每 0.1 次/分钟的验证假警报预算下,CaptainCook4D 召回率 .154 对比 .128,HoloAssist .034 对比 .015,并在所有预算下领先。流水线运行速度 58-70 FPS,代码开源。

Conclusion: 在完整试次、假警报预算约束与纯时间基线控制下的在线错误检测中,显式区分"实际执行状态"与"计划正确进度"优于现有方法。局限是绝对召回率仍偏低(尤以 HoloAssist 为著),属于第一人称视频错误检测这一偏领域任务,而非具身导航或 3D 重建核心方向。

Abstract: An assistant watching egocentric video should notice a mistake from past frames alone, before the next step begins, and keep working once the person recovers. A mistake changes the state of the work, so every later step has to be read against what was done rather than against the plan. The first-mistake protocol that current online methods report on cuts each recording at its first mistake, so a fixed-time rule that never looks at the video is right on every case. We evaluate on complete trials, where mistakes and recoveries arise naturally, under a validation false-alarm budget and against controls that use timing alone. PROVIA keeps two records apart: a factual state, a learned summary of the steps each actor performed, mistakes included, and the accepted progress, an exact posterior over the state of an automaton induced from correct demonstrations by Bayesian state merging and over the execution status of each actor. Procedure-state transitions occur only in the correct-status branch; the mistake and correction branches retain the source state. A sequential test turns the per-frame mistake probability into alarms. With one filter and one optimization rule, PROVIA ranks mistakes best among the evaluated controlled baselines on CaptainCook4D, IndustReal, HoloAssist and IMPACT-ego. At a validation budget of 0.1 false alarms per minute it recalls .154 against .128 on CaptainCook4D and .034 against .015 on HoloAssist, where it leads at every budget. The pipeline runs at 58-70 frames per second. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [93] [Earth Surface Immune System for Rapid Monitoring of Unknown Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20662)
*Jingtao Li,Qian Zhu,Xinyu Wang,Deren Li,Liangpei Zhang,Yanfei Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ESIA借鉴生物免疫系统三重机制（先天非特异定位、适应性负选择开放词汇识别、突变机制测试时微调），实现地表异常检测与属性识别的统一框架，具备实时性与开放词汇能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地表异常由气候变化与人类活动驱动，频率与类型不断增加，但历史数据稀缺、难以预测，与传统遥感目标差异巨大。现有方法多针对特定异常类别，且仅停留在定位阶段，检测与可操作信息之间存在断层。

Method: 提出ESIA（Earth Surface Immune System）三段式架构：1）非特异先天免疫阶段，把异常视为时序卫星影像中的未观测变化，不假设任何异常类别，产出二值定位图，速度14.51 km²/s；2）特异适应性免疫阶段，用负选择过滤文本提示，将存活提示与定位图像块通过多模态基础模型匹配，实现未知异常属性的开放词汇识别（类别、受影响面积、损毁程度）；3）突变机制在测试时微调极少量嵌入，用单张参考影像对在3.26 s内适配每个场景。

Result: 在覆盖6类异常、19,801.60 km²的全球尺度数据集上与22个模型对比：定位F1较最强通用基线提升37%；属性识别F1超过80%；并应用于Kakhovka大坝溃决后Dnipro三角洲退化农田量化及2025年洛杉矶Palisades火灾烧伤严重度评估。

Conclusion: ESIA以生物免疫启发的三阶段设计，在无类别假设下兼顾高速度定位与开放词汇属性识别，对未知异常展现出前所未有的灵活性，为实时灾害响应与环境监测开辟新路径。

Abstract: Earth surface anomalies, driven by escalating climate change, and expanding human activities, are increasing in both frequency and diversity, yet their limited historical data and unpredictability make them fundamentally different from conventional remote sensing targets. Existing methods address specific anomaly categories or stop at localization, leaving a gap between detection and actionable information. Here we present ESIA, an Earth Surface Immune System whose architecture is constrained by three principles from the biological immune system, refined over millions of years against equally diverse and uncertain threats. A non-specific innate immune stage treats anomalies as unobserved changes in time-series satellite imagery, generating binary localization maps at 14.51 km2/s without assuming any anomaly category, surpassing the strongest general baseline by 37% in F1. A specific adaptive immune stage applies negative selection to filter text prompts and matches surviving prompts with localized image patches through a multi-modal foundation model, enabling open-vocabulary recognition of unknown anomaly attributes including category, affected area, and damage severity, with recognition F1 exceeding 80%. A mutation mechanism tunes minimal embeddings at test time, adapting to each scene in 3.26s using a single reference image pair. We validate ESIA on a global-scale dataset covering 19,801.60 km2 across six anomaly categories, comparing against 22 models, and further apply it to quantify degraded farmland in the Dnipro Delta following the Kakhovka Dam collapse and assess burn severity from 2025 Palisades Fire in Los Angeles. This unprecedented flexibility in handling unknown anomalies opens new avenues for real-time disaster response and environmental surveillance.

</details>


### [94] [FunArt: Decoding Functional Structure and Articulation from Generative 3D Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20673)
*Dennis Rotondi,Abdelrhman Werby,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FunArt 从单次静态配置下的 RGB-D 观测构建"关节感知的功能性 3D 场景图"，利用 TRELLIS.2 的冻结稀疏压缩 VAE 作为结构先验，通过轻量查询式解码器联合分割可动部件与功能交互元素，并回归运动类型、轴、原点与范围，在 Articulate3D 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人要在人类环境中作业，必须识别关节物体、分割其可动与可交互部件并估计运动学模型。现有工作存在两点不足：一类依赖交互观测才能恢复运动学，另一类在静态扫描上往往把关节结构与功能性交互元素割裂处理，无法在物理交互之前就获得可操作的结构线索。

Method: FunArt 在单帧静态配置的 posed RGB-D 输入上工作：先重建物体实例，将融合几何直接转换为 TRELLIS.2 的 O-Voxel 表示，并把其冻结的稀疏压缩 VAE 当作结构先验使用；随后由一个轻量查询式解码器将紧凑的物体级 latent 与稠密的表面对齐特征相结合，联合完成可动部件分割、功能交互元素分割，以及运动类型、轴、原点、范围的估计。

Result: 在 Articulate3D 数据集上，Movable-part 分割、关节参数估计与功能元素分割三项任务均达到 SOTA，且在是否提供 GT 物体输入的两种设定下均成立；端到端设定中，可动部件提升 1.5 AP50，联合 origin-and-axis 约束下提升 2.8 AP50，功能元素提升 6.7 AP50。

Conclusion: 结果表明生成式 3D latent 编码了可操作的结构线索，可在物理交互发生之前为机器人感知与规划提供初始化，为"静态观测即可得关节+功能结构"这一范式提供了实证支持。

Abstract: To operate effectively in human environments, robots must identify articulated objects, segment their movable and interactive parts, and estimate their kinematic models. Existing articulated scene representations typically recover kinematics from observed interactions, while methods operating on static scans often decouple articulation from functional interactive elements. We present FunArt, a framework that constructs articulation-aware functional 3D scene graphs from posed RGB-D observations captured in a single static configuration. FunArt reconstructs object instances, converts their fused geometry directly into the O-Voxel representation of TRELLIS.2, and exploits its frozen, sparse-compression VAE as a structural prior. A lightweight query-based decoder combines compact object-level latents with dense, surface-aligned features to jointly segment movable parts and functional interactive elements while estimating motion type, axis, origin, and range. On the Articulate3D dataset, FunArt achieves state-of-the-art performance across movable-part segmentation, articulation estimation, and functional-element segmentation, both with and without ground-truth object input. In the end-to-end setting, it outperforms the strongest baselines by 1.5 AP_{50} points for movable parts, 2.8 AP_{50} points under joint origin-and-axis constraints, and 6.7 AP_{50} points for functional elements. These results demonstrate that generative 3D latents encode actionable structural cues that can initialize robotic perception and planning before physical interaction.

</details>


### [95] [Should This Case Be Adapted? Prediction Fragmentation Controls Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20700)
*Lili Wang,Jing Li,Xiaowen Sun,Xiangyu Hu,Zhuangzhuang Gu,Jian Liu,Srihari Nelakuditi,Yan Tong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"有害接受面积"(HA)指标量化逐例测试时自适应的危害，发现预测碎片化几何可无标签预测HA，据此构建案例级路由器，在多个医学分割基准上大幅降低危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐例测试时自适应（episodic TTA）在每个case上重置为源权重并固定步数适配，但固定预算混淆了"该适配多远"（群体级）与"该case是否应适配"（个体级）。群体均值会掩盖个体危害：跨厂商心脏MRI上ΔDice均值与零无显著差异，但58.7%的case被单独恶化。作者希望显式量化并控制这种逐例危害。

Method: 定义"有害接受面积"(HA)，即控制器部署的编辑区域中有害的比例。先检验held-out调参（全局预算）的迁移性；随后提出"预测碎片化"（M0与适配掩码Mk之间的分歧几何）作为HA的无标签代理信号，无需额外反向传播。基于该信号构建case级路由器，阈值在独立标注划分上拟合一次，决策时不用标签或梯度，跨架构（nnU-Net→SegFormer）与跨域（Cityscapes→ACDC）按域实例化坐标、阈值与分桶动作。

Result: 预测碎片化在三个基准上与HA相关（Spearman ρ 0.50–0.60），延迟仅为梯度范数的四分之一。在未参与设计的基准上，路由器将HA从0.228降至0.139（仅重校准切点）。在心脏基准上，HA从0.129降至0.013（Dice匹配、1.10次部署更新），优于事后在评测标签上找到的retrospective-best预算，并将58.7%降至20.0%（量化上界）。在前列腺等"保留case非净获益"场景，路由器仍降低HA但牺牲精度，作为边界如实报告。

Conclusion: 固定horizon掩盖了逐例适配决策；预测碎片化几何可作为无标签、低延迟的HA预测器，支撑可迁移的case级路由，在多个医学基准上显著减少有害编辑，同时明确报告其在非净获益域上的精度代价边界。

Abstract: Episodic test-time adaptation resets a frozen segmenter to source weights $M_0$ on each case and adapts for a fixed step count. A fixed horizon conflates a cohort-level question, how far to adapt, with an irreducibly per-case one, whether this case should be adapted at all. Cohort means hide that decision: on cross-vendor cardiac MRI the mean $\Delta$Dice from adaptation is statistically indistinguishable from zero while 58.7% of cases are individually made worse. We quantify this harm as harmful accepted area (HA), the harmful fraction of the edited area a controller deploys. Held-out tuning gives a stronger baseline than a fixed horizon, but the budget it selects transfers on neither of the two main medical benchmarks, and no global budget can condition on the case. We show that prediction fragmentation---the disagreement geometry between $M_0$ and the adapted mask $M_k$---predicts HA with no labels or extra backward passes at decision time, comparably on three benchmarks (Spearman $\rho$ 0.50--0.60), at a quarter of gradient-norm's latency. A case-level router built on it cuts HA from 0.228 to 0.139 on a benchmark that took no part in its design, with the design frozen and only cut-points recalibrated there. On the cardiac benchmark the design was selected on, the router cuts HA from 0.129 to 0.013 at matched Dice and 1.10 deployed updates, against the retrospective-best budget found post hoc on evaluation labels, and reduces that 58.7% to 20.0%, an upper bound we quantify. Where the retained cases are not net-helped (as on prostate), the router still cuts HA but concedes accuracy, a boundary we report. Thresholds are fit once on a labeled split disjoint from evaluation; decisions use no labels or gradients. The template ports across architecture and domain (nnU-Net$\to$SegFormer, Cityscapes$\to$ACDC) with coordinate, thresholds and per-bucket actions instantiated per domain.

</details>


### [96] [FlowSGS: Improving Flow Matching Priors for Inverse Imaging with Stochastic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20769)
*Tianao Li,Xinhui Qian,Emma Alexander*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowSGS 用拆分吉布斯采样将逆问题后验拆成似然步与先验步，似然步用 Langevin 动力学，先验步用随机插值的反向时间 SDE 接入预训练流模型，并通过直线概率路径与时间步校正减少网络评估次数，在线性与非线性（傅里叶相位恢复）逆问题上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的即插即用逆问题求解器普遍假设线性前向模型或在后验采样中引入简化近似，限制了其在更一般（如非线性）逆问题中的适用性与采样精度。

Method: 提出 FlowSGS：以 Split Gibbs Sampling 将后验分解为似然步与先验步；似然步采用 Langevin 动力学采样；先验步基于 Stochastic Interpolants 的反向时间 SDE 形式，将预训练流模型作为 PnP 先验融入，并给出与既有 PnP 方法的联系；利用流先验的直线概率路径和一种新的反向 SDE 时间步校正技术降低先验步的网络评估次数。

Result: 在一系列逆问题上达到 SOTA 性能；先验步所需网络评估次数少于基于扩散的即插即用采样器；首次给出面向流式逆问题求解器的非线性逆问题（傅里叶相位恢复）实验。

Conclusion: FlowSGS 通过在 SGS 框架中用 SI 反向时间 SDE 构建先验步，去除了线性前向模型假设与采样近似，兼顾采样质量与效率，并将基于流的逆问题求解扩展到非线性场景。

Abstract: Flow matching has emerged as the state-of-the-art generative model and has been used for plug-and-play (PnP) priors to solve inverse problems in computational imaging. However, existing flow-based inverse solvers assume linear forward models and/or make simplifying approximations in posterior sampling. To circumvent these problems, we introduce FlowSGS, a flow-based posterior sampling method using Split Gibbs Sampling (SGS) to decompose the posterior into a likelihood step and a prior step. Specifically, we sample from the likelihood step using Langevin dynamics and leverage the Stochastic Interpolants (SI) framework to integrate a pretrained flow model into the prior step. We provide a form for the prior step that uses SI's reverse-time SDE, and show connections to previous PnP methods. Moreover, with the aid of the flow prior's straight probability paths and a novel timestep correction technique for the reverse-time SDE, FlowSGS requires fewer network evaluations in its prior step than plug-and-play diffusion samplers. Our experiments show state-of-the-art performance on a range of inverse problems. For the first time, we provide an experiment on a nonlinear inverse problem (Fourier phase retrieval) for flow-based inverse solvers.

</details>


### [97] [ERCPMP-Gx: Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Morphological, Histopathological, and Genomic Characterization of Colorectal Polyposis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20815)
*Zahra Ghaffari,Massih Bahar,Mojgan Forootan,Ali Darvishi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发布ERCPMP-Gx数据集，将结直肠息肉病的内镜、组织病理与胚系基因组数据在患者层面关联，用于支持AI识别与分类遗传性息肉病综合征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共内镜数据集多围绕散发性单发息肉组织，缺乏将息肉病表型与组织病理及胚系检测结果在患者层面关联的资源，限制AI在遗传性息肉病综合征识别与鉴别分类中的应用。

Method: 使用Olympus EVIS X1系统采集，涵盖WLE、NBI、放大NBI及近聚焦NBI模式，产出160张图像及配套视频；约80%为临床和/或基因确诊的遗传性息肉病综合征（FAP、PJS、JPS、GNS），20%为非遗传性息肉及形态重叠的类息肉病变以支持鉴别；每条记录尽可能关联标准化内镜标注、代表性组织病理与临床胚系结果。

Result: 构建了患者级、AI就绪的标注框架，数据集在Mendeley公开，更新信息见DataBioX网站。

Conclusion: 该数据集为遗传性结直肠息肉病的AI识别、表征与分类提供多模态、患者层面的资源基础，但样本规模有限，尚属数据资源而非方法创新。

Abstract: Hereditary polyposis syndromes can be precursor lesions to colorectal cancer and are associated with a broad spectrum of extracolonic tumors. Early identification and accurate classification of these syndromes are essential for timely diagnosis, individualized patient management, and targeted surveillance strategies for affected families. However, public endoscopic datasets are largely organized around the individual sporadic polyp, and none links the polyposis phenotype to histopathology and germline findings at the patient level. Here, we present ERCPMP-Gx, an endoscopic, histopathological, and genomic dataset developed to support the application of artificial intelligence (AI) in the recognition, characterization, and classification of colorectal polyposis. Most procedures were performed using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE), narrow-band imaging (NBI), magnifying NBI (M-NBI), and NBI with near focus modes, yielding 160 images and accompanying video clips. Approximately eighty percent of cases represent clinically and/or genetically confirmed hereditary polyposis syndromes (PG), including familial adenomatous polyposis (FAP), Peutz-Jeghers syndrome (PJS), juvenile polyposis syndrome (JPS), and ganglioneuroma syndrome (GNS), while the remaining twenty percent comprise non-hereditary polyps and polyp-mimicking lesions with overlapping morphological features (Non-PG), included to support differential classification. Each released record is linked, where available, to standardized endoscopic annotations, representative histopathology, and clinically reported germline findings, forming an AI-ready, patient-level annotation framework. The dataset is publicly accessible at Mendeley ( this https URL ). For the latest updates and further information, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: this https URL .

</details>


### [98] [Paint-Anything: Unified Any-Color Control for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20816)
*Ji Xie,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Zhennan Chen,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Paint-Anything 提出共享的十六进制颜色提示接口，通过对象级颜色监督实现文生图与编辑的任意色控制，并构建 Paint-500K 数据管线与 ACBench 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业设计需要"任意色控制"，即用任意 24 位十六进制值精确指定目标颜色。已有颜色生成/编辑/上色方法多依赖专用颜色表示或特殊推理流程；而大模型虽天然能关联 hex 值与颜色语义，却缺乏面向生成与编辑统一的精确颜色控制方案。

Method: 学习一个共享的 hex-prompt 接口，统一用于生成与编辑，并施加对象级颜色监督。数据侧通过目标 grounding、感知颜色标注与编辑对合成，从真实图像构建 Paint-500K；由于阴影使真实图像标签仅为近似色，补充"纯色锚点"使像素与配对 hex 值精确一致，且仅在低噪声早期（高噪声时间步）使用，低噪声阶段仍用自然图像训练。同时提出 ACBench（含 T2I 与 Edit 两个子集）度量对象级 hex 颜色保真度。

Result: 在 FLUX.2-4B 上，Paint-Anything 相对基模型将 ACBench-T2I 与 ACBench-Edit 分数分别提升 85.3% 与 28.3%，消融实验支持该训练配方；并在对比方法中取得最高平均 CompColor 分数。

Conclusion: 通过共享 hex 提示接口、纯色锚点与自然图像混合的时序化监督，以及配套基准，可在通用生成模型上实现高保真的任意色对象级控制，方法简洁且有效。

Abstract: Professional design requires any-color control: the ability to specify an object's target color with any 24-bit hex value for image generation and editing. Prior work has explored color generation, editing, and colorization, but often relies on dedicated color representations or specialized inference procedures. Advances in large language models offer a simpler starting point: even compact models can associate hex values with color semantics. We present Paint-Anything, which learns a shared hex-prompt interface for generation and editing through object-level color supervision. We develop a data pipeline that constructs Paint-500K from real images through object grounding, perceptual color labeling, and editing-pair synthesis. Since shadows make real-image labels only approximate colors, we complement this supervision with pure-color anchors whose pixels exactly match their paired hex values. These anchors are used only at high-noise timesteps, leaving low-noise training to natural images. We further introduce Any Color Benchmark (ACBench), comprising ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit, to measure object-level hex color fidelity across both tasks. On FLUX.2-4B, Paint-Anything improves ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit scores by 85.3% and 28.3%, respectively, relative to the base model, with ablations supporting the training recipe. It also achieves the highest average CompColor score among the compared methods.

</details>


### [99] [FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20817)
*Kevin Qu,Tao Sun,Massimiliano Viola,Liyuan Zhu,Zhizhuo Zhou,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Konrad Schindler,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAMOS：从稀疏无序部分点云集合前馈预测铰接物体可动部件分割与关节参数，支持可变输入数量，通过多状态铰接Transformer与观测铰接跨度目标提升性能，并用程序化数据生成器扩充训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目稀疏视图下每个观测仅含部分几何与运动信息，现有前馈方法多依赖单次观测和类别级形状先验，泛化与数据受限。

Method: 提出FAMOS前馈模型，输入稀疏无序的部分点云集合；设计Multi-state Articulation Transformer，交替进行状态内注意与全局注意以聚合多观测铰接线索；提出observed articulation span目标，监督各部件在输入观测中体现的运动范围；引入程序化数据生成器，训练时合成自带标注的资产。

Result: 在PartNet-Mobility、ACD与ArtiCraft-10K上相较前馈与优化类基线取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 多观测联合推理与运动跨度监督可有效利用输入集合，程序化数据生成缓解数据规模与多样性不足。

Abstract: Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. Our model jointly reasons over multiple observations and naturally supports a variable number of inputs, including a single view. To aggregate articulation cues across observations, we introduce a Multi-state Articulation Transformer with alternating state-wise and global attention. We further propose an observed articulation span objective that supervises the motion range each part exhibits across the input observations, encouraging the model to leverage the full observation set. To overcome the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce a procedural data generator that synthesizes self-annotated assets during training. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility, ACD, and ArtiCraft-10K demonstrate consistent improvements over both feed-forward and optimization-based baselines. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [100] [SplashSplat: Reconstructing Splashing Liquids from Real-World Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20818)
*Peiyu Liu,Dingxi Zhang,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Christina Tsalicoglou,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个多视角同步飞溅液体数据集（20个真实场景，7台4K/60fps相机），并给出SplashSplat方法：用掩码融合逐帧液体SDF做几何，通过level-set输运得到粗速度场，再用拉格朗日载体随流平流、与新观测校正并在覆盖丢失处重播种，解码局部高斯进行可微渲染；在真实与合成基准上优于现有动态高斯泼溅方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞溅液体持续时间极短（片状破裂成韧带与液滴）、外观依赖视角且几乎无纹理、难以追踪，因此现有重建研究多聚焦烟雾、合成液体或缓慢变形表面；据作者所知尚不存在同步多视角的飞溅液体数据集，缺少评测基准阻碍了该方向的发展。

Method: 核心原则是"仅在观测能约束处施加物理结构"：(1) 逐帧液体SDF由各视角掩码融合得到几何；(2) 相邻SDF间的level-set输运给出粗速度场；(3) 拉格朗日载体沿该流场平流，每次新观测后进行校正，在覆盖丢失处重播种，解码为局部高斯以支持可微渲染。

Result: 在自采真实数据和合成基准上均超越最先进的动态高斯泼溅方法，运动物理更合理且训练开销更低；同一表示无需重新优化即可支持时间插值与风格迁移。

Conclusion: 同步多视角飞溅液体数据集为动态液体重建提供了新的真实评测基准；将物理结构约束限制在可观测范围内、结合拉格朗日载体与局部高斯的混合表示，是重建快速瞬态、无纹理、以薄片/液滴为主液体的有效方案。

Abstract: A splash lives for a fraction of a second: sheets tear into ligaments and droplets, appearance is view-dependent and nearly textureless, and little persists long enough to track. Reconstruction research has consequently focused on smoke, synthetic liquids, or gently deforming surfaces. To our knowledge, no synchronized multi-view dataset of splashing liquids exists. We therefore introduce a benchmark of 20 real scenes, from coherent streams to violent splashes, captured by seven synchronized, calibrated 4K cameras at 60 fps, with manually refined per-view liquid and container masks and fixed evaluation splits. We further present SplashSplat, built on a single principle: impose physical structure only where the observations can constrain it. Per-frame liquid SDFs fused from the masks provide the geometry, level-set transport between consecutive SDFs yields a coarse velocity field, and Lagrangian carriers advected along this flow, corrected against each new observation and reseeded where coverage is lost, decode local Gaussians for differentiable rendering. SplashSplat outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic Gaussian splatting methods on our real captures and on a synthetic benchmark, with physically more plausible motion and a lower training cost. The same representation supports temporal interpolation and style transfer without re-optimization.

</details>


### [101] [Can 4D Foundation Models Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20819)
*Guangzhao He,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Wei-Chiu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 PersistBench，利用 360° 全景视频作为"全知"真值，从物体恒存性、运动连续性、外观保持三个方面评测 4D 基础模型（可控视频生成与 4D 重建）的视觉记忆能力，发现现有模型仅在物体处于视野内时保持短期一致，一旦离开视野便显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 基础模型（可控相机视频模型、4D 重建模型）已具备感知与重建动态环境的能力，但其"记忆"所感知内容的能力尚不明确。现有基准依赖像素级指标，且对离开视野的物体缺乏真值，无法以物体为中心对照参考评测视觉记忆，因此存在评测空白。

Method: 构建 PersistBench 数据集与指标套件：以 360° 视频作为全知（omniscient）真值来源，从而为离开视野的物体提供参考；设计三个评测维度——物体恒存性（object permanence）、运动连续性（motion continuity）、外观保持（appearance preservation）；在多种类别模型上进行横向评测。

Result: 跨多类模型的评测显示，当前 4D 基础模型仅能维持短期一致性，物体一旦离开视野，其一致性显著下降，暴露出与稳健视觉记忆之间的明显差距。

Conclusion: 论文以"看见不等于记住"（seeing is not remembering）概括当前 4D 基础模型的能力缺口，为未来视觉记忆导向的 4D 基础模型发展提供评测基准与方向指引。

Abstract: Perceiving and remembering the visual world is fundamental to navigating and interacting with our environment. Current 4D foundation models, such as camera-controllable video models or 4D reconstruction models, can perceive and reconstruct dynamic environments, but how well they remember what they have perceived remains an open question. Existing benchmarks largely rely on pixel-level metrics and lack ground truth for objects once they leave the field of view, making them unable to evaluate visual memory in an object-centric manner against references. To fill this gap, we introduce PersistBench, a dataset and metric suite that leverages 360° videos as omniscient ground truth and proposes three evaluation aspects: object permanence, motion continuity, and appearance preservation. Evaluating various models across diverse categories reveals that current models can only maintain short-term consistency that degrades significantly once objects leave the field of view. Our findings highlight the gap between current model capabilities and robust visual memory ("seeing is not remembering"), providing guidance for future development of 4D foundation models. Dataset and code are available on the project page: this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [AthenaZero: A low-inertia, bimanual robot for dynamic manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19194)
*Andrew S. Morgan,Gregory Xie,Capprin Bass,Rachel Thomasson,Chunpeng Wang,Erfan Shahriari,Harrison Busa,Oluwaseun Araromi,Joseph Aronov,Michael Burgess,Velin D. Dimitrov,Matthew A. Estrada,Faris Hamdi,Samuel Kendig,Taeyoon Lee,Jose Oscar Mur-Miranda,Emma Sommers,Paul Titchener,Margaret Wang,Achu Wilson,Mark Yeatman,Osman Dogan Yirmibesoglu,Alfred A. Rizzi,Annan Mozeika,Nicolas Rojas,Lael Odhner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AthenaZero是一款双臂操作机器人，通过准直驱驱动与传动远置技术将末端等效质量降至人类水平（约为传统机械臂的十分之一），并在棒球式投掷、接球、击球三类高动态任务中验证了毫秒级时间尺度下的操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械臂惯量大、力矩透明度差，难以在毫秒级时间尺度上完成投掷、接球、击球等强动态交互任务。作者希望在不牺牲控制权限的前提下最小化惯量，使机器人具备接近人类的动态操作能力。

Method: 采用准直驱（quasi-direct drive）执行器与传动远置（transmission remotization）方案进行双臂机构设计，降低远端等效惯量并提升力矩透明性；文中给出了该设计的方法论，并在投球、接球、击球三类棒球启发任务上开展系统验证。

Result: 末端等效质量与人类相当，约为常规机械臂的十分之一。投掷速度超过30 m/s；在7.3 m短距离内接球与击球速度超过14 m/s；并完成机器人对机器人、人类对机器人的接抛球练习与对抗性击球。

Conclusion: 低惯量、高力矩透明度的准直驱双臂平台能够支撑人类时间尺度上的高加速动态操作，在机器人与人类交互的高动态任务中展现出有效性与适应性。

Abstract: AthenaZero is a bimanual manipulator designed to minimize inertia without compromising control authority. By utilizing quasi-direct drive actuation and transmission remotization techniques, the system achieves an effective endpoint mass comparable to that of a human---about an order of magnitude less than conventional robot manipulators. This characteristic, combined with its inherent torque transparency, makes AthenaZero exceptionally well-suited for dynamic manipulation. We describe the methodology} that led to this design and demonstrate the robot's capabilities on three baseball-inspired tasks: throwing, catching, and batting, which showcase complex interactions on human-comparable timescales where milliseconds matter. AthenaZero was capable of throwing at speeds in excess of 30 m/s, with catching and batting at speeds in excess of 14 m/s over a short 7.3 m distance. Batting practice and a game of catch were subsequently performed in robot-to-robot and human-to-robot variations, showcasing the efficacy and adaptability of our system in tasks that require high acceleration.

</details>


### [103] [DITTO: Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19196)
