### [171] [AnyViewDex: View-Invariant Dexterous Manipulation from RGB Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20107)
*Soham Patil,Om Sanjay Gunjal,Sourabh Bhosale,Arhan Chavare,Ramandeep Singh Hora,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyViewDex 通过训练期的多视角对比对齐与特权 3D 几何监督（回归物体绝对 3D 坐标），使多指灵巧操作策略在部署时仅用未标定单目 RGB 与本体感知即可实现视角不变控制，硬件上在 xArm7+16-DoF LEAP Hand 达到 76.7% 抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧操作的视觉运动策略对相机视角变化高度敏感。已有实现视角不变性的方法依赖显式 3D 模态（RGB-D、点云），带来硬件依赖、标定要求和真实部署中的传感器噪声脆弱性。作者希望在不使用测试期显式 3D 传感的前提下获得视角不变控制。

Method: 提出 AnyViewDex，一种非对称训练流水线：将几何知识编码进仿真中的视觉表征。方法结合多视角对比对齐与特权 3D 几何监督，在仿真训练中通过回归物体绝对 3D 坐标提供几何锚定信号，缓解全局池化对比嵌入的空间坍缩；部署时策略以零样本方式仅使用未标定单目 RGB 和本体感知。在强化学习与学生-教师蒸馏两种范式下验证。

Result: 在 xArm7 + 16-DoF LEAP Hand 硬件评测中，AnyViewDex 在八种未见物体、六个未标定视角下取得 76.7% 抓取成功率（480 次试验；全部消融条件共 2,400 次），表明几何锚定的单目策略可在无测试期深度的情况下零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 无需测试期显式 3D 传感即可实现视角不变的灵巧操作控制；通过训练期特权几何监督（绝对 3D 坐标回归）结合多视角对比对齐，可有效避免对比嵌入的空间坍缩，并使单目策略零样本跨视角迁移。为降低真实部署的硬件与标定成本提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies for multi-fingered dexterous manipulation are highly sensitive to camera viewpoint shifts. To achieve view invariance, recent methods increasingly rely on explicit 3D modalities like RGB-D or point clouds, which can introduce hardware dependencies, calibration requirements, and vulnerability to sensor noise during real-world deployment. In this work, we show that view-invariant control can be achieved without explicit test-time 3D sensing by encoding geometric knowledge into the visual representation during simulation. We present AnyViewDex, an asymmetric training pipeline that combines multi-view contrastive alignment with privileged 3D geometric supervision. By regressing absolute 3D object coordinates during simulated training, this auxiliary objective provides a geometric grounding signal that mitigates the spatial collapse of the globally pooled contrastive embedding. At deployment, the policy operates zero-shot using only uncalibrated monocular RGB and proprioception. We validate this approach across both reinforcement learning and student-teacher distillation. In hardware evaluation on an xArm7 with a 16-DoF LEAP Hand, AnyViewDex reaches 76.7% grasping success across eight unseen objects and six uncalibrated viewpoints (480 trials; 2,400 across all ablation conditions), indicating that geometrically grounded monocular policies transfer zero-shot without test-time depth. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [172] [Universal Navigation Interface: Robot-Free Data for Wheeled Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20114)
*Sarvesh Prajapati,Ananya Trivedi,Lorena Maria Genua,Drake Moore,Bruce Maxwell,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 UNI（通用导航接口）：用四轮助行车+智能手机采集物理受约束的人类演示，无需目标机器人即可获得真实世界导航数据，采集37.2km轨迹并用于监督目标条件导航模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界导航数据采集通常依赖平台特定遥操作，成本高、难扩展；需要一种与目标机器人无关、低成本的采集范式。

Method: 以四轮助行车为"物理代理"平台，因无法爬楼、跨越未切坡道或通过窄缝，演示天然偏向轮式可行路径；配合智能手机恢复度量轨迹，直接监督目标条件导航模型，并微调视觉导航模型。

Result: 在留出的UNI演示上轨迹预测误差降低17.4–24.8%；在其他导航数据集上的收益随数据集与指标变化；并实现到电动轮椅的闭环迁移（路缘、楼梯、路缘切口场景）。

Conclusion: 低成本物理代理可作为无需目标机器人即可采集导航监督的实用来源。

Abstract: Collecting real-world navigation data for mobile robots typically requires platform-specific teleoperation, making large-scale collection expensive and difficult to scale. We introduce Universal Navigation Interface (UNI), a robot-free data collection paradigm that uses a four-wheeled rollator walker (rollator) and smartphone to collect physically constrained human demonstrations. Because the rollator cannot climb stairs, negotiate uncut curbs, or pass through narrow gaps, demonstrations are naturally biased toward wheeled-feasible routes. Using UNI, we collect 37.2 km of real-world navigation data and recover metric trajectories that directly supervise goal-conditioned navigation models. Fine-tuning visual-navigation models on UNI reduces trajectory prediction error by 17.4-24.8% on held-out UNI demonstrations. Evaluation on other navigation datasets shows benefits that vary by dataset and metric. We further demonstrate closed-loop transfer to a powered wheelchair in curb, staircase, and curb-cut scenarios. These results support low-cost physical proxies as a practical source of navigation supervision collected without the target robot.

</details>


### [173] [GPT-6-Astra in a Navigation Workflow: Behavioral Analysis in Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20116)
*Guangzhao Dai,Qi Wu,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对 GPT-6-Astra 在零样本 VLN-CE 系统上的评测：无微调、无 agent harness，仅靠直接 API 调用完成"观测—决策—执行"闭环。在 R2R-CE val-unseen 的 50 个 episode 上取得 SR 52.0%、SPL 48.9%、nDTW 70.8%，并揭示"局部判断正确但难以转化为持续进展与恰当停止"这一核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验通用大模型在无需导航微调、无专用封装框架（agent harness）的零样本条件下，能否胜任连续环境视觉语言导航；同时评估应覆盖完整系统（含上下文管理与动作控制），而非只测单步决策。

Method: 构建 GPT-6-Astra 零样本 VLN-CE 系统，采用通用的"观测—决策—执行"工作流，通过直接模型 API 调用实现；每个请求接收精选观测、执行反馈与保留的进度记录；以 50 个 R2R-CE val-unseen episode（取自 Open-Nav 使用的 100 个）进行完整系统评测。

Result: SR 52.0%、SPL 48.9%、nDTW 70.8%；终止时 36.0% 的 episode 以工作流接受的 STOP 成功，另有 16.0% 在步数上限处满足距离判据。分析发现三点：模型能借助观测与历史把地标、先前动作关联到指令；会主动请求额外视角并修正不确定判断；存在"任务理解"与"自主完成"之间的落差（如识别到未完成穿越却持续旋转）。

Conclusion: 零样本通用模型在 VLN-CE 中展现出可用的局部推理与自我修正能力，但核心瓶颈在于把正确的局部判断转化为持续的进度推进与恰当终止；上下文管理与动作控制是完整系统性能的关键组成部分。

Abstract: We study GPT-6-Astra in a zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) system, where it interprets instructions, assesses its surroundings, and proposes actions. The system uses a common observation--decision--execution workflow with direct model API calls, without a packaged agent harness or navigation-specific fine-tuning. In this workflow, each request receives selected observations, execution feedback, and retained progress records. Evaluation covers the complete system, including context management and action control. We evaluate the system on 50 of the 100 R2R-CE val-unseen episodes used by Open-Nav. It achieves a success rate of 52.0\%, an SPL of 48.9\%, and an nDTW of 70.8\%. Our analysis highlights three findings. First, recorded responses link landmarks and earlier actions to instructions using observations and supplied history. Second, reviews include requests for additional views and revisions of uncertain judgments. Third, the results suggest a gap between task understanding and autonomous completion: an unfinished crossing is recognized while rotation continues. At termination, 36.0\% of episodes succeed with a workflow-accepted STOP, while another 16.0\% meet the distance criterion at the step limit. These results highlight a central challenge: translating correct local judgments into sustained progress and appropriate stopping.

</details>


### [174] [VLN on the Fly: An Onboard Vision-Language Navigation Stack for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20191)
*Marco S. Tayar,Felipe Tommaselli,Gianluca Capezutto,Pedro Antonio Rabelo Saraiva,Pedro H. V. de Freitas,Lucas Kido,Guilherme Sonego,Ricardo V. Godoy,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLN on the Fly：一种完全机载的模块化空中视觉语言导航栈，将指令接地、规划与控制保持为可检查的独立阶段，在15次实飞中13次成功、平均目标误差5.72 cm、GPU占用39.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端空中策略把接地、规划、控制融合进单一网络，牺牲了模块化栈的可观测性与安全检查能力；同时在机载算力受限条件下难以定位单点错误。作者希望保留模块化可解释性的同时实现机载实时运行。

Method: 四阶段级联：量化VLM将语言指令接地到粗略图像单元格 → 深度信息将其提升为3D目标点 → 快速B样条规划器生成可行轨迹 → 预训练强化学习策略跟踪轨迹并输出电机指令，可跨四旋翼平台迁移；各阶段独立可检查。

Result: 室内受控空间、3类日常参照物的15次机载飞行中13次到达目标，平均目标误差5.72 cm，平均GPU利用率39.3%；另在6次杂乱环境试验中，在机载感知门控下跟踪无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: 模块化、可检查的机载栈能在受限算力下实现真实空中视觉语言导航，兼顾实时性、精度与安全可观测性，为空中具身导航提供可部署范式。

Abstract: Running vision-language navigation fully onboard an aerial robot is hard, since grounding, planning, and control must share limited compute and a single-stage error is difficult to isolate in flight. End-to-end aerial policies fuse these stages into one network, giving up the observability and safety checks a modular stack keeps available. We propose VLN on the Fly, an onboard stack that keeps grounding, planning, and control as separate, inspectable stages. A quantized VLM grounds an instruction to a coarse image cell, depth lifts it to a 3D goal, a fast B-spline planner returns a feasible trajectory, and a pretrained reinforcement learning policy tracks it to motor commands across quadrotors. Across 15 onboard flights over three everyday referents in a controlled indoor volume, the stack reaches the target in 13 of 15 trials with 5.72 cm mean goal error and 39.3% average GPU utilization. In 6 additional cluttered-environment trials, the stack tracks collision-free trajectories under onboard perception gating.

</details>


### [175] [Strategic Transformer for Resource-Constrained Multi-Object Navigation in Ultra-Large-Scale Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20227)
*Daiki Iwata,Kanji Tanaka,Senta Hishida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将资源受限的多目标室内导航重构为集合定向问题(SOP)，提出轻量Strategic Transformer作优先级规划器，配合有界2-opt局部优化，在ProcTHOR上以41.03ms推理、94倍加速超越重元启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: >2000㎡大型室内环境中的资源受限多目标导航，需在效率与策略规划间平衡。传统方法要么规划僵硬，要么行为短视；而SOP为NP-hard，实时求解困难，因此需要可预测、低延迟的规划方案。

Method: 把任务形式化为SOP：利用由SOP模型驱动、独立启发式切换器管理探索。学生网络Strategic Transformer将专家组合逻辑内化为单次前向(41.03ms)的优先级规划，引入几何注意力偏置建模长程结构依赖，降低师生信息不对称；再在受限候选图上做有界迭代2-opt局部精修，形成宏观规划+局部优化的耦合框架。

Result: 相较重元启发式实现94倍加速，推理延迟有界，适合机载部署；在ProcTHOR上以PPL(按路径长度加权的进度)指标超过精心重实现的基线，成为可扩展资源受限导航的新基准。

Conclusion: 该工作以SOP建模+轻量Transformer规划+有界局部搜索，有效弥合探索与利用的鸿沟，为大规模、资源受限的具身导航提供了可实时部署的可行方案。

Abstract: Resource-constrained multi-object navigation in vast indoor environments ($>2,000\text{ m}^2$) poses significant challenges for efficiency and strategic planning. To tackle this, we reformulate the task as a Set Orienteering Problem (SOP), providing an optimization framework under resource constraints where exploitation is governed by the SOP model and exploration is managed by a separate heuristic switcher. Conventional baselines suffer from either rigid planning or myopic behaviors. To overcome these limitations and resolve the NP-hard computational challenges of SOP for real-time navigation, we develop the Strategic Transformer. This lightweight architecture functions as a priority planner that internalizes expert combinatorial logic into a predictable $41.03\text{ ms}$ forward pass while reducing teacher-student information asymmetry. Incorporating geometric attention biases allows the network to effectively model long-range structural dependencies. By coupling the Transformer's macro-plan with a bounded iterative 2-opt local refinement on a capped candidate graph, our framework achieves a $94\times$ speedup compared to heavy meta-heuristics, ensuring bounded-latency inference suitable for onboard deployment. Experiments on ProcTHOR validate that our method successfully bridges the gap between exploration and exploitation, outperforming carefully re-implemented baselines under Progress weighted by Path Length (PPL) and establishing a new benchmark for scalable, resource-constrained navigation.

</details>


### [176] [LLM-Guided Transformation of Non-Critical Driving Scenes into Safety-Critical Scenarios Using Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20318)
*Noura Fady,Farah Khaled,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出融合计算机视觉、LLM与AR的自动化流程，将安全驾驶场景转化为安全关键场景，用于ADS测试，在nuScenes上达到97.52%安全性分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统测试需要安全关键场景，但真实道路采集此类数据成本高且不安全，因此需要自动生成逼真且危险的可控场景。

Method: 检测跟踪道路参与者，提取距离、速度、运动方向、TTC等安全特征并评估场景关键性；对安全场景用LLM生成逼真、引发碰撞的对象与行为，并通过AR叠加融合进原始场景。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上安全性分类准确率达97.52%，成功生成行人横穿、后车超车、急停等逼真安全关键场景。

Conclusion: 该流程为自动驾驶系统的测试与验证提供了一种有效、灵活的自动化安全关键场景生成方法。

Abstract: Testing Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) requires realistic safety-critical scenarios, but collecting such data from real-world driving is costly and unsafe. This paper presents an automated pipeline that transforms safe driving scenes into safety-critical scenarios by combining computer vision, Large Language Models (LLMs), and Augmented Reality (AR). The system detects and tracks road users, extracts safety features including distance, velocity, motion direction, and Time-to-Collision (TTC), and assesses scene criticality. Safe scenes are modified by an LLM, which generates realistic collision-inducing objects and behaviors that are integrated into the original scene using AR. The proposed pipeline was evaluated on the nuScenes dataset, achieving 97.52% safety classification accuracy and successfully generating realistic scenarios such as pedestrian crossings, rear overtaking vehicles, and sudden-stop events. The results demonstrate an effective and flexible approach for automated generation of safety-critical scenarios to support the testing and validation of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [177] [Implementation of Tightly-Coupled SLAM Fusion of GPS, IMU, and LiDAR for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20321)
*Amr O. Elmehrath,Farah Khaled,Rana Nahas,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 实现 Velodyne 3D LiDAR、IMU 与 GPS 紧耦合融合 SLAM 系统，基于 FAST-LIO2 的 IESKF 前端与 GTSAM 位姿图后端消除漂移，生成高精度稠密点云与 HD 地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一传感器在自动驾驶中均存在失效场景：LiDAR 在无特征长廊退化，IMU 存在累积漂移，GPS 在城市峡谷中频繁失锁。因此需要多模态紧耦合融合以维持连续空间感知。

Method: 采用分阶段开发：先建立 2D 基线验证硬件时间同步与坐标变换几何，再升级为基于 FAST-LIO2 的 3D 架构。前端使用迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波（IESKF）融合稠密 3D 激光点与高频惯性数据，消除高速运动模糊；后端采用 GTSAM 位姿图优化执行多模态回环闭合以消除长期漂移。

Result: 在仿真环境与实机部署中评估，紧耦合 3D 融合能有效克服各单传感器盲区，生成细节丰富的高精度点云，为下游自主规划器提供所需 HD 地图基础。

Conclusion: 紧耦合多模态 SLAM 融合可解决单传感器失效问题，是自动驾驶连续定位与高精建图的有效方案，并为下游规划提供地图支撑。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend entirely on Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) to navigate safely in unknown environments. However, relying on a single sensory modality introduces critical failure points: LiDAR systems degrade in featureless corridors, Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) accumulate mathematical drift, and GPS drops frequently in urban canyons. This paper presents the implementation of a tightly-coupled SLAM fusion architecture that integrates a Velodyne 3D LiDAR, a high-frequency IMU, and GPS to achieve continuous spatial awareness. Utilizing a phased development methodology, we establish a 2D baseline to validate hardware synchronization and transform geometries before upgrading to a full 3D architecture driven by FAST-LIO2. This advanced approach uses an Iterated Error-State Kalman Filter (IESKF) to process dense 3D laser points alongside continuous inertial data, eliminating motion blur at high speeds. To eradicate long-term drift, a GTSAM pose-graph optimization back-end executes multi-modal loop closures. Evaluated across simulated environments and physical deployments, the results demonstrate that tightly-coupled 3D fusion effectively overcomes individual sensor blind spots to generate highly detailed point clouds, providing the foundational High-Definition (HD) maps required for advanced downstream autonomous planners.

</details>


### [178] [RoboFind: Multi-Agent Personalized Object Search for People Who Are Blind or Have Low Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20330)
*Ruiping Liu,Shaofang Quan,Qian Yin,Jingqi Zhang,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Di Wen,Weijia Fan,Kailun Yang,M. Saquib Sarfraz,Tamim Asfour,Kunyu Peng,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboFind 用手机教学 + 四足机器人多智能体搜索特定个人物品，目标身份核验显著提升真实任务成功率并抑制误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人及低视力用户常需定位某个特定的个人物品，而非同类物品的任意实例；这要求机器人主动移动至用户无法到达的视角，并提供可访问的交互界面以确认目标与搜索结果。

Method: 提出 RoboFind 多智能体框架：手机端 Target Teaching Agent 通过 AR 引导、语音与触觉反馈及屏幕阅读器支持的可访问采集流程，将引导录像转化为语义目标档案与可复用的多视角参考库；运行时由 Navigation Agent 探索并提出候选目标，Verification Agent 依据参考库核验身份，Coordination and Recovery Agent 决定完成任务或触发恢复与继续搜索。搭载于四足机器人。

Result: 32 次真实机器人任务成功率 85.0%，重建的顺序式首站停基线为 25.0%（20 次试验、十个目标）；误报成功由 75.0% 降至 5.0%；六个共享目标上 10/12 成功，对照独立执行的 12 次仅用视觉模型的试验为 5/12。

Conclusion: 多智能体设计契合个性化物品搜索需求，在宣布任务完成前先核验物品身份是使结果可信的关键。

Abstract: Blind and low-vision users often need to locate a specific personal object rather than an arbitrary instance of the same category. The task calls for a robot that can move through the space and reach viewpoints the user cannot, and for an accessible interface where the user says which object is meant and learns whether the right one was found. We present RoboFind, a multi-agent framework in which a smartphone teaches the target and a quadruped robot carries out the search. A Target Teaching Agent converts guided smartphone recordings into a semantic target profile and a reusable multi-view reference bank through an accessible capture flow with AR guidance, speech and haptic feedback, and screen-reader support, so later missions refer to a stored object without repeating the teaching process. At runtime, a Navigation Agent explores the environment and proposes candidate targets, a Verification Agent checks each candidate against the stored references, and a Coordination and Recovery Agent completes the mission or triggers recovery and continued search. Across 32 real-robot missions, RoboFind reaches 85.0% success against 25.0% for a reconstructed sequential first-stop baseline over 20 trials with ten targets, and reduces false success from 75.0% to 5.0%. On six shared targets it succeeds in 10/12 trials, against 5/12 for 12 independently executed GPT-6 Astra-only trials. These results show that the multi-agent design fits the demands of personalized object search, where verifying object identity before declaring completion is what makes the outcome something a user can rely on.

</details>


### [179] [Navi-Agent: Unlocalized Monocular Navigation Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20388)
*Wenyuan Xie,Mengyang Hong,Yongzhong Wang,Yanbiao Ji,Yijin Zhou,Shaokai Wu,Shalayiding Sirejiding,Huayi Zhou,Yi-Chao Chen,Ma Ling,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Navi-Agent 提出一种零样本 VLN-CE 智能体，用视觉观测与运动历史构建无坐标的导航拓扑（节点=视觉地点，边=运动转移），支持基于观测的近似自定位、任务进度验证与视觉回访式恢复，在零样本基准与真机上达到几何约束方法中的 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本 VLN-CE 系统多依赖几何定位或坐标表示来维持空间状态；近期几何约束方法去除了深度与全局一致坐标，但难以持续维持用于地点确认、进度验证与失败恢复的空间感知。作者希望在不使用显式坐标的情况下保持持久空间意识。

Method: 将视觉观测与执行过的运动历史组织为无坐标的导航拓扑：节点为视觉地点，边为运动转移。该表示支持基于观测的近似自定位、任务进度验证和基于视觉回访的恢复。采用闭环导航：把指令分解为子目标、执行局部视觉导航、并通过构建的空间状态校验已访问地点。

Result: 在零样本 VLN-CE 基准和真实机器人平台上取得几何约束方法中的 SOTA，同时与依赖几何定位的方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 无坐标的拓扑空间状态可替代几何定位，在零样本连续环境导航中实现持久的空间感知与恢复能力，兼顾性能与部署可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an embodied agent to execute long-horizon instructions in unknown environments. Existing zero-shot VLN-CE systems typically maintain spatial states through geometric localization or coordinate-based representations. Recent geometry-constrained navigation removes depth and globally consistent coordinates, but maintaining persistent spatial awareness for place confirmation, progress verification, and recovery remains challenging. We present Navi-Agent, a zero-shot VLN-CE agent that constructs a coordinate-free spatial state from visual observations and executed motion histories. Navi-Agent organizes this state as a navigation topology, where nodes represent visual places and edges represent motion transitions. This representation enables observation-based approximate self-localization, task progress verification, and visual revisitation-based recovery. Navi-Agent performs closed-loop navigation by decomposing instructions into sub-goals, executing local visual navigation, and verifying visited places through the constructed spatial state. Experiments on zero-shot VLN-CE benchmark and real-world robot platforms show that Navi-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance among geometry-constrained methods while remaining competitive with approaches relying on geometric localization.

</details>


### [180] [Imagine-TAMP: Imagination-Guided Task and Motion Planning in Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20396)
*Antareep Singha,Shivaram Kumar,Yoonwoo Kim,Yoonchang Sung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Imagine-TAMP 提出一种交织规划与执行框架，利用 VLM 塑造目标位置的粒子信念、生成式场景模型想象遮挡区域几何，在部分可观测下用非单位代价比较"先观测"与"先操作遮挡物"的策略，再精化执行并重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境中目标位置常仅部分可观测，机器人需在"再观测一次"与"先操作可能遮挡目标的物体"之间抉择。传统 TAMP 用符号代价或昂贵几何规划做此决策，无法刻画观测揭示被遮挡目标的可能性。

Method: Imagine-TAMP 在昂贵运动规划之前，以语义与几何想象比较候选任务级策略：VLM 依据目标与可见物体的常识关系塑造粒子信念；生成式场景模型估计未观测区域几何；对给定目标假设与想象场景生成多个符号规划骨架，并赋予非单位代价，近似操作代价与感知动作带来的目标可见性收益；选定骨架后精化为可行连续计划并执行，新观测更新信念并触发重规划。

Result: 视角受限的货架场景中，非单位几何评估将成功率从 46.0% 提升至 84.0%；语义信念塑造进一步减少操作与重规划。真机实验中，完整系统相比仅几何的消融将规划时间降低 32%。

Conclusion: 以语义与几何想象引导的任务级评估能显著改善"观测 vs 操作"决策，提升部分可观测下操作成功率与规划效率，并经真机验证。

Abstract: Robots operating in cluttered environments must often manipulate objects whose locations are only partially observable. A central challenge is deciding whether to acquire another observation or to first manipulate objects that may occlude the target. Conventional task and motion planning (TAMP) approaches typically make this decision using symbolic action costs or expensive geometric planning, neither of which adequately captures how likely an observation is to reveal an occluded target. We introduce Imagine-TAMP, an interleaved planning and execution framework that uses semantic and geometric imagination to compare alternative task-level strategies under partial observability before committing to expensive motion planning. A vision-language model shapes a particle belief over target locations using commonsense relationships between the target and visible objects, while a generative scene model estimates plausible geometry in unobserved regions. Given a target hypothesis and imagined scene, Imagine-TAMP generates multiple symbolic plan skeletons and assigns non-unit costs that approximate both manipulation effort and target visibility from sensing actions, distinguishing a short but poorly informative observation strategy from a longer strategy that first manipulates an occluder to better expose the target. The selected skeleton is then refined into a feasible continuous plan and executed, with new observations updating the belief and triggering replanning when necessary. Experiments show that imagination-guided evaluation improves observation-versus-manipulation decisions: in viewpoint-constrained shelf scenes, non-unit geometric evaluation increases success from 46.0% to 84.0%, while semantic belief shaping further reduces manipulation and replanning. On a real robot, the complete system reduces planning time by 32% relative to a geometry-only ablation.

</details>


### [181] [Resilient Motion Planning for Free-Flying Space Robots under Actuator Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20407)
*Nicolas de Maddalena,Joris Verhagen,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种概率式主动运动规划框架，将执行器故障建模为马尔可夫链，结合预计算可达集与RRT*路径拼接，最大化自由飞行机器人到达目标的总概率，并在物理平台上注入故障完成实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由飞行机器人依赖多推进器机动，任一推进器故障都会导致失去控制力而危及任务；但其自由飞行特性意味着即使无驱动也会沿（局部）直线轨迹继续运动。目前规划方法通常不显式考虑执行器故障，因此需要一种能够在规划阶段主动纳入故障概率、并利用自由飞行惯性特征提升任务弹性的方法。

Method: 将执行器故障模式建模为马尔可夫链，并在规划时域上传播到达目标的成功概率；预先计算可达集（reachable sets）以评估在潜在故障下抵达各路点的能力；再由基于RRT*的规划器将这些路点拼接成完整轨迹，从而最大化整体目标到达概率，得到最大弹性的运动规划。方法在物理自由飞行平台上注入执行器故障进行验证。

Result: 在搭载故障注入的物理自由飞行平台上进行了实验验证，规划结果能够提升整体目标到达概率，实现利用自由飞行特性的弹性运动规划。摘要未给出定量对比数据或基线提升幅度。

Conclusion: 通过在规划中显式建模并传播执行器故障概率，并结合可达集与RRT*路点拼接，可在推进器失效情形下生成最大化目标到达概率的弹性运动规划；该框架在真实自由飞行器上得到验证，为空间机器人故障容错规划提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Free-flying robots rely on multiple thrusters to maneuver in space. If one or more of these thrusters fail, the robot may lose control authority and risk mission failure. At the same time, their free-flying nature implies that, even in the absence of actuation, they continue along (locally) straight-line trajectories. In this work we present a probabilistic, proactive, motion planning framework that explicitly accounts for actuator failures in space. We model actuator failure modes as a Markov chain and propagate the probability of successfully reaching the goal along the planning horizon. Precomputed reachable sets evaluate the robot's capabilities of reaching waypoints under potential failures and an RRT$^*$-based planner concatenates these waypoints. The resulting algorithm maximizes the overall target-reaching probability, providing maximally resilient motion plans utilizing free-flying properties. We validate our approach experimentally on a physical free-flyer platform with injected actuator failures.

</details>


### [182] [Time-Efficient Iterative Learning Planning for Safety-Critical Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20435)
*Zhiyi Chen,Shuli Lv,Chen Min,Yong Xu,Jian Sun,Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将迭代学习规划(ILP)与前瞻性风险混合控制障碍函数(ARB-CBF)结合，实现计算受限平台上兼具时效率与动态避障安全保证的自主导航框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在算力受限条件下需兼顾规划时效性与安全关键的动态避障。ILP虽轻量高效但缺乏动态障碍感知与规避机制，现有基于优化的方法计算开销较大。

Method: 扩展ILP：基于局部障碍风险，用分数幂更新律学习通行速度与转向偏置曲线，生成标称控制指令；运行时由ARB-CBF对指令进行修正，提供实时安全保证。复杂度分析为ILP重规划O(kN)，ARB-CBF为线性复杂度。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验验证了框架有效性，相比基于优化的基线在时间效率与安全性上更优，计算开销更低，适合资源受限平台。

Conclusion: ILP+ARB-CBF可在低算力条件下实现安全关键的动态避障导航，为资源受限平台提供实用方案。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots require timeefficient planning and safety-critical dynamic obstacle avoidance under constrained onboard computation. While Iterative Learning Planning (ILP) offers lightweight and efficient traversal planning, it lacks explicit mechanisms for dynamic obstacle perception and avoidance. This article extends ILP to safety-critical navigation in dynamic environments by integrating an anticipatory risk-blended control barrier function (ARB-CBF). The extended ILP learns traversal-speed and steering-bias profiles via a fractionalpower update based on local obstacle risk, generating nominal control commands that ARB-CBF modifies at runtime for real-time safety guarantees. Algorithmic analysis demonstrates that the ILP replanning stage scales at O(kN) for k iterations and N waypoints, while ARB-CBF executes with linear complexity. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments validate the framework, demonstrating superior temporal efficiency and safety with lower computational overhead compared to optimizationbased baselines, making it highly suitable for resourceconstrained platforms.

</details>


### [183] [Spatial-Semantic Uncertainty in VLM-Based Target Search: Balancing Exploration and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20443)
*Alkesh K. Srivastava,Jonathan Diller,Vijay Kumar,Philip Dames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间-语义不确定性分解框架，将目标搜索中的位置不确定性与身份语义不确定性分别建模，并用期望信息增益（EIG）规划器在探查与识别之间显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于VLM的自然语言目标搜索系统常把"在哪里找"（空间不确定性）与"哪个候选才是目标"（语义不确定性）两类不确定性混为一谈，导致探索与识别决策缺乏明确的权衡机制，也忽略了对未发现目标概率质量的建模。

Method: 维护空间与语义两套独立信念，将VLM概率证据融合进全局目标身份后验（含未发现目标的质量）；由此定义空间EIG与语义EIG，供信息论规划器分别评估"候选发现"与"目标消歧"的价值；在500个合成目标上评测6种VLM不确定性引出接口的校准性，并进行退化观测下的搜索-识别实验，对比不同空间-语义权重。

Result: 6种接口在识别准确率相近的情况下校准与过度自信差异显著；EIG规划器在75.0%–92.5%的试验中达到置信决策，随机搜索仅20.0%；不同空间-语义权重在达到置信后识别准确率相当，提高语义权重可减少无效探索与VLM查询次数。

Conclusion: 不确定性表示与不确定性驱动规划在具身VLM系统中承担不同角色；显式对语义不确定性进行规划可加速目标确认而不损害决策质量。

Abstract: Robots searching for a target from a natural-language description must determine not only where to search, but also which observed candidate is the desired target. These decisions reflect two distinct sources of uncertainty - spatial uncertainty over candidate locations and semantic uncertainty over target identity - that are often conflated in VLM-based search systems. We introduce a spatial-semantic uncertainty formulation that maintains separate beliefs over each component and integrates probabilistic VLM evidence into a global target-identity posterior, including probability mass for undiscovered targets. This decomposition allows an information-theoretic planner to independently value candidate discovery and target disambiguation through spatial and semantic expected information gain (EIG), providing an explicit mechanism for trading broader exploration against earlier identification. We evaluate six VLM uncertainty-elicitation interfaces on 500 synthetic targets and show that similar recognition accuracy can conceal substantial differences in calibration and false confidence. In degraded-observation search-and-identify experiments, EIG-based planners reach confident decisions in 75.0%-92.5% of trials, compared with 20.0% for Random search, while different spatial-semantic weightings achieve comparable identification accuracy once confidence is attained. Increasing semantic emphasis reduces unnecessary exploration and VLM queries, demonstrating that explicitly planning over semantic uncertainty can accelerate target resolution without sacrificing decision quality. These results highlight the distinct roles of uncertainty representation and uncertainty-driven planning in embodied VLM systems.

</details>


### [184] [Visual Sim-to-Real Learning for Robotic Insertion under Geometric Variations: Application to Rebar Installation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20477)
*Tao Sun,Beining Han,Patrick Yin,Rui Xu,Harry He,Abhishek Gupta,Szymon Rusinkiewicz,Yi Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RebarSim 是一个完全在仿真中训练的视觉 sim-to-real 系统，通过特权状态教师策略蒸馏为多视角学生策略，实现钢筋插入任务零样本真机迁移，真实工厂批次钢筋插孔成功率 91.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钢筋插入是建筑工地最具重复性和体力消耗的任务之一，间隙仅 1.4 mm，属于强接触问题。零件在两层上变化（结构件的标称设计、每件标称设计周边的制造公差），导致真实数据必须随设计和批次变化重新采集。作者希望避免反复采集真机数据，用纯仿真训练实现零样本真机迁移。

Method: 用强化学习在程序化生成的钢筋几何上训练特权状态教师策略；再通过蒸馏将其迁移到多视角学生策略，学生直接从原始 RGB 与本体感受映射到动作；训练中使用大量域随机化（外观随机化）与 DAgger 混合数据。

Result: 学生在真机上零样本迁移，对真实工厂生产线钢筋的插孔成功率为 91.3%。几何多样性训练比单一设计训练更能提升教师和学生在未见设计上的零样本成功率，且学生策略在单设计专家自己的设计上仍优于该专家。预训练学生适应新设计所需蒸馏样本比从头训练少 4–6 倍。去除外观随机化或 DAgger 混合任一，成功率都显著下降。

Conclusion: 纯仿真训练 + 教师-学生蒸馏 + 外观随机化与 DAgger 混合，可实现接触丰富、公差敏感装配任务的零样本真机迁移；几何多样性与预训练是关键增益来源。

Abstract: Rebar insertion is among the most repetitive and physically demanding tasks on construction sites, and a contact-rich problem at 1.4 mm clearance. The parts, however, vary at two levels: a nominal design per structural member, and fabrication tolerance around each nominal design. Real-world data therefore has to be re-collected as designs and batches change. We present RebarSim, a visual sim-to-real system trained entirely in simulation. A privileged state-based teacher is trained with reinforcement learning over procedurally generated rebar geometries, then distilled into a multi-view student that maps raw RGB and proprioception directly to actions under extensive domain randomization. The student transfers to the real world zero-shot, seating rebars taken from a real factory production run in 91.3% of real-robot rollouts. Underlying that result, geometry diversity and pretraining both bring benefits. Training across a diverse set of nominal designs rather than one lifts the zero-shot success of both the teacher and the student on unseen designs, and the student policy outperforms a single-design specialist on that specialist's own design. A pretrained student then adapts to a new design with 4--6x fewer distillation samples than one trained from scratch. Visual sim-to-real transfer depends on appearance randomization and the DAgger mixture: removing either one sharply lowers success. Videos, code, and task assets are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [185] [Worst-Case Hidden-Vehicle Trajectory Search in Spatiotemporal Occlusion Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20480)
*Ruichen Tan,Zengxiang Lei,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HC-MTS 将时序遮挡推理与响应感知的极小极大搜索结合，构造可证隐藏智能体轨迹模态并求解双层极小极大问题，在 Waymo 数据集上显著削减隐藏种子并找出可避免的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中遮挡带来根本性不确定性；现有方法多依赖逐帧假设传播或针对既定隐藏智能体预测优化自车行为，未探索与历史一致的最坏交互情形。

Method: HC-MTS 首先构造有限隐藏状态模态，每个模态由满足多帧可见性、占据、语义地图支持与类别运动学约束的后向见证认证；随后求解双层极小极大问题：内层有限 oracle 在到达目标与乘坐舒适度上最大化自车行驶得分，外层搜索选择使该最优响应值最小的合法隐藏车辆轨迹。

Result: 在 8 个 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 场景中，可见性记忆时域从 K=1 增至 K=20 使每场景平均保留的车辆、行人及总隐藏种子数分别减少 18.12%、21.67%、18.45%；HC-MTS 识别出 6 个可避免反例，其余 2 个场景在有限搜索预算内未找到能产生碰撞的合法攻击者。

Conclusion: 结合历史一致性的遮挡推理与对抗性极小极大搜索可有效压缩隐藏假设空间并暴露安全关键反例，为遮挡场景下的自动驾驶安全评估与规划提供新范式。

Abstract: Occlusion creates fundamental uncertainty in autonomous driving. Existing methods often propagate frame-wise hypotheses or optimize ego behavior against prescribed hidden-agent predictions, leaving the worst history-consistent interaction unexplored. We introduce History-Conditioned Minimax Trajectory Search (HC-MTS), which combines temporal occlusion reasoning with response-aware search. First, HC-MTS constructs finite hidden-state modes, each certified by a backward witness satisfying multi-frame visibility, occupancy, semantic-map support, and class-specific kinematic constraints. It then solves a bilevel minimax problem: an inner finite oracle maximizes the ego driving score over destination attainment and ride comfort, while the outer search selects the legal hidden-vehicle trajectory that minimizes this best-response value. Across eight Waymo Open Motion Dataset scenarios, increasing the visibility-memory horizon from K=1 to K=20 reduces the mean per-scenario vehicle, pedestrian, and total retained hidden-seed counts by 18.12%, 21.67%, and 18.45%, respectively. HC-MTS identifies six avoidable counterexamples, while no legal collision-producing attacker is found in the remaining two scenes within the finite search budget.

</details>


### [186] [Towards AI-enhanced control: a numerical technique for trajectory smoothing of a parallel robot for pancreatic surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20499)
*Iosif Birlescu,Alexandru Pusca,Bogdan Gherman,Calin Vaida,Ionut Zima,Damien Chablat,Doina Pisla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向微创胰外科并联机器人，提出基于3D SpaceMouse速度指令的实时末端S曲线轨迹平滑方法，通过分段生成S曲线限制加速度，数值仿真验证稳定过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术中主从遥操作的速度指令会带来末端加速度突变，加剧组织损伤；需要一种可用于实时主从控制架构的轨迹平滑方法。

Method: 在末端速度场中生成S曲线（梯形/Jerk受限速度规划），依据3D SpaceMouse的指令输入对S曲线分段以支持实时控制；讨论加速度时间恒定的特例；求解并联机器人逆运动学以映射到主动关节空间。

Result: 数值仿真显示末端参数空间与主动关节参数均实现无突变的稳定过渡，验证方法有效性；尚未进行实物实验。

Conclusion: 方法为医疗并联机器人的实时速度平滑提供可行方案，后续将开展实验模型验证并集成到基于AI的训练模块。

Abstract: The paper presents a numerical approach for the end-effector trajectory smoothing of a parallel robot designed for minimally invasive pancreatic surgery. The approach is tailored for real-time master-slave control architecture and uses a 3D space mouse for command input for velocity control. The trajectory smoothing is achieved by generating S-curves in the end-effector velocity fields, thus controlling the accelerations, which in turn reduces tissue trauma in the minimally invasive procedures. Real-time control is enabled by segmenting the S-curves based on the command inputs from the 3D space mouse. A special case is considered where the acceleration time is constant for all command inputs. Numeric results demonstrate stable transitions (without abrupt changes) in both the end-effector parameter space and in the active joints parameters, thereby validating the proposed approach. Further work aims to test the approach on an experimental model and integrate it into AI-based training modules.

</details>


### [187] [Integrated Guidance and Control of a Mother-Child UAV-UGV System for Cooperative Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20540)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实飞验证了一套异构“巡视车-母机-子机”自主空中回收框架，实现子机部署、出击、自主返航、空中回收与同步下降，20次户外任务成功17次（85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中回收面的特殊性在于回收平台本身是主动控制、推力受限的飞行器（悬停多旋翼母机），与地面或舰船平台回收差异显著，现有方法难以直接适用，需系统级自主回收方案。

Method: 回收栈包含：jerk有界参考轨迹生成、扰动观测器增强的平面跟踪、可行性感知的垂直控制、基于离散时间障碍函数（CBF）的相对垂直几何安全滤波器、通信感知的母机状态预测；在PX4-ROS 2架构上实现，融合RTK GNSS/IMU/气压，母机侧1D激光雷达仅作近接触辅助线索。

Result: 20次户外协同任务中17次完成部署、出击与回收，成功率85%；成功回收平均末端对准时间6.3 s，接受时刻平均平面误差0.18 m，终端最大平面偏差0.32 m（捕获半径0.40 m），耦合下降最小相对垂直间距0.41 m；母机平面定点RMS误差0.25 m；全过程维持RTK固定解。3次失败分布在不同任务阶段并单独分析。

Conclusion: 结果表明在所测试的户外运行包线内，悬停多旋翼母机对子机的自主空中回收具备实用可行性；核心贡献在于多方法协调的系统级集成与全尺寸户外验证。

Abstract: Autonomous recovery of a small multirotor onto a hovering multirotor carrier differs from recovery onto ground or shipborne platforms because the recovery surface is itself an actively controlled, thrust-limited aerial vehicle. This paper presents a field-validated autonomy framework for a heterogeneous rover-mothership-child system executing rover supervision, mothership transit, child deployment and sortie, autonomous return, aerial recovery, and synchronized descent. The recovery stack combines jerk-bounded reference generation, disturbance-observer-augmented planar tracking, feasibility-aware vertical control, a discrete-time barrier-based safety filter for relative vertical geometry, and communication-aware carrier-state prediction. The contribution is the coordinated system-level integration of these methods for recovery onto a hovering multirotor and its full-scale outdoor validation. The framework is implemented on a PX4-ROS 2 architecture using RTK-enabled GNSS, IMU, and barometric fusion, with mothership-side 1D lidar used only as an auxiliary near-contact cue. RTK-fixed positioning was maintained throughout testing. Across 20 outdoor cooperative missions, 17 successfully completed deployment, sortie, and recovery, giving an observed mission success rate of 85%. For successful recoveries, mean terminal-alignment time was 6.3 s, mean planar alignment error at acceptance was 0.18 m, maximum terminal planar deviation was 0.32 m within a 0.40 m capture radius, and minimum logged relative vertical separation during coupled descent was 0.41 m. Mothership planar station-keeping RMS error was 0.25 m. The three unsuccessful trials occurred at different mission stages and are analyzed separately. Results demonstrate practical autonomous aerial recovery within the tested outdoor operating envelope.

</details>


### [188] [Learning Slope-Adaptive Whole-Body Locomotion for Humanoid Robots in Roofing Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20558)
*Songyang Liu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务语义+度量屋顶场景约束的全身运动学习框架，将人类演示重定向后经轨迹级优化与执行感知RL落地到Unitree G1，实现屋面行走、钉枪、锤击、推压等施工动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在屋面坡面上作业需同时协调行走、平衡与工作姿态，直接重定向的人类演示虽保持动作外观，却使脚/手相对屋面的空间关系错误（支撑点、作业间隙、穿透）。因此需要一个把任务语义与度量场景几何绑定的人体运动学习方法。

Method: （1）用跟踪系统采集人类演示并重定向到Unitree G1；（2）用度量屋顶模型补充跟踪系统缺失的空间参考；（3）轨迹级优化把推断出的支撑接触与标注的作业关系锚定到屋顶几何；（4）执行感知强化学习，使策略在动态跟踪误差下仍保持这些几何关系。评估含多动作跟踪、屋面坡度覆盖矩阵、五路钉枪消融、锤击/侧推跨任务实验，以及与纯RL、零样本遥操作的对比。

Result: 所有评估种子上均满足支撑、作业间隙与非穿透判据；钉枪、锤击、推压的工作间隙误差为0.256–0.531 cm，各任务3/3成功；物理实验复现上坡行走、钉枪、锤击、弯腰动作，基座坐标系平均运动误差低于80 mm。

Conclusion: 以场景为基准的人类运动学习可作为面向施工的人形运动原语的有效基础，为坡度作业等任务语义强耦合的场景提供了从演示到实机的可行路径。

Abstract: Roofing requires workers to coordinate locomotion, balance, and work-related body motions on pitched surfaces, creating a challenging application for humanoid robots. Directly retargeted human demonstrations, however, may preserve motion appearance while placing the robot's feet or hands incorrectly relative to the roof. This study presents a task-semantic scene-grounded framework for learning roofer-style whole-body motions on a Unitree G1. Human demonstrations are captured using a tracking system and retargeted to the robot, while a metric roof model supplies the spatial reference unavailable from the tracking system. A trajectory-level optimization grounds inferred support contacts and annotated work relations to the roof, and execution-aware reinforcement learning encourages the resulting policy to preserve these relations under dynamic tracking errors. The framework is evaluated through a multi-motion tracking study, a roof-pitch coverage matrix, a five-way nailgun ablation, cross-task experiments on hammering and lateral pushing, and comparisons with pure reinforcement learning and zero-shot teleoperation. Our method enables the robot to satisfy support, work-clearance, and nonpenetration criteria across all evaluated seeds. Across nailgun, hammering, and pushing, it achieves work-clearance errors between 0.256 and 0.531 cm and 3/3 successful evaluations per task. Physical experiments reproduce uphill walking, nailgun, hammering, and bending motions with mean base-frame motion errors below 80 mm. These findings establish scene-grounded human motion learning as a promising basis for construction-oriented humanoid motion primitives.

</details>


### [189] [OmniMimic: Dynamics-completed Motion Augmentation for Multi-style Omnidirectional Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20566)
*Sheng Wu,Guoqiang Zhao,Zhe Yang,Fei Teng,Zhikun Zhou,Yanlin Yang,Zheng Fang,Hong Zheng,Yaonan Wang,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniMimic 通过时序反转、约束动力学补全与矢状面镜像扩充动物示教数据，并配合渐进命令扩展与软门控步态专家，将方向受限的动物示教训练成覆盖目标各轴速度范围的多步态策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物示教能为四足机器人提供难以用人工奖励指定的自然步态风格，但示教数据方向覆盖狭窄，缺少后退、侧移与转向命令的风格一致监督信号，难以直接扩展为全向多步态策略。

Method: OmniMimic 分两步：(1) 用时间反转、约束动力学补全和矢状面反射，构造超出原始示教方向的机器人运动学与物理监督；(2) 将命令从示教速度分布逐步扩展到目标各轴速度边界，并采用共享 actor 加软门控、步态专用残差专家的架构，兼顾可复用运动技能与步态特定修正。

Result: 在仿真中对四种步态评估，相比匹配的 APEX 基线，前后向参考速度下的平均足部位置 RMSE 降低 12.9%，均匀笛卡尔命令网格上的速度跟踪 RMSE 降低 63.1%。

Conclusion: 通过数据侧的方向扩展与训练侧的渐进命令扩展和专家门控，可有效把方向受限的动物示教转化为全向多步态运动策略，显著提升足部跟踪与速度跟踪精度。

Abstract: Animal demonstrations provide quadruped robots with natural and distinctive gait styles that are difficult to specify through hand-crafted rewards. However, their narrow directional coverage leaves little style-consistent supervision for backward, lateral, and turning commands. We present OmniMimic, a training framework that turns directionally limited animal demonstrations into a single multi-gait policy over target per-axis velocity ranges. OmniMimic first combines temporal reversal, constrained dynamics completion, and sagittal reflection to construct robot-specific kinematic and physical supervision beyond the observed directions. It then expands commands progressively from the demonstrated velocity distribution toward the target per-axis bounds, and uses a shared actor with soft-gated, gait-specialized residual experts to balance reusable locomotion skills with gait-specific corrections. Across four gaits in simulation, OmniMimic reduces mean foot-position RMSE at forward and backward reference velocities by 12.9% and velocity-tracking RMSE on a uniform Cartesian command grid by 63.1%, compared with the matched APEX baseline. The project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [190] [Walking on the Slope: Stable Bipedal Gaits with Genetic-Algorithm-Optimized Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20570)
*Madhav Rijal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对一台8自由度双足机器人进行运动学/动力学建模、轨迹生成与稳定性分析：DH参数与齐次变换建立正运动学，闭式逆运动学将三次样条生成的髋与摆动足笛卡尔轨迹映射为关节角，Newton-Euler迭代法求关节力矩，ZMP判据评估动态稳定性，并用遗传算法在ZMP可行性惩罚下优化髋高、最大抬足高度与额状面倾角以最小化关节做功。MATLAB仿真表明该模型在步时0.5 s以内、坡度22.5°以内ZMP稳定；超出极限则ZMP逸出支撑多边形，需修改足部尺寸或轨迹参数；ZMP稳定性由各连杆质量分布而非总质量主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为8自由度双足机器人在平地与斜坡行走提供一套完整的建模、轨迹规划与稳定性评估框架，并借助优化手段在保证ZMP稳定的前提下降低关节能耗，同时厘清质量总量与质量分布对稳定性的不同影响。

Method: 采用DH参数与齐次变换推导正运动学，闭式逆运动学实现笛卡尔轨迹到关节空间的映射；髋与摆动足轨迹用三次样条生成；关节力矩由Newton-Euler迭代算法计算；稳定性以ZMP判据评价；用遗传算法以关节做功最小为目标、并施加ZMP可行性惩罚，优化髋高、最大抬足高度与额状面倾角。全部验证基于MATLAB仿真。

Result: 名义8自由度模型在给定足部几何下，步完成时间可短至0.5 s、坡度可达22.5°仍保持ZMP稳定；超过该界限ZMP移出支撑多边形，需调整足部尺寸或轨迹参数。此外，ZMP稳定性主要受各连杆质量分布影响，而非机器人总质量。

Conclusion: 论文给出了从建模、轨迹生成到稳定性和能耗优化的完整双足行走分析流程，量化了步时与坡度的稳定边界，并指出质量分布是ZMP稳定性的主导因素；但结论均来自仿真，缺乏物理样机验证。

Abstract: This paper presents the kinematic and dynamic modeling, trajectory generation, and stability analysis of an 8-degree-of-freedom (DOF) biped robot walking on flat and inclined terrain. Denavit-Hartenberg (DH) parameters and homogeneous transformations are used to derive the forward kinematics, while closed-form inverse kinematics maps the desired hip and swing-foot Cartesian trajectories, generated with cubic splines, to joint angles. Joint torques are computed using the Newton-Euler iterative algorithm, and dynamic stability is evaluated using the zero moment point (ZMP) criterion. A genetic algorithm (GA) optimizes the hip height, maximum swing-foot lift, and frontal-plane tilt angle by minimizing the work done by the joints subject to a ZMP feasibility penalty. Simulation results in MATLAB show that the nominal 8-DOF model remains ZMP-stable for step completion times down to 0.5 s and for slope inclinations up to 22.5 degrees with the given foot geometry. Beyond these limits, the ZMP leaves the support polygon, and either the foot dimensions or the trajectory parameters must be modified. The results also show that ZMP stability is governed by the mass distribution among the links rather than the total mass of the robot.

</details>


### [191] [Accelerating Visual Policy Learning with Sampling-Based Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20575)
*Yilang Liu,Haoxiang You,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SGPS将采样型MPC的目标动作精化与一阶策略梯度（可微仿真）交替耦合，配合行为克隆初始化与解耦渲染的可微计算图，从深度观测直接训练视觉运动策略，并在真机Go2上零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微仿真带来的一阶策略梯度（FoPG）训练成本低，但局部优化易收敛到非预期的接触模式；纯MPC采样则难以学习可复用的视觉闭环策略。作者希望兼得采样搜索的全局性与FoPG的端到端高效性。

Method: 核心是"采样引导"的交替优化：(1) 用采样型MPC反复精化动作目标，行为克隆从采样动作初始化策略；(2) 训练在扰动初始状态与随机化动力学下，交替进行采样精化与短时域FoPG更新；(3) 视觉策略采用解耦FoPG，把渲染排除在计算图外，从而无需状态-策略教师、直接从深度图学习。

Result: 单GPU上在Unitree Go2与G1的仿真中完成行走、越障、推箱、双臂搬运任务；消融表明精化环节带来的收益超出仅初始化与轨迹跟踪。蒸馏后的策略零样本迁移到真实Go2，仅用机载深度自主实现小跑、匍匐、跨栏及行为切换。

Conclusion: 采样精化与一阶可微优化的耦合能缓解接触局部最优、降低视觉策略训练成本，并具备单卡仿真到真机的零样本迁移能力，为接触丰富任务提供实用训练范式。

Abstract: Learning visual policies for locomotion and manipulation requires coordinating contact with the environment and can incur substantial computation and GPU memory costs. First-order policy gradients (FoPG) reduce training cost through differentiable simulation, but local optimization can converge to unintended contact patterns. To address this shortfall, we propose Sampling-Guided Policy Search (SGPS), which couples recurring action-target refinement by sampling-based model-predictive control with first-order policy optimization. Behavior cloning initializes the policy from sampled actions; training then alternates sampling-based refinement with short-horizon FoPG updates under perturbed initial states and randomized dynamics. For visual policy training, we use a decoupled FoPG formulation that excludes rendering from the computation graph, enabling direct learning from depth observations without a state-policy teacher. On a single GPU, SGPS learns policies for locomotion, obstacle traversal, crate pushing, and bimanual carrying on simulated Unitree Go2 and G1 robots. Our experiments further show that refinement improves policy learning beyond initialization and tracking alone. For hardware deployment, the distilled policy transfers zero-shot to a real Go2 and uses onboard depth to autonomously trot, crawl, clear hurdles, and switch between these behaviors.

</details>


### [192] [V2-STRep: VLM-Grounded Structured Task Representations for Reusable Robot Skills Acquired from Generated Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20582)
*Yexin Hu,Dongheui Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V2-STRep 提出零样本框架，将人类操作视频生成模型产出的运动转化为可复用的机器人技能：借助 VLM 构建结构化任务表示（相位、参考、约束），用点/点法线/轴/平面/6D 位姿等最小几何结构描述目标，并通过几何规则与任务约束轨迹优化实现跨场景迁移与指令复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类操作视频蕴含丰富的运动与交互线索，可从无机器人示教的条件下获取技能；但视频生成模型对每个任务只能给出一次场景特定的实现，恢复出的运动仅隐含任务结构、几何关系与约束，难以复用。作者希望在不重新生成视频的前提下，把生成视频中的运动转为可跨场景、跨指令复用的机器人技能。

Method: 零样本框架 V2-STRep：(1) 用 VLM 将生成视频运动落地为结构化任务表示，显式给出运动相位、参考系与任务相关约束；(2) 目标用最小几何结构表达——点、点法线、轴、平面与完整 6D 位姿；(3) 将 VLM 提供的 2D 图像空间线索通过 RGB-D 观测提升到 3D，重建任务几何与候选抓取位姿；(4) 几何专用规则把运动迁移到新场景；(5) 任务约束轨迹优化联合抓取选择与完整机器人运动规划，在满足任务要求的同时利用剩余旋转自由度兼容关节限位；(6) 更新部署时的落地与约束即可在新兼容指令下复用，无需再次生成视频。

Result: 在六个真实世界操作任务上，执行成功率优于基线方法；成功获得的技能可实现可靠的跨场景迁移，并能适应更改后的部署指令。

Conclusion: 将生成视频运动提升为 VLM 落地的结构化、几何化任务表示，可把一次性、场景特定的视频演示蒸馏为可复用、可迁移、可随指令更新的机器人技能，为视频生成驱动的技能获取提供了可操作的中间表示与优化流程。

Abstract: Human manipulation videos provide rich motion and interaction cues for acquiring robot skills without robot demonstrations. Video generation models synthesize such demonstrations from an initial scene image and task instruction, avoiding the need to record demonstrations for each task. However, the recovered motion captures only one scene-specific realization, leaving task structure, geometric relations, and constraints implicit. We present V2-STRep, a zero-shot framework that converts generated video motion into reusable robot skills through VLM-grounded structured task representations. The representation specifies motion phases, references, and task-relevant constraints, with targets described by minimal geometric structures: points, point-normals, axes, planes, and full 6D poses. VLM-provided 2D image-space cues are lifted into 3D using RGB-D observations to reconstruct task geometry and candidate grasp poses. Geometry-specific rules transfer motion to new scenes, while task-constrained trajectory optimization couples grasp selection with complete robot motion planning. It preserves task requirements while using remaining rotational freedom to accommodate joint limits. Updating deployment grounding and constraints enables reuse under new compatible instructions without generating another video. Experiments on six real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improved execution success over baselines, reliable cross-scene transfer of successfully acquired skills, and adaptation to changed deployment instructions.

</details>


### [193] [CoRef-GS: Cooperative Referring Gaussian Splatting for Multi-Agent Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20586)
*Zhikun Zhou,Kunyu Peng,Runyi Yang,Junhao Cai,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Danda Pani Paudel,Yi Zhou,Luc Van Gool,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRef-GS：面向多智能体协同的指代式高斯溅射定位框架，构建开放词汇实例感知高斯地图，跨智能体几何+语义对齐后以视角条件掩码关系图进行指代定位，并提出双四足基准CoQuad-Ref。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身机器人指代场景理解需在指定视角下对物体/关系语言查询进行grounding。单个智能体的局部语义高斯图可实现，但协同场景下地图独立重建后经对齐融合，被指代目标或其上下文地标可能来自另一智能体的观测，而空间关系仍需从查询机器人视角解释。现有语言感知高斯方法仅面向单地图查询，高斯配准方法仅优化几何/光度对齐，未保持语言grounding所需的语义兼容性。

Method: CoRef-GS构建局部开放词汇实例感知高斯地图；通过跨智能体对齐模块，以几何与语义一致性对齐部分重叠地图；再用视角条件掩码关系图（view-conditioned mask relation graph）完成指代grounding。同时提出CoQuad-Ref双四足基准，覆盖真实与仿真室内场景。

Result: 仿真场景中旋转误差由粗初始化后的2.58°降至精化后0.15°；真实场景指代mIoU从ReferSplat的52.6%提升至68.8%。基准与源码将公开。

Conclusion: 面向融合地图的协同指代高斯grounding需同时满足几何可对齐、实例级语义可比与视角条件关系推理；CoRef-GS将语义兼容性纳入跨智能体对齐，在多四足协同指代任务上显著优于单图基线，但真实世界验证规模与部署细节仍有限。

Abstract: Referring scene understanding for embodied robots requires grounding object- and relation-centric language queries from a designated viewpoint. While a local semantic Gaussian map can support such grounding within one agent's observations, cooperative settings require this ability to remain effective after independently reconstructed maps are aligned and fused. In this setting, the referred target or its contextual landmark may come from another agent's observations, while spatial relations must still be interpreted from the querying robot's viewpoint. We formulate this problem as cooperative referring Gaussian grounding over fused maps, which requires geometric alignability, instance-level semantic comparability, and view-conditioned relation reasoning. Existing language-aware Gaussian methods mainly focus on single-map querying, whereas Gaussian registration methods optimize geometric or photometric alignment without preserving language-grounding-oriented semantic compatibility. We propose CoRef-GS, a cooperative referring Gaussian splatting framework. CoRef-GS constructs local open-vocabulary instance-aware Gaussian maps, then aligns partially overlapping maps with a cross-agent alignment module by geometric and semantic consistency, and grounds queries using a view-conditioned mask relation graph. We further introduce CoQuad-Ref, a dual-quadruped benchmark spanning both real-world and simulated indoor scenes. Experiments show that, on simulated scenes, CoRef-GS reduces the rotation error from 2.58° after coarse initialization to 0.15° after refinement, and improves real-world referring mIoU over ReferSplat from 52.6% to 68.8%. The established benchmark and source code will be publicly released at this https URL .

</details>


### [194] [Semantic SLAM in Precision Agriculture using Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20604)
*Ruben Beumer,Sander Doodeman,René van de Molengraft,Duarte Antunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向精准农业的实时语义世界建模框架，将贝叶斯概率语义地图与基于g²o的图优化SLAM融合，用YOLOv8n从深度相机提取作物类别/尺寸/健康语义，在Gazebo与Spot实机上验证了至少400株植物的实时建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人既要定位又要理解作物语义，而单纯依赖GPS在田间不可靠；需要把语义属性与SLAM统一到一个可在线运行的世界模型中，支撑边作业边建图。

Method: 图优化SLAM（g²o）提供位姿与地图骨架，贝叶斯推断维护对象及其语义属性的概率分布并随观测更新；YOLOv8n在深度相机观测上检测对象并输出类别、尺寸、健康等语义；语义与几何在同一因子图/地图中融合，实现不依赖GPS的定位与语义建图。

Result: 在Gazebo仿真与室内人工植物场地、使用Boston Dynamics Spot的实机实验中验证，系统可实时完成至少400株植物的建图与语义标注，定位不依赖GPS。

Conclusion: 语义SLAM可与概率语义属性推断紧耦合，在农业场景实现实时、可扩展的语义世界模型；但验证仍限于室内受控环境与仿真，语义仅作映射输出，未闭环用于作业决策，泛化性与大规模田间验证待补。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time semantic world modeling framework specialized for precision agriculture using autonomous robots. The framework combines probabilistic mapping of objects and their semantic attributes, updated through Bayesian inference, with a graph-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) approach implemented using $g^2o$, a general framework for graph optimization. This integration enables accurate mapping and localization without relying solely on GPS. By leveraging semantic information such as plant type, size, and health, the robot can perform tasks while mapping and localizing itself within a field of crops. The proposed framework was validated through Gazebo simulations and physical experiments on an indoor field with artificial plants using Boston Dynamics' robot dog Spot. A YOLOv8n object detection model was trained to extract object and semantic data from depth camera observations. These simulations and experiments demonstrate that the system can successfully perform real-time mapping of up to at least 400 plants.

</details>


### [195] [Bayesian Continuum Robot Dynamics and State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20605)
*James M. Ferguson,Tucker Hermans,Alan Kuntz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将连续体机器人的Cosserat杆动力学近似引入因子图状态估计，把惯性与阻尼写成等效外载荷，使动态平衡保持静态形式的代数结构，从而在无主干观测时退化为随机前向仿真，有观测时联合优化运动学与动力学状态并推断外载荷，并用腱驱连续体机器人的仿真与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有连续体机器人因子图状态估计依赖准静态假设或白噪声运动先验，在惯性效应显著的动态运动中无法刻画真实物理，精度受限。作者希望在保留先前静态因子图代数形式与工程便利性的前提下，把真实的杆动力学纳入估计框架。

Method: 以Cosserat杆动力学为物理模型，将惯性项与阻尼项表示为等效外载荷，使动态平衡方程在代数形式上与准静态情形一致，从而复用既有因子图结构。该框架在缺少主干观测时等价于对机器人运动做随机前向仿真；在给定观测时，联合精化运动学与动力学状态，并同时推断外载荷等隐状态。

Result: 在腱驱动连续体机器人上通过仿真与实物实验进行验证，展示了随机前向仿真能力以及在动态运动下的状态估计效果。

Conclusion: 将惯性与阻尼重构为等效外载荷是一种保持因子图简洁性的连续体机器人动力学建模途径，使同一框架统一支持动态前向仿真与状态估计，为动态场景下的连续体机器人感知提供了物理一致的方案。

Abstract: Recent factor graph approaches to continuum robot state estimation have been successful for quasi-static applications and spatiotemporal estimation using white-noise kinematic motion priors. However, when inertial effects are significant, these approximations may fail to capture the underlying physics, limiting accuracy during dynamic motions. In contrast, our approach approximates the Cosserat rod dynamics of continuum robots. We write inertia and damping as equivalent applied loads, so that the dynamic balance retains the algebraic form of the static one from prior work with quasi-static robots. Without backbone observations, the framework reduces to a stochastic forward simulation of the robot's motion. Given observations, it jointly refines kinematic and dynamic states and infers external loads, among other states. We validate the approach through simulation and experiments, demonstrating stochastic forward simulation as well as state estimation on tendon-driven continuum robots.

</details>


### [196] [INSPECT: Learning Robot View Selection from Assistant Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20615)
*Di Wen,Kailun Yang,Wenhao Guo,Yitian Shi,Junwei Zheng,Yufan Chen,Ruiping Liu,Jiale Wei,Rania Rayyes,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INSPECT：从智能眼镜助手记录中学习机器人视角偏好，用于装配零件在位与安装状态的检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 装配检查中机器人需判断零件是否在位、是否安装正确，但视角选择缺乏监督标签，且需仅凭当前观测与已知位姿决策。

Method: Presence-Invariant TwinSwap 通过成对身份干预校准物体证据；claim-indexed 监督将证据需求与相机可复现的观测变化解耦；object-centered calibration 将相对视角偏好适配到机器人位姿；clause-level screening 校验预测证据；仅用当前观测与已知位姿选视角。

Result: 物理变速箱装配图像上，非 oracle 策略中视角效用最高，人工评分完整可验证性由 34.8% 提升至 41.7%；IMPACT 角磨机记录上冻结感知头时正确决策率由 50.6% 提升至 54.3%。

Conclusion: 无需目标域视角标签即可学得可迁移的视角偏好策略，提升装配检查可验证性与决策正确率。

Abstract: Robots inspecting an assembly must determine which parts are present and whether they are correctly installed. During egocentric assembly assistance, head motion and workpiece handling reveal evidence for these checks, while spoken state confirmations link observations to procedural outcomes. We introduce INSPECT, which learns robot view preferences from records of a smart-glasses assistant that answers part queries and provides next-step guidance. Presence-Invariant TwinSwap (PI-TwinSwap) calibrates object evidence through paired identity interventions. Claim-indexed supervision separates evidence requirements from camera-reproducible observation changes. Object-centered calibration adapts relative view preferences to robot poses, while clause-level screening checks predicted evidence. The robot selects views using only its current observation and known poses, without candidate images. Evaluation uses annotated assistant-video replay to simulate state feedback, without target-domain view labels for policy training. On images of physical gearbox assemblies, INSPECT achieves the highest view utility among the compared non-oracle policies and raises human-rated full verifiability from 34.8% to 41.7% compared with keeping the current view. On commercial angle-grinder recordings in IMPACT, the transferred relative-view selector increases the correct decision rate from 50.6% to 54.3% with a frozen perception head. The source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [197] [A Simulation Platform for AUV Fault Recovery: Exploring LLM-Based Diagnostic Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20620)
*Khalid Halba,Kylie Cooper,James G. Bellingham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SPAR 闭环仿真平台，将实时 C 语言 AUV 控制软件与 LLM 编排层耦合，实现物理故障注入、结构化提示、任务文件生成与 LLM 评判打分，并通过 480 次集成试验评估前沿与本地 LLM 在质量偏移故障下的诊断与恢复规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV 在通信受限环境下需无人工干预地从故障中恢复；传统确定性分层控制只能应对预期故障，而 LLM 的随机性使单次演示不足以证明其可靠性，因此需要可复现的集成式评估框架。

Method: 构建 SPAR 闭环仿真架构：实时 C 语言载具软件 + 上层编排层，支持基于物理的故障注入、结构化提示、语言模型交互、任务文件生成、验证、执行与 LLM-judge 打分；以质量偏移（CG-shift）故障为对象，跨故障实现、提示结构、推理模型与任务条件进行 480 次集成试验，对比一个前沿模型与三个可本地部署的开源 LLM。

Result: 模型选择主导诊断性能：前沿模型在 85–90% 试验中将 CG 偏移机理列入前三假设，最佳本地模型为 60–78%；推理分析显示本地模型成功与遵循完整诊断流程相关，较弱模型常在升降舵指令实际跟踪正常时过早归因于舵机故障；本数据集上诊断性能与操作决策性能未见耦合。

Conclusion: 贡献在于将非预期故障恢复从"检测"扩展到"缓解"的架构，以及面向低功耗 AUV 的 LLM 辅助任务管理集成评估方法；LLM 更适合作为可调用的诊断/恢复规划器，而非替代确定性控制系统。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operating beyond reliable communications must recover from failures without human intervention. We investigate an architecture in which conventional deterministic layered control autonomy manages normal operations, while an invokable large language model (LLM) serves as a diagnostic and recovery planner when onboard anomaly detection identifies performance outside expected limits. Because language models are stochastic, rigorous evaluation requires ensemble testing rather than individual demonstrations. We present a closed-loop simulation architecture that couples real-time C vehicle software with a higher-level orchestration layer for physics-based fault injection, structured prompting, language-model interaction, mission file generation, validation, execution, and LLM-judge scoring. The framework, which we call SPAR (Simulation Platform for AUV Recovery), supports evaluation across fault realizations, prompt structures, reasoning models, and mission conditions. We vary these for a mass-shift fault over 480 SPAR trials, evaluating a frontier model and three off-the-shelf locally deployable LLMs. Model choice dominates diagnosis: the frontier model places the CG-shift mechanism in its top three hypotheses in 85-90% of trials, versus 60-78% for the best local model. Reasoning analysis indicates that local-model success is associated with following the complete diagnostic procedure, whereas weaker models often commit prematurely to elevator failure even though the actuator tracks its command. Diagnosis and operational decision performance do not appear to be coupled in this dataset. The contributions are an architecture extending unanticipated-fault recovery from detection to mitigation and an ensemble methodology for evaluating LLM-assisted mission management on low-power AUVs.

</details>


### [198] [SmellDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Quadruped Navigation with Olfactory Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20624)
*Faith Ogunwoye,Iana Zhura,Hajira Amjad,Timofei Kozlov,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Fedor Fedorov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SmellDiffusion 是一个嗅觉导航仿真流程，用开放词汇嗅觉场景图表示物种特异气体区域，并以峰值局部几何门控进行选择性气源校正，再共享目标给经典与扩散规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人被派遣到指定气体泄漏点时，需保持气体身份、估计气源并导航至目标。真实流场中浓度峰值常偏离气源超过 0.5m，导致源估计误差较大，需要选择性的校正机制与高效的气体引导轨迹生成。

Method: 在开放词汇嗅觉场景图中表示物种特异气体区域，经典与扩散规划器共享同一源估计目标；采用峰值局部几何门控（仅用训练集校准，在观测峰值处评估）决定是否触发预计算的前向匹配校正；扩散规划器生成气体引导轨迹，并与气源引导 A* 对比。

Result: 424 种源-风配置中 28 例峰值偏离源超 0.5m；几何门控在 10 例留出偏移中检出 9 例，精度 0.64；门控校正使偏移案例平均误差由 1.468m 降至 0.592m（60%），仅对 14/204 案例启用匹配；全案例平均误差 0.205m→0.180m。扩散 best-of-ten 平均气体暴露 0.0476 对比 A* 0.0455；单次扩散提案 41.7ms 对比 A* 72.3ms。Gazebo 六次匹配运行：A* 0.39m，扩散 0.31m。

Conclusion: 几何门控能以低调用率大幅降低偏移案例的源估计误差，扩散规划在精确度上可与气源引导 A* 相当，且单次提案更快，但 best-of-ten 序列采样增加总耗时；纯 A* 仍是最快且路径最短的方法。

Abstract: A robot sent to a named gas leak must preserve gas identity, estimate the source, and navigate to the resulting goal. We present SmellDiffusion, a simulation pipeline that represents species-specific gas zones in an open-vocabulary olfactory scene graph and shares the selected goal between classical and diffusion planners. Its key components are a peak-local geometric gate for selective source correction and diffusion-based, gas-guided trajectory generation. Among 424 unique source-wind configurations in solved flow, 28 have a concentration peak displaced more than 0.5m from the source. A source-independent geometric gate, calibrated only on the training split and evaluated at the observed peak, detects 9 of 10 held-out displacements at 0.64 precision. Gating a precomputed forward-matching correction reduces mean error on the displaced cases from 1.468m to 0.592m (60%), using matching for only 14/204 cases. All-case mean error falls from 0.205m to 0.180m. All planners receive the same scene-graph source estimate as their goal. In a controlled comparison, best-of-ten diffusion achieves mean gas exposure comparable to gas-guided A* (0.0476 versus 0.0455). A single diffusion proposal takes 41.7ms, compared with 72.3ms for gas-guided A*, although best-of-ten sequential sampling increases total runtime. Plain A* also reaches the same goal and remains the fastest and shortest-path method. Six matched Gazebo runs give mean robot-to-source errors of 0.39m for A* and 0.31m for diffusion.

</details>


### [199] [RTK-Vision PPO for Autonomous Micro UAV Recovery on an Airborne Carrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20629)
*Aashish Sahu,R Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RTK-视觉引导的强化学习框架，实现小型无人机从移动空中母机自主起飞、独立任务、返回再对接的完整循环；PPO终端回收策略在MuJoCo中训练并迁移到实机，仿真空载成功率99.55%，14次外场试验成功13次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复用"投放-任务-回收"需要把远程会合、近距感知、载体运动、气动干扰与不连续接触事件耦合起来，现有工作多只做孤立的降落动作，缺少完整自主循环的实机验证。

Method: 母子机均载RTK-GNSS，母机持续共享导航状态；近端由下视相机+基准标记检测提供相对位姿线索；终端回收阶段用PPO策略，物理仿真中引入传感器噪声模型、气动扰动代理与标记延迟随机化后迁移到真机；PX4负责底层稳定，确定性安全门独立于学习策略授权下降。

Result: PPO在2000个留出随机终端回合中成功率99.55%，调优PD基线同条件78.4%，平面终端误差中位数6.62 cm；14次室外试验成功13次（92.9%），覆盖近区回收与母机位移后的回收。

Conclusion: 实现了完整的自主空中投放-回收闭环而非孤立着陆，为巡检、监视与移动物流中的可复用母子机作业提供了实用基础。

Abstract: Autonomous recovery of a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) onto a moving airborne carrier enables reusable deploy-mission-recover operation, but couples long-range rendezvous, close-range perception, carrier motion, aerodynamic interaction, and a discontinuous contact event. This paper presents an RTK-vision-guided reinforcement-learning framework in which a child UAV is physically transported by a larger carrier, takes off from the carrier while airborne, executes an independent sortie, returns to the carrier's current position, redocks, and subsequently descends with the carrier. Both vehicles carry RTK-GNSS, and the carrier continuously shares its navigation state with the child. Near the recovery deck, RTK remains active while a downward-facing camera with a fiducial marker detector provides marker-relative alignment cues. A proximal policy optimization (PPO) policy governing the terminal recovery phase is trained in a physics-based MuJoCo simulation environment with explicit sensor noise models, an aerodynamic disturbance surrogate, and marker-latency randomization, then transferred to hardware. PX4 retains low-level stabilization, and a deterministic safety gate authorizes descent independently of the learned policy. The PPO checkpoint achieves 99.55% success over 2,000 held-out randomized terminal episodes, compared with 78.4% for a tuned PD baseline under identical conditions, with a median planar terminal error of 6.62 cm. Across 14 outdoor trials, the full mission succeeds in 13 trials (92.9%), spanning both near-region recovery and recovery after the carrier translates away from the release point. The results demonstrate a complete autonomous aerial deployment-and-recovery cycle rather than an isolated landing maneuver, establishing a practical basis for reusable carrier-child operation in inspection, surveillance, and mobile-logistics applications.

</details>


### [200] [TraceFlow: Guiding Frozen Flow-Matching Robot Policies with Success and Failure Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20646)
*Jiaxuan Zhang,Ruizhe Liu,Yu Zhang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TraceFlow：用检索到的成功/失败 rollout 动作密度构造有界引导场，为冻结的 flow-matching 动作专家提供测试时纠偏，仅需一个终局结果比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结权重的 VLA 策略在测试时无法利用自身失败经验：现有测试时方法依赖检索成功样本、critic、verifier 或动力学模型，但都没把机器人自己的失败 rollout 当作负证据，且通常需要比"终局成败比特"更多的监督信号。

Method: 构建 TraceBank，存储带终局标签的时序状态-动作轨迹（trace），从目标任务训练轨迹起步，后续可纳入部署机器人自身的 rollout；将检索到的成功与失败 rollout 的动作密度对齐为进度一致的引导场，作为有界修正项叠加到冻结的 flow-matching 动作专家的速度场上，无需任何权重更新。引导场仅使用每次 rollout 的一个终局结果比特，无其他标签。

Result: 真机有序装箱任务：基线 50 次按序完成 21 次，TraceFlow 39 次，一轮 stacking 不减权重达 47 次，错序回合从 20 降到 0。仿真增益具选择性：逐套件调参后 RoboMemArena Sequence 从 78.92% 升至 91.50%，Transferring 在 stacking 第 2 轮从 54.41% 升至 62.00%；26 任务总聚合不变，Counting 与 Occlusion 分别降 1.12 与 1.42 点，LIBERO-Plus (Long) +1.27 点（p=0.0733）。stacking 增益有限，各分支约在第 10 轮前达峰；bank 的成功/失败比无法预测检索分配。

Conclusion: 以失败轨迹为负证据、仅靠终局比特的测试时引导，可在不更新权重的情况下显著改善有序长程任务的成功率与顺序正确性，但增益随任务类型选择性出现、存在上限，且 bank 组成不构成有效的检索分配先验。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a flow-matching action expert generates each action chunk (a short command sequence) by integrating a learned velocity field; once its weights are fixed, the success or failure of an earlier rollout cannot change the chunk generated now. Concurrent test-time methods give a frozen policy such an input from retrieved successes, a learned critic, a verifier, or a dynamics model, but none uses the robot's own failed rollouts as negative evidence with nothing but a terminal outcome bit. We introduce TraceFlow, a progress-aligned guidance field that turns the action densities of retrieved successful and failed rollouts into a bounded correction to a frozen flow-matching action expert, using one terminal outcome bit per rollout and no other label. Its TraceBank stores traces, time-ordered state-action records with a terminal label, starts from the target-task training traces, and later admits the deployed robot's own rollouts. On an ordered real-robot packing task the base completes 21 of 50 trials in order, TraceFlow 39, and one stacking round without any weight update 47, with wrong-sequence episodes falling from 20 to 0. In simulation the gain is selective: with per-suite selected settings, TraceFlow raises RoboMemArena Sequence from 78.92\% to 91.50\% task success and Transferring from 54.41\% to 62.00\% at stacking round 2, leaves the 26-task aggregate unchanged, lowers Counting and Occlusion by 1.12 and 1.42 points, and changes LIBERO-Plus (Long) by +1.27 points (p = 0.0733). Stacking gains are finite, every branch peaking before round ten, and the bank's success-to-failure ratio predicts no retrieval allocation.

</details>


### [201] [SkipVLA: Skipping VLA Steps with Classical Planning for Fast Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20648)
*Kaivalya Agrawal,Md Ashiqur Rahman,Raymond A. Yeh,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkipVLA 提出一种混合策略，将预训练 VLA 与经典运动规划器结合：规划器负责自由空间运动，VLA 仅用于抓取、放置等接触丰富技能；并复用冻结的 VLA 视觉-语言骨干来预测每个规划运动的目标位姿，无需额外示教数据。实验在 13 个 LIBERO 仿真任务（三种 VLA）与 6-DoF YAM 真机三个抓放任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型测试时推理缓慢，尤其长时程任务需大量策略查询；现有加速手段（蒸馏小模型、异步动作块重叠、搭配快速低层策略）仍需全程运行学习策略。而经典运动规划器求解自由空间避障运动极快，但缺乏语义理解与目标定义能力。作者希望结合两者优势。

Method: SkipVLA 是一种混合策略：在自由空间运动段调用经典运动规划器，仅对接触丰富技能（抓取、放置）查询 VLA。为让规划器知道目标，复用 VLA 冻结的视觉-语言骨干预测每次规划运动的目标位姿，并利用 VLA 已有知识训练该位姿预测器，不引入额外示教数据。

Result: 在仿真中于 13 个 LIBERO 任务上配合三种 VLA 评估，并在真实 6-DoF YAM 机械臂上完成三个抓取-放置任务；任务完成速度最高提升 2.5 倍，能耗显著降低，同时保持相同的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 混合"VLA 语义决策 + 经典规划器快速运动"的分层范式可在不损失成功率的前提下大幅降低 VLA 延迟与能耗，且无需额外示教数据即可复用 VLA 骨干训练目标位姿预测器，为长时程机器人策略提速提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a class of generalist robot policies that map camera images and language instructions directly to robot actions. While promising, these models remain slow at test time, particularly for long-horizon tasks that require many queries to the policy. Recent efforts reduce VLA latency by distilling smaller models, overlapping asynchronous action chunks, or pairing the VLA with a fast low-level policy, but still run a learned policy for the entire task. In contrast to VLA, classical motion planners quickly find collision-free motions, but require an explicit goal and have no semantic understanding of the task. In this work, we present SkipVLA, a hybrid policy that combines a pretrained VLA with a classical motion planner, using the planner for free-space motion and querying the VLA only for contact-rich skills such as grasping and placing. SkipVLA reuses the frozen vision-language backbone of the VLA to predict a target pose for each planned motion, and learns this predictor without additional demonstrations introduced into the system by using what was already learnt by the large VLA. We evaluate SkipVLA with three VLAs on 13 LIBERO tasks in simulation and three pick-and-place tasks on a physical 6-DoF YAM arm, demonstrating up to 2.5x faster task completion and significantly lower energy consumption while achieving the same task success rate.

</details>


### [202] [DexTouch-WM: Learning Action-Conditioned Tactile World Models from Human Touch for Dexterous Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20649)
*Yan Qin,Yue Chen,Wenwei Lin,Shujia Liu,Chuqiao Lyu,Kailun Su,Chenze Yu,Ping Luo,Wenbo Ding,Tianxing Chen,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出动作条件世界模型 DexTouch-WM，利用可扩展的人类触觉数据（人手与灵巧手共用柔性压阻阵列与动作空间重定向），联合预测未来 RGB 观测与双侧触觉动力学，实现人→机器人预测能力的规模化迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的灵巧操作预测模型需要密集触觉交互数据，但在真机上采集成本高且强依赖特定本体与传感器。作者假设：当触觉观测与动作空间被对齐后，人类与机器人操作共享可迁移的接触动力学，因此人类触觉交互可作为监督同一动力学模型的补充数据轴。

Method: 在人与灵巧手上部署具有共享传感布局的柔性压阻阵列，并将人类运动重定向到机器人动作空间，使人类交互能监督与真机预测同一动力学模型。模型为动作条件世界模型：将预训练视频专家与轻量触觉专家通过解剖学感知触觉 token（anatomy-aware tactile tokens）及对齐的动作条件耦合，联合预测未来 RGB 观测与双侧触觉动力学。

Result: 在人→机器人规模化实验中，固定 5 小时真机监督，将人类交互数据从 0 小时增至 100 小时：在人类与机器人任务集不相交的前提下，机器人域留出数据的视觉、几何与接触预测均有显著提升；进一步将世界模型用作策略评估的代理环境，并作为合成轨迹生成器用于真机策略学习。

Conclusion: 可扩展的人类触觉交互为灵巧机器人世界模型学习提供了一条互补的数据轴；跨本体共享接触动力学并配合共享传感器布局与动作对齐，可有效把人类数据迁移到机器人预测与策略学习中。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of contact-rich dexterous manipulation requires dense tactile interaction, but such data are costly to scale on real robots and remain tied to embodiment-specific sensors. We introduce DexTouch-WM, an action-conditioned world model that learns from scalable human touch to jointly predict future RGB observations and bilateral tactile dynamics. Our insight is that human and robot manipulation share transferable contact dynamics when their tactile observations and action spaces are made compatible. We deploy flexible piezoresistive arrays with a shared sensing layout on both human and dexterous robot hands, and retarget human motion into the robot action space so that human interaction can supervise the same dynamics model used for real-robot prediction. DexTouch-WM couples a pretrained video expert with a lightweight tactile expert using anatomy-aware tactile tokens and aligned action conditioning. In human-to-robot scaling experiments, we keep five hours of real-robot supervision fixed while increasing human interaction from 0 to 100 hours, and observe substantial improvements in held-out robot-domain visual, geometric, and contact prediction despite disjoint human and robot task sets. Beyond prediction, we evaluate the world models as surrogate environments for policy evaluation and as generators of synthetic trajectories for real-robot policy learning, showing that scalable human interaction provides a complementary data axis for learning dexterous robot world models.

</details>


### [203] [HIL-UMI: Bringing Human-in-the-Loop Post-Training of Vision-Language-Action Models to Universal Manipulation Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20659)
*Zimu Han,Yiming Zeng,Jiyao Zhang,Zihao Zhao,Yuanfei Wang,Yixiang Jin,Shiqi Li,Shuangben Chen,Wei Huang,Ruodai Li,Hui Shen,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIL-UMI：无需真机执行的手持UMI人在环后训练框架，用Energy Score触发OOD采集、用优势估计器精选片段，优势条件BC混合数据微调VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型适配具体部署场景困难：任务级SFT受限于静态演示覆盖不足且无法区分有用进度行为；而人在环交互后训练通常需要反复在物理机器人上执行策略并依赖人工干预，成本高、难规模化。

Method: 基于手持UMI设备：采集人手演示时同步把同一观测流喂给当前策略做推理但不执行。用Energy Score比较人类动作轨迹与策略推断的差异，差异大即判定为OOD并触发采集；另一路反馈环用低在线优势预测定位关键片段，精化基于进度的优势估计器，再用其引导优势条件行为克隆，训练数据为基座演示与新策略数据的均衡混合。

Result: 在四个真实世界任务（含长时序与精细操作）上，HIL-UMI相对SFT取得一致提升，且定向采集与优势精化均带来增益；在Clean Up Table任务上优于HG-DAgger，同时单帧采集耗时更低。

Conclusion: 将人在环学习的迭代性与策略感知特性同真机部署解耦，为跨操作者、跨地点的VLA后训练提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models provide powerful priors for robot manipulation, yet adapting them to a specific deployment remains challenging. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on task-specific demonstrations provides a step toward deployment, but faces two persistent limitations: static data provide limited coverage of out-of-distribution states, and standard imitation objectives do not distinguish progressing behavior from less useful data. Interactive post-training can address these limitations, but typically requires repeated policy execution and human intervention on a physical robot. We introduce HIL-UMI, a policy-guided Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) framework for robot-free human-in-the-loop VLA post-training. During handheld UMI demonstrations, HIL-UMI queries the current policy on the same observation stream without executing its predictions. The Energy Score compares the human action trajectory with policy inference and triggers collection when their discrepancy indicates an out-of-distribution region. In a separate feedback loop, low online advantage predictions identify essential segments for refining a progress-based advantage estimator. The updated estimator then guides advantage-conditioned behavioral cloning using a balanced mixture of base demonstrations and new policy data. This design preserves the iterative and policy-aware nature of human-in-the-loop learning while decoupling data collection from robot deployment. Experiments on four real-world tasks spanning long-horizon and precise manipulation show that HIL-UMI achieves consistent improvement over SFT and benefits from both targeted collection and advantage refinement. Moreover, HIL-UMI outperforms HG-DAgger on Clean Up Table with lower per-frame collection time, suggesting a scalable path for VLA post-training across operators and locations.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Foresight without Explicit Trajectories for 3D Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20669)
*Zhongbo Zhang,Zaibin Zhang,Yifan Wang,Changbo Yan,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Movement Trend Guidance，从短观测历史中学习交互演化趋势的紧凑隐表示，训练时用稀疏未来夹爪状态监督，推理时仅保留该隐表示作为未来导向条件，为 3D 扩散策略提供前瞻性而不需显式规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 扩散策略能生成本身几何可行的动作，但成功操作还需预判交互走向。现有策略主要靠动作学习隐式涌现这种前瞻能力，缺乏显式的趋势引导，导致前瞻性不足。

Method: 基于 DP3：从短历史学习交互演化的紧凑隐表示，训练时以稀疏未来夹爪状态监督；推理时保留该隐表示，与当前观测共同作为未来导向条件，对动作生成提供全局条件，另在 UNet bottleneck 加入门控 FiLM 分支。仅增加 3.52% 参数，保留原有密集动作与滚动时域框架。

Result: RoboTwin2.0 50 任务混合训练 62.8% vs 56.1%；LIBERO-40 71.93% vs 37.08%；五个真实机器人任务 72.0% vs 49.0%，在三个基准上均稳定优于 DP3。

Conclusion: 扩散策略可显著受益于交互走向的前瞻信息，且无需被告知具体移动目标；该方法轻量、通用，可作为即插即用的未来导向条件增强 3D 扩散策略。

Abstract: 3D diffusion policies are strong at generating geometrically grounded actions from current observations, but successful manipulation requires not only knowing what motion is feasible now, but also anticipating where the interaction is heading. Existing policies largely leave such foresight to emerge implicitly from action learning. We introduce Movement Trend Guidance, a simple but effective way to provide this foresight without introducing an explicit plan. From a short observation history, the policy learns a compact latent representation of interaction evolution. During training, sparse future gripper states supervise this representation; at inference, only the latent is retained as future-oriented conditioning alongside the current observation. The latent provides global conditioning for action generation, while an additional gated FiLM branch is used only at the UNet bottleneck. Despite adding only 3.52% more parameters to DP3, our method preserves the original dense-action and receding-horizon formulation and consistently improves upon DP3 across RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-40, and DexArt. It reaches 62.8% vs. 56.1% in 50-task RoboTwin2.0 mixed training, 71.93% vs. 37.08% on LIBERO-40, and 72.0% vs. 49.0% on five real-robot tasks. These results show that a diffusion policy can benefit substantially from knowing where an interaction is heading, without being told exactly where to move.

</details>


### [205] [MAGNETAR: Multipath-Guided Spatial Posteriors for Transmitter Pose Inference in the Upper Mid-Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20670)
*Haozhe Lei,Ruibin Chen,Yuhan Jiang,Ali Rasteh,Aditya Dhananjay,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAGNETAR从单次异步RF多径快照中，联合推断发射器的平面位置与朝向后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人定位无线电发射源时，单点估计不足：在杂乱房间中单次测量可对应多个位置；且由于中高频段天线具方向性，同一测量也可能对应多个朝向。因此需要联合位置-朝向的后验分布。

Method: 输入为单次异步RF多径快照（AoA与SNR估计），并给定房间布局与接收机位姿。方法采用共享2D U-Net作为神经评分器，以候选朝向为条件，在离散化的位置-朝向网格上联合归一化得分；对五种神经评分器进行比较。训练使用经真实-仿真标定的10 GHz仿真数据加少量实测子集，输出网格化联合后验。

Result: 在留出仿真上，基于网格的联合后验优于参数化后验；以朝向为条件的评分器向机器人实测迁移效果最好；融合联合后验优于仅融合位置边缘分布。

Conclusion: 单快照RF多径下，以朝向为条件的网格化联合后验能更稳健地实现位置与朝向的联合推断，并改善机器人实测的迁移与多源融合效果。

Abstract: Robots that localize a radio transmitter need more than a point estimate: in cluttered rooms, one measurement is often consistent with several transmitter locations and, because upper-mid-band antennas are directional, several headings. We present MAGNETAR, which infers a joint posterior over planar transmitter position and heading from a single asynchronous radio-frequency (RF) multipath snapshot, represented by angle-of-arrival and signal-to-noise-ratio estimates, given the room layout and receiver pose. Among our five neural scorers, MAGNETAR adopts a shared 2D U-Net conditioned on each candidate heading, jointly normalizing scores over a discretized position-heading grid. Training uses real-to-sim-calibrated 10 GHz simulations and a small measured subset.
