Grid-based joint posteriors outperform parametric ones on held-out simulations, the heading-conditioned scorer transfers best to robotic measurements, and fusing joint posteriors improves on fusing position-only marginals.

</details>


### [206] [Towards Scaling Marine Perception with Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20680)
*Haoyu Ma,Onur Bagoren,Anja Sheppard,Elias Fandi,Ashrith Edukulla,Tanner Aslan,Natasha Sieh,Jingyu Song,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作扩展了基于 IsaacSim 的水下感知模拟器 OceanSim，加入合成数据生成（SDG）流水线，可自动生成大规模、带标注、可配置的逼真数据集，并在真实海胆检测任务上评估不同合成场景变化对 sim-to-real 性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中大规模真实标注数据难以获取且成本高昂，制约了基于学习的感知方法；合成数据有望弥合这一数据鸿沟。

Method: 在 IsaacSim 平台的水下模拟器 OceanSim 基础上构建 SDG 流水线，支持可配置的场景外观、结构与传感器参数，自动生成带标注的逼真合成数据集，并系统研究不同合成场景变化形式。

Result: 在真实水下海胆检测任务上验证了流水线有效性，并给出不同合成场景变化对 sim-to-real 泛化性能影响的实验发现，同时讨论了当前流水线在渲染保真度与场景多样性方面的局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为水下感知提供了可用的合成数据生成工具与 sim-to-real 实验洞察，未来将聚焦提升水下渲染保真度、场景多样性以及 sim-to-real 泛化评估方法。

Abstract: Scalable machine learning in challenging underwater environments is strongly limited by the lack of labeled real-world training data. This data is often expensive and laborious to gather, making large-scale real-world data challenging to gather and curate. However, simulated data can help close the gap, enabling many learning-based tasks for underwater perception. In this work, we extend OceanSim, an IsaacSim-based underwater perception simulator, with a Synthetic Data Generation (SDG) pipeline for training models to be used in underwater scenarios. The proposed pipeline enables users to generate large, automatically labeled, photorealistic datasets with configurable scene appearance, structure, and sensor settings. We evaluate the pipeline on a real-world sea urchin detection task and study how different forms of synthetic scene variation affect sim-to-real performance. Based on these experiments, we discuss findings on our results, main limitations of the current pipeline and identify future directions for improving underwater rendering fidelity, scene diversity, and the evaluation of sim-to-real generalization. The open-source code can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [207] [Custom PX4 firmware for autonomous hybrid aerial-marine missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20691)
*Andrea Capuozzo,Fabio Ruggiero,Vincenzo Lippiello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 扩展PX4固件以支持水空两栖无人机的水面导航模式，并与PX4任务流程和QGroundControl集成，仿真验证了平静与波浪条件下的稳定水面航行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水空两栖无人机可在同一任务中结合飞行与水面航行，用于水生环境测绘与监测，但PX4等主流固件缺乏对水面航行模式的支持，难以统一规划混合任务。

Method: 在PX4固件中新增手动与自主水面导航模式，整合进标准PX4任务管线与QGroundControl界面，支持区分空中与水面航点的统一规划执行，并保留原有飞行功能与安全机制。

Result: 通过仿真案例验证了实现的有效性，在平静与波浪条件下均表现出稳定的水面导航性能。

Conclusion: 该框架为两栖无人机提供了统一的空中-水面任务规划与执行能力，可作为此类平台的控制基础；但验证仅限于仿真，欠缺真实平台实验。

Abstract: Mapping and monitoring aquatic environments can benefit from hybrid aerial-amphibious drones able to combine flight and water-surface navigation within the same mission. This paper presents a PX4 firmware extension for such platforms, introducing manual and autonomous marine navigation modes integrated with the standard PX4 mission pipeline and QGroundControl interface. The proposed framework preserves existing flight functionalities and safety mechanisms while enabling unified planning and execution of hybrid aerial-marine missions with differentiated aerial and marine waypoints. Simulated case studies validate the implementation and demonstrate stable surface navigation under calm and wavy conditions.

</details>


### [208] [HOPHY: A Hierarchical Hypergraph Representation for Off-Road Path and Mission Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20694)
*Pranay Meshram,Charuvahan Adhivarahan,Prithvi Poddar,Ehsan Tarkesh Esfahani,Chen Wang,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOPHY：面向越野场景的可复用分层地形表示（语义区域GSNode+关键区域Coarse Region+类型化超边），支持状态变化时局部更新而非重建层级，实现近oracle成本的快速重规划，并实机验证1.5km八任务越野任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害响应、搜救与战术UGV任务中，地形、智能体类型和目标不断变化，需要反复进行路径与任务规划。像素栅格搜索在公里级重复查询下代价高，而现有语义抽象必须在地形条件变化时仍保持代价有效性与连通性。

Method: 提出HOPHY分层越野规划：将地图尺度地形组织为几何连通的语义区域（GSNodes）、保持连通性的关键区域（Coarse Regions）以及表征地形/智能体/天气上下文的类型化超边；通过超边交集定位受影响区域与关联边，实现状态增量更新而无需重建层级。

Result: 在公里级真实越野地图上，规划成功率100%，相对oracle（像素A*）中位代价偏差<0.01%，查询与重规划延迟显著低于像素与抽象基线；在MRTA问题上总计算量较像素A*减少79倍、较最快抽象基线减少7.2倍，任务makespan与像素A*相当；在Clearpath Jackal实机上完成1.5km、八任务、混合路面任务并成功处理阻塞触发的重规划。

Conclusion: HOPHY提供了一种可复用、可增量更新的分层地形抽象，在保持近最优代价与连通性的同时大幅提升重复查询与重规划效率，适用于多机器人任务分配与真实越野自主导航。

Abstract: Mission-level autonomy for disaster response, search and rescue, and tactical UGV operations requires repeated path and mission planning as terrain conditions, agent types, and objectives change. Pixel-grid search is costly for repeated kilometer-scale queries, while semantic abstractions must maintain valid costs and connectivity as conditions change. We present HOPHY (Hierarchical Off-Road Planning using Hypergraphs), a reusable hierarchical terrain representation that organizes map-scale terrain into geometrically connected semantic regions (GSNodes), connectivity-preserving critical regions (Coarse Regions), and typed hyperedges for terrain, agent, and weather context. Hyperedge intersections select affected regions and incident edges for state updates without rebuilding the hierarchy. Across real off-road maps spanning kilometer-scale areas, HOPHY achieves 100% planning success and less than 0.01% median cost deviation from the oracle (pixel A*), with substantially lower query and replanning latency than the evaluated pixel and abstraction baselines. Applied to a multi-robot task-allocation (MRTA) problem, these gains reduce total computation by 79x over pixel A* and 7.2x over the fastest abstraction baseline, with mission makespan comparable to pixel A*. Finally, we demonstrate HOPHY on a physical Clearpath Jackal that successfully executes a 1.5-km, eight-task mission across mixed-surface outdoor terrain and a blockage-triggered replanned route.

</details>


### [209] [MoWAM: Explicit Future Motion Prediction for Efficient World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20709)
*Jiayu Wang,Bin Zhu,Yue Yu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoWAM 用显式未来运动预测替代未来视频生成，在训练时联合学习视觉动力学、运动与动作，推理时完全移除视频生成，并通过运动感知验证器实现推理时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 通过引入未来动力学提升机器人策略学习，但推理时显式生成未来视频带来巨大计算开销；若直接去掉未来生成可提效，却使未来动力学仅隐式编码于观测特征中，在分布偏移下鲁棒性受限。因此需要一种既保留显式未来表示、又避免视频生成开销的方案。

Method: 提出 MoWAM：以结构化机器人运动作为未来的紧凑抽象，仅建模机器人预期演化而非重建完整未来场景；采用 Mixture-of-Transformer 架构，在训练中学习未来视觉动力学并联合预测运动与动作，使推理时彻底移除视频生成；利用紧凑运动表示在推理时采样多组运动-动作候选，并用运动感知任务进度验证器进行筛选。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus 及真实世界操作任务上，MoWAM 取得强分布内性能、更优分布外鲁棒性，真实世界平均成功率高于代表性 WAM 基线；随候选数量增加性能持续提升，验证推理时扩展的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式未来运动是未来视频的有效且高效的替代抽象，可在不生成视频的前提下保留未来显式表示，并天然支持推理时扩展，为高效 WAM 提供新范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by incorporating future dynamics, yet explicitly generating future videos at inference introduces substantial computational overhead. Removing future generation improves efficiency, but leaves future dynamics only implicitly encoded in observation features, which can limit robustness under distribution shifts. We propose MoWAM, an efficient WAM that replaces future video generation with explicit future motion prediction. Instead of reconstructing the complete future scene, MoWAM models structured robot motion as a compact abstraction of the future, capturing how the robot is expected to evolve under the current scene and interaction constraints. A Mixture-of-Transformer architecture learns future visual dynamics during training while jointly predicting motion and action, allowing video generation to be removed entirely at inference while retaining an explicit representation of the future. The compact motion representation further enables efficient inference-time scaling by sampling multiple candidates of motion and action pairs and selecting among them with a motion-aware task-progress verifier. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that MoWAM achieves strong in-distribution performance, improved out-of-distribution robustness, and higher average real-world success than representative WAM baselines. In addition, performance improves as more candidates are explored, demonstrating that explicit future motion provides an effective and efficient basis for inference-time scaling.

</details>


### [210] [Underwater Visual Target Tracking with Target-Specific Depth Estimation and Adaptive Model-Fusion Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20731)
*Yuheng Zhou,Haiyang Cheng,Yanqi Feng,Pangkit Fong,Mei Xuan Lee,Marcus Gee,Chongrong Fang,Jianping He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于AUV的水下立体视觉伺服框架：感知端通过目标深度掩膜提取可靠深度并分别对深度与图像中心做卡尔曼滤波，控制端解耦偏航与平动，采用自适应模型融合MPC（匀速+零速目标模型），并施加执行器/跟随距离/视场约束，仿真与实机实验优于现有框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉水下目标跟踪面临深度测量不可靠、目标运动未知两大难题，现有方法难以在实时性与鲁棒性之间取得平衡，且多自由度耦合优化计算代价高。

Method: 感知：基于颜色、视差与时间线索构建目标深度掩膜以筛选可靠像素，提取3D相对状态，对深度测量与检测图像中心分别进行卡尔曼滤波。控制：将偏航调节与平动控制解耦，避免耦合多自由度优化，实现实时平动MPC；平动采用自适应模型融合预测控制，融合匀速与零速目标模型，依据历史预测误差在线更新模型权重，并在执行器、跟随距离与视场约束下求解控制量。

Result: 通过仿真与真实水下实验验证框架有效性，性能优于现有对比框架；未给出具体量化指标（如定位误差、跟踪成功率等）。

Conclusion: 该方法为水下AUV视觉伺服提供了一种感知-控制协同的实用方案，但缺少定量基准与代码/数据开源信息，通用性有待验证。

Abstract: Vision-based underwater target tracking is challenged by unreliable depth measurements and unknown target motion. This paper proposes a stereo visual-servoing framework for an autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV). For perception, the framework derives a stable 3D relative state from stereo images through target-specific depth extraction and Kalman filtering. It constructs a target-depth mask from color, disparity, and temporal cues to select reliable target pixels, and then filters the resulting depth measurement and detected image center separately. For control, the framework decouples yaw regulation from translational control, avoiding computationally expensive coupled multi-DOF optimization and enabling real-time translational MPC. The translational controller employs adaptive model-fusion predictive control, combining constant-velocity and zero-velocity target models to accommodate different target-motion patterns. It updates the model weights using historical prediction errors and computes translational commands subject to actuation, following-distance, and field-of-view constraints. Through simulations and real-world experiments, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and show it has better performance than existing frameworks.

</details>


### [211] [MILER: Semantic Mid-Level Representation for Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning in Unstructured Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20747)
*Thomas Steinecker,Denis Trescher,Alexander Bienemann,Thorsten Luettel,Mirko Maehlisch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MILER：用语义中层表征(MLR)模拟器离线RL训练策略，实车端用BEVFusion生成一致鸟瞰语义表示，配合轨迹对齐策略实现感知与控制的zero-shot sim-to-real，在3.0 km混合赛道无人干预行驶17.3 km，全部算力部署于Jetson AGX Orin。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在自动驾驶落地稀少，非结构化环境更是如此，核心障碍在于非结构化场景的sim-to-real迁移困难（感知域差、控制动力学差）。作者希望绕过真实数据采集与微调，实现零样本迁移。

Method: 提出MILER端到端策略框架。离线：自定义语义中层表征(MLR)模拟器训练RL策略网络，控制输出直接作用于自行车模型。部署：相机+LiDAR经BEVFusion生成与MLR模拟器一致的语义BEV表征；策略输出动作不直接下发车辆，而是经轨迹对齐(trajectory-alignment)策略转化为可执行轨迹，从而同时桥接感知与控制的sim-to-real gap。端侧部署于Jetson AGX Orin。

Result: 在两辆不同车辆、3.0 km测试赛道（含多种障碍物、发卡弯、最高33.6 km/h速度、越野路段）上，累计无人干预行驶17.3 km，验证有效性；全套软件栈实时运行于Jetson AGX Orin。

Conclusion: 语义中层表征+轨迹对齐的组合可有效缓解非结构化环境的sim-to-real难题，实现零样本迁移；端侧算力即可满足实时性，为RL在真实自动驾驶（含非结构化场景）的部署提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning constitutes a promising approach owing to its potential for superhuman performance and self-learned policies. However, its application to real-world autonomous driving remains scarce, particularly in unstructured environments, because of the challenges associated with sim-to-real transfer for unstructured environments. In this work, we present MILER, an end-to-end policy framework with zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. During offline training, we employ a custom semantic mid-level representation (MLR) simulator and train the policy network using reinforcement learning, with its control outputs applied directly to a bicycle model. During deployment on the real vehicle, camera and LiDAR data are processed by BEVFusion to generate a semantic bird's-eye-view representation consistent with that of the MLR simulator. The actions generated by the policy network are not applied directly to the real vehicle. Instead, we employ a trajectory-alignment strategy that enables zero-shot sim-to-real transfer of both perception and control. We extensively evaluate the proposed framework on a diverse test track comprising numerous challenges, including various obstacles, hairpin curves, velocities of up to 33.6 km/h, and off-road sections. In total, we drove 17.3 km with two different vehicles on a 3.0 km test track without human intervention, thereby demonstrating the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, the entire software stack runs on a Jetson AGX Orin.

</details>


### [212] [OPTED: On-Policy Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Driving using a Render-Free Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20756)
*Damiano Da Col,Maximilian Igl,Peter Karkus,Kashyap Chitta,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 OPTED，将闭环后训练解耦为“特权教师 RL + 学生策略监督”，在只用矢量化输入训练的教师指导下，对相机端到端驾驶策略做 on-policy 微调，显著降低所需仿真交互量并提升驾驶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练数据规模扩展收益递减，后训练变得关键；端到端驾驶策略用行为克隆开环预训练，闭环部署时误差累积导致偏离训练分布，出现安全风险。但基于传感器的闭环后训练需昂贵仿真，直接 RL 成本高。

Method: OPTED：先用 RL 在矢量化输入（HD 地图 + 边界框）上训练特权教师；教师再在闭环后训练中为预训练学生（相机策略）提供监督，实现 RL 与端到端策略后训练的解耦。应用于 TransFuser 与 VaVAM，在 AlpaSim 中利用真实驾驶日志的 3DGS 神经重建进行微调。

Result: 驾驶分数分别提升 1.6× 与 9.5×；控制实验中，OPTED 以约少三个数量级的仿真交互达到与直接 RL 后训练相当的闭环性能，同时更贴近人类先验分布。

Conclusion: 解耦式特权教师监督是一种高效、低交互成本的端到端驾驶闭环后训练范式，可推广至多种相机策略模型。

Abstract: As scaling pre-training data alone yields diminishing returns, post-training is becoming increasingly important across physical AI domains such as autonomous driving. End-to-end driving policies are pre-trained in open loop with behavior cloning on human demonstrations. However, compounding errors during closed-loop deployment can take the vehicle outside the training data distribution, increasing the risk of safety-critical incidents. Closed-loop post-training can mitigate this risk but requires costly simulation for sensor-based policies. We propose OPTED (on-policy fine-tuning for end-to-end driving) which decouples reinforcement learning from the post-training of the end-to-end policy: a privileged teacher is trained using RL on vectorized inputs (HD-map and bounding boxes). This teacher then provides supervision to the pre-trained student during closed-loop post-training. We apply OPTED to two camera-based models, TransFuser and VaVAM, and fine-tune them in AlpaSim, using neural reconstructions (3DGS) of real driving logs. Driving scores increase by factors of 1.6$\times$ and 9.5$\times$, respectively. In controlled experiments OPTED matches closed-loop performance with approximately three orders of magnitude fewer simulator interactions than direct RL post-training, while staying closer to the human prior. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [213] [Agile-WAM: An Agile Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20761)
*Hanchu Zhou,Brendan Lynch,Raman Goyal,Dechen Gao,Begum Kasap,Boqi Zhao,Junshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ABBR：一种敏捷的触觉世界动作模型（WAM），将视觉-触觉观测编码为共享隐空间，通过直接的双模态到动作流匹配过程联合生成动作块与未来视觉/触觉隐表示，并引入多时间尺度监督（视觉大时间偏移、触觉下一帧）以兼顾效率与细粒度接触动力学。五个真实世界任务中成功率相对提升29.4%，推理延迟11.9ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型依赖大规模预训练生成骨干来建模富接触物理动力学，导致推理效率低、部署不灵活，难以支撑高频精确的机器人控制。同时视觉与触觉信号在时间尺度上本质不同（视觉帧间高度相似，触觉在接触瞬间剧烈变化），单一时域预测难以兼顾。

Method: ABBR 将视觉与触觉观测编码进一个共享隐空间，作为直接"视觉-触觉到动作"流匹配（flow matching）过程的源，联合生成动作块隐表示与未来视觉/触觉隐表示；提出多时域多模态预测：对视觉隐表示施加更大时间偏移的监督，对触觉隐表示则预测下一帧以捕捉细粒度接触动力学；整体架构避免了对大型生成骨干的依赖以降低延迟。

Result: 在9个仿真与5个真实世界富接触操作任务上，ABBR 在成功率上优于最强基线并保持低推理延迟；真实世界五个实验中总体成功率相对提升29.4%，推理延迟为11.9ms。

Conclusion: 多模态世界动作模型可以在敏捷架构下实现，无需大型生成骨干即可适用于精确、高频的机器人控制，为触觉富接触操作提供了高效的 WAM 范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) advance beyond conventional visuomotor policies by jointly predicting future world states and robot actions, enabling the policy to learn physical dynamics that support effective control. However, recent tactile WAMs often rely on large-scale pretrained generative backbones to capture contact-rich physical dynamics, which limit their inference efficiency and flexible deployment. In this paper, we present \ABBR{}, an agile tactile World Action Model for contact-rich robot control. \ABBR{} encodes visual and tactile observations into a shared latent that serves as the source of a direct vision-tactile-to-action flow-matching process, which can jointly generate latent representations of action chunks and future visual/tactile latents. A key observation is that vision and tactile signals evolve at inherently different timescales: adjacent visual frames are often highly similar, whereas tactile signals can change abruptly upon contact. We therefore introduce multi-horizon multimodal prediction in \ABBR{}, which provides supervision for visual latent at a larger temporal offset while predicting the tactile latent in the next frame to capture fine-grained contact dynamics. Across nine simulated and five real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, \ABBR{} demonstrates strong and robust performance, outperforming the strongest baseline in success rate while maintaining low inference latency. In particular, in five real-world experiments, \ABBR{} yields a relative gain of $\textbf{29.4\%}$ in overall success rates while achieving inference latency of $\textbf{11.9 ms}$. These results demonstrate that multimodal WAM can be achieved with an agile architecture suitable for precise and high-frequency robot control. More details are available on our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [214] [GeoAAC: Geometry-Based Adaptive Action Chunking from Denoising Trajectories in VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20776)
*Xin Chen,Sen Chen,Yujuan Ding,Jian Liu,Guoqing Wang,Wei Ye,Heng Tao Shen,Yi Bin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoAAC：利用 Flow Matching 去噪轨迹的几何信息（前缀几何变化与预测不确定性正相关）构造 horizon-wise 几何剖面，在单次生成中自适应确定动作块长度，无需额外训练，在 GR00T N1.5 与 π0.5 上于 LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365 及真机任务一致优于固定 horizon 与现有自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略普遍采用固定动作 horizon（action chunking），但 rollout 中不同任务阶段对动作连续性、控制精度和闭环反馈的需求不同，固定 horizon 无法适配变化的控制需求，需按当前预测可靠性动态调整。

Method: 提出 GeoAAC：基于 flow-based VLA 策略的几何自适应动作分块。观察到 Flow Matching 去噪轨迹的几何为预测可靠性提供过程级信息，动作前缀间的几何变化与预测不确定性正相关；据此按前缀几何构造 horizon-wise 几何剖面，在单次生成中自适应确定动作 horizon，无需额外训练，可直接套用于现有流式 VLA。

Result: 在 GR00T N1.5 与 π0.5 上，于 LIBERO、LIBERO-Pro、RoboCasa365 及真实世界操作任务中，一致优于固定动作 horizon 基线与已有自适应方法：仿真最高提升 8.7 个百分点，真机平均成功率从 53.3% 提升至 74.4%。

Conclusion: 动作 horizon 的自适应性可由生成过程的几何信号（无需训练）直接导出，是一种通用、即插即用的推理期改进；跨仿真与真机的稳定增益表明其对 VLA 执行控制具有实用价值。

Abstract: Action chunking is widely used for action generation and execution in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, yet existing approaches commonly use a fixed action horizon. During a rollout, different task stages may require different levels of action continuity, control precision, and closed-loop feedback, making a fixed horizon unable to accommodate changing control requirements. We propose \textbf{GeoAAC}, a geometry-based adaptive action chunking method for flow-based VLA policies that adjusts the action horizon according to the reliability of the current action prediction. We show that the geometry of Flow Matching denoising trajectories provides process-level information for characterizing prediction reliability, with geometric variation across action prefixes remaining positively correlated with predictive uncertainty. GeoAAC uses this prefix-wise geometry to construct a horizon-wise geometric profile and adaptively determine the action horizon from a single generation without additional training. Experiments with GR00T N1.5 and {\pi}0.5 on LIBERO, LIBERO-Pro, RoboCasa365, and real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements over fixed-action-horizon baselines and existing adaptive methods, including up to 8.7 percentage points in simulation and an increase in average real-world success rate from 53.3\% to 74.4\%.

</details>


### [215] [StageGuard: Learning Stage Transitions for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks via Agentic Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20791)
*Jinbang Huang,Yuanzhao Hu,Zhiyuan Li,Ran Qi,Yixin Xiao,Yangzheng Wu,Tengyue Ba,Zhanguang Zhang,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StageGuard：用教师 VLM 推理结合示范轨迹生成子任务完成/策略切换的结构化解释，蒸馏出轻量学生 VLM 生成紧凑自解释并做监督微调，实现准确、低延迟的层级规划阶段切换判断，在 BEHAVIOR-1K 与真机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级规划需判断何时终止当前技能并切换到下一子任务，但现有方法依赖难以获取的预设计完成信号检查器；大规模 VLM 虽有推理能力，其决策边界与任务完成判据不对齐，且云端部署与长推理带来高延迟，难以实时监控。

Method: agentic 蒸馏框架：教师模型推理 + 示范轨迹生成子任务完成与策略切换的结构化解释；轻量学生 VLM 据此产生紧凑自解释，用于监督微调，实现高效在线阶段切换预测。

Result: 在两个基准的轨迹上评估阶段切换预测，并在 BEHAVIOR-1K 层级机器人控制中闭环评估任务成功率，另有真机验证；阶段切换预测显著提升，同时支持高效在线监控。

Conclusion: 通过解释驱动的知识蒸馏，可将 VLM 推理能力压缩为低延迟的学生模型，实现准确、实时的阶段切换监控，提升长时序层级机器人任务执行可靠性。

Abstract: Hierarchical planning frameworks combine skills from multiple robot control policies for long-horizon task execution, where determining when to terminate the current skill and advance to the next subtask is essential. Existing approaches often rely on pre-designed completion signal checkers that are hard to obtain in real-world execution. Large-scale vision-language models (VLMs) offer strong reasoning capabilities, but their decision boundaries are not inherently aligned with task completion criteria, while cloud deployment and lengthy reasoning introduce substantial latency, limiting real-time monitoring. We propose StageGuard, an agentic distillation framework for accurate and efficient stage-transition decisions. StageGuard combines teacher-model reasoning with demonstration trajectories to generate structured explanations of subtask completion and policy switching. A lightweight student VLM uses these explanations to generate compact self-explanations, which are used for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate stage-transition prediction on trajectories from two benchmarks and assess closed-loop task success through integration into hierarchical robot control on BEHAVIOR-1K, with further validation on real robots. Results show substantial improvements in stage-transition prediction while supporting efficient online monitoring.

</details>


### [216] [Workspace Models: Lightweight Robotic Memory via Saliency-Driven Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20820)
*Nitish Dashora,Douglas Chen,Idan Shenfeld,John Marangola,Pulkit Agrawal,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 workspace token 作为轻量潜在记忆表示，训练时用 VLM 蒸馏历史相关信息，部署时无需 VLM 在环即可替代观测，提升长时记忆密集型操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人操作任务需要历史记忆，但直接用完整历史条件化策略易受虚假相关影响、性能下降；现有方法依赖在环 VLM 查询压缩历史信息，计算开销大、部署昂贵。

Method: 将昂贵 VLM 查询移至训练阶段：先用 VLM 识别完成任务所需的当前与历史关键信息，再通过集合重建解码器损失（set-reconstruction decoder loss）将这些信息蒸馏进轻量的 workspace token；部署时以 workspace token 作为观测的即插即用替代，实现无 VLM 在环的记忆查询。

Result: 在仿真与真实硬件上验证，workspace token 可替代观测支撑记忆密集型任务，且相比在环 VLM 方法更轻量、策略性能更优。

Conclusion: 训练期蒸馏、部署期轻量查询的记忆表示范式有效，为长时操作提供高效替代在环 VLM 的方案；token 不仅更轻量还带来更好性能。

Abstract: Complex robotic manipulation tasks frequently require a long-term memory of past events and actions. As conditioning on full histories renders policies prone to spurious correlations and degrades performance, many approaches to policy memory involve compressing historical information through expensive VLM queries in-the-loop to process only task-salient information. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach in which computationally intensive VLM queries are made during train-time to learn a lightweight latent memory that can be efficiently queried at deployment time. Our representation, which we call the \textbf{workspace token}, is trained by (1) using a VLM to identify current and historical information necessary for completing a task, then (2) distilling these into the workspace token using a set-reconstruction decoder loss. In both simulation and hardware, we show that the workspace token can be used as a drop-in replacement for observations during deployment, enabling policies to solve memory-intensive tasks without the need for VLM reasoning in-the-loop. Interestingly, we found that workspace tokens are not only more lightweight but also lead to better policy performance.

</details>


### [217] [Coding Agents with an Obstacle-Aware Harness for Safe Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20822)
*Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang,Zhen Dong,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究编码智能体在安全约束下的机器人操作表现，发现失败根源在于规划阶段未将避障约束优先级化，提出SafeHarness双harness方案，实现71.9%任务成功率与87.5%避障率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体（语言模型写机器人控制器程序）已成为机器人操作的有前景范式，且无需机器人专用数据即可运行。然而该范式是否安全从未被研究。作者希望在"任务目标+不可触碰障碍物"的安全约束下评估编码智能体，检验其是否将安全与任务完成同等对待。

Method: 在安全约束任务上评估编码智能体，发现多数情况下发生碰撞。通过将操作分解为路径阶段（route phase）与接触密集时刻（contact-rich moment）定位失败来源：路径阶段模型缺乏"可通行路径"概念且无法在路径失效时重规划；接触阶段未意识到执行同样受约束限制。为此提出SafeHarness：(1) 障碍物感知路径规划——将物体表示为包围盒，在其上绘制由路点组成的候选路径，智能体预先规划、验证、必要时重规划后再执行；(2) 障碍物感知接触执行——选择接触位置使接触本身避开障碍物。

Result: SafeHarness达到71.9%任务成功率与87.5%避障率，分别超过先前SOTA 6.5%与27.0%；相比同一智能体无harness时提升2.3倍与1.5倍。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在安全约束下失败并非感知或指令问题，而是约束在规划中从未成为优先级。通过显式的障碍物感知路径规划与接触执行两个harness注入约束优先级，可显著提升安全性与任务成功率。

Abstract: Coding agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot manipulation: a language model writes the robot controller as a program, and agents built in this way now operate robots without robot-specific this http URL this paradigm is also safe, however, has not been asked. We evaluate coding agent under a safety constraint, where each task pairs a manipulation goal with an obstacle the robot must not touch. The agent pursues the goal but collides with the obstacle in most cases, treating task completion as its sole objective while neglecting safety. The agent reasons about the obstacle in its traces, and the prompt already forbids touching it, so neither perception nor instruction is at fault; the fault lies in the planning, where the stated constraint never becomes a priority. By decomposing manipulation into a route phase and a contact-rich moment, we locate the source of the failure. Along the route, the model cannot prioritize the safety constraint, having no notion of a clearing route and none of replanning once a chosen route becomes infeasible. At the contact, it is unaware that contact execution is bounded by the same constraint. To close this gap, we present SafeHarness, which equips the model with two obstacle-aware harnesses that enable it to prioritize the safety constraint. Obstacle-aware route planning grounds the objects as bounding boxes and draws candidate routes over them as sequences of waypoints. The agent then plans a route in advance, verifies it, replans when necessary, and only then executes it. Obstacle-aware contact execution instead selects the contact position so that the contact itself avoids the obstacle. SafeHarness attains 71.9% task success and 87.5% collision avoidance, surpassing the previous SOTA by 6.5% and 27.0%, respectively. These results are $2.3\times$ and $1.5\times$ those of the same agent without harnesses.

</details>
